Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SVEET 通过骨干特征解耦与条件帧独立性两条原则，将双向视频扩散模型适配为自回归流式视频编辑框架，实现实时高质量编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频到视频扩散方法多为双向、非因果结构，难以支持流式（自回归）视频编辑。作者希望在不重新设计生成模型的前提下，仅通过训练适配预训练双向视频扩散模型，实现高质量、实时的流式视频编辑。

Method: 系统回顾现有 video-to-video 扩散方法，提炼出流式适配的两条关键原则：骨干特征解耦（backbone feature disentanglement）与条件帧独立性（conditional frame independence）。据此提出新范式：辅助模型分支以时间独立的 self-attention 编码源视频输入，将中间特征注入对应骨干模块以实现流式兼容控制；并提出解耦训练方案，显式约束视频可控性与模型因果性两个优化方向正交，以弥合双向与流式模型特征空间差异，支持零样本知识迁移。

Result: 实验表明 SVEET 在保持实时性能的同时实现更优编辑质量，在单张 H100 GPU 上达到 15 FPS，且未使用任何辅助加速技术。

Conclusion: 将双向视频扩散模型经训练改为自回归流式编辑是可行且高效的，特征解耦与解耦训练是实现流式兼容与跨架构零样本迁移的关键。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose SVEET, a framework that requires merely training on a pretrained bidirectional video diffusion model but supports high-quality streaming video editing in an auto-regressive fashion. To tackle this problem, we first systematically revisit existing video-to-video diffusion approaches and identify two key principles for such streaming adaptation: backbone feature disentanglement and conditional frame independence. Building on these insights, we develop a novel paradigm for controllable video generation. At its core, an auxiliary model branch encodes source video inputs with temporally independent self-attention, and the intermediate features are injected into the corresponding backbone blocks for streaming-compatible control. Moreover, to bridge the discrepancy between the feature spaces of bidirectional and streaming models, we propose a decoupled training scheme that explicitly enforces the orthogonality between the optimization directions of video controllability and model causality. Such disentanglement ensures compatibility between the two objectives at inference and facilitates smooth zero-shot knowledge transfer across heterogeneous backbone architectures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SVEET achieves superior editing quality while maintaining real-time performance, attaining 15 FPS on a single H100 GPU 17 without any auxiliary acceleration techniques. Codes are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [207] [INTCORT: Training-Free Spatial Reasoning Enhancement for Vision-Language Models via Input Transformations and Confidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24813)
*Haoran Sun,Jingqi Xu,Yanhui Li,Enci Liu,Kaidi Xu,Yanwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练空间推理增强框架 INTCORT：通过几何变换与问题反转构造多推理视图，并用关系token置信度路由聚合预测，平均提升10.01%，最高提升25.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 空间推理能力差；现有训练依赖方法计算开销大且灾难性遗忘，免训练方法干扰内部机制、损害通用能力。

Method: 先验证两个假设（适当几何图像变换与query-reversal可纠正错误预测；正确预测的关系token置信度更高），再据此构建多视图推理，按关系token置信度做路由聚合，不修改模型内部机制。

Result: 在多个常用基准、多种VLM上显著提升空间推理精度，全模型全基准平均提升10.01%，相比先前工作最高提升25.01%。

Conclusion: 免训练、不改内部结构的多视图+置信度路由是一种有效且通用的VLM空间推理增强方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in multimodal tasks, yet they still exhibit poor ability in spatial reasoning. Existing training-dependent and training-free enhancement methods suffer from high computational costs with catastrophic forgetting and internal mechanism interference that compromises general capabilities, respectively. In this work, we first verify two key hypotheses: appropriate geometric image transformation and query-reversal transformation can recover incorrect spatial predictions, and correct predictions exhibit higher relation-token confidence than incorrect ones. Based on these findings, we propose INTCORT, a training-free spatial reasoning enhancement framework that constructs multiple inference views through input transformations and aggregates their predictions via relation-token confidence routing, without modifying the VLM's internal mechanisms. Experimental results on several commonly-used benchmarks demonstrate that INTCORT substantially improves spatial reasoning accuracy across diverse VLMs, achieving an average improvement of 10.01% over all models and benchmarks. Compared with prior works, INTCORT achieves superior performance with improvements of up to 25.01%.

</details>


### [208] [Mobile Imaging Solutions for Medical Diagnosis: Trends and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24814)
*Syed Muhammad Ibne Zulfiker,Tanzima Hashem,Fariha Tabassum Islam,Md Sultanul Arifin,Khandker Aftarul Islam,Nishat Anjum Bristy,Faria Huq,Priyeta Saha,Syeda Nahida Akter,Arpita Saha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一篇综述，系统梳理基于手机/网络摄像头等非医疗设备的移动图像医疗诊断研究，按疾病类别对比分析现有方案的优势与局限，提炼理想诊断工具特性并指出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机与移动设备的算力、摄像与图像分析能力提升，使低成本、可及的医疗诊断成为可能，尤其面向偏远和资源受限地区的人群；但目前缺乏跨应用类别的系统性对比与挑战梳理。

Method: 综述性研究：调研现有移动图像医疗诊断文献，按眼/耳鼻喉、营养不良、心率变异性、皮肤与口腔、外伤等应用类别对比state-of-the-art方案，归纳优劣，提炼理想工具特性，梳理应用特定与共性挑战。

Result: 给出跨类别的对比性分析结论，识别已取得进展的方向和尚需进一步研究的空白，并总结常见挑战（如成像标准化、标注数据、泛化性等）。

Conclusion: 移动图像诊断在低成本疾病筛查与监测方面潜力显著，尤其服务欠发达地区，但需在数据集、成像规范与验证标准上继续推进。

Abstract: Advances in processing power, camera technologies, and mobile image analysis have made smartphones and other mobile devices, such as laptops, increasingly suitable for medical diagnosis and healthcare applications. Researchers have developed low-cost solutions for the early detection and monitoring of various health conditions, including eye and ENT diseases, malnutrition, heart rate variability, skin and oral conditions, and injuries, using images captured by non-medical devices such as smartphones and webcams. This survey examines existing research on mobile image-based medical diagnosis, with an emphasis on its potential to enable low-cost and accessible healthcare. We comparatively analyze state-of-the-art solutions across different healthcare application categories, examining their advantages and limitations. Based on this analysis, we identify desirable characteristics of mobile image-based diagnostic tools and highlight areas where existing approaches have made progress as well as areas requiring further research. We also discuss application-specific and common challenges and outline directions for future research. Overall, this study provides a comprehensive overview of mobile image-based healthcare solutions and their potential to support low-cost disease diagnosis and monitoring, particularly for underserved populations in remote and resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [209] [ZVeC: A Zero-Shot Framework for Instance-Level Vehicle Extraction and Generative Point Cloud Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24825)
*Daisy Li,Kyle Gao,Quanyun Wu,Boris Jutzi,John S. Zelek,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZVeC 提出零样本、实例级驱动的 LiDAR 点云补全框架，将场景级补全分解为语义物体实例补全，用深度与3D高斯条件扩散模型完成每个车辆实例后再重组场景，并在自建地下停车场稠密LiDAR基准上验证其几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下环境 LiDAR 点云因遮挡与视点受限存在严重几何缺失，且缺乏大规模监督数据集，传统场景级补全方法泛化困难。

Method: 零样本实例驱动的组合式框架：先将场景分解为语义物体实例以降低杂乱环境中的重建歧义，对每个分割出的车辆实例独立使用“深度+3D高斯”条件的扩散模型，利用通用几何先验补全，最后将实例重组成原始场景；无需场景特定训练。

Result: 在自建真实地下停车场稠密 LiDAR 基准上，量化指标与视觉质量均优于代表性场景级基线；补全结果与原测量输入差异显著（平均 KL 散度约 2.1），但将输入降至原始测量的 1% 时补全结果仅轻微变化（KL < 0.50），显示极端稀疏下的几何一致性。

Conclusion: 实例级分解+生成式先验的组合式零样本补全可有效应对地下场景的严重遮挡与稀疏输入，为无监督数据条件下的 LiDAR 点云补全提供可行路径。

Abstract: LiDAR point clouds acquired in underground environments exhibit severe geometric incompleteness due to occlusions and limited sensor viewpoints, making reliable point cloud completion challenging without large supervised datasets. We propose ZVeC, a zero-shot, instance-driven framework that reformulates scene-level completion as compositional object-level reconstruction. By decomposing a scene into semantic object instances, ZVeC reduces reconstruction ambiguity in cluttered environments while eliminating the need for scenario-specific training. Each segmented vehicle is completed independently using a depth- and 3D Gaussian-conditioned diffusion model that exploits generalized geometric priors before the reconstructed instances are recomposed into the original scene. To evaluate our approach, we construct a real-world dense LiDAR benchmark of underground parking environments. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements over representative scene-level baselines in both quantitative metrics and visual quality. The completed point cloud differs substantially from the measured input (average KL divergence ~ 2.1), yet reducing the input to only 1% of the original LiDAR measurements changes the completed reconstruction only marginally (KL divergence < 0.50). This demonstrates that ZVeC produces geometrically consistent completions even under extreme input sparsity.

</details>


### [210] [When Wider Views Fail: Stress-Testing Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24839)
*Daisy Li,Kyle Gao,Quanyun Wu,Hanna Chomko,John S. Zelek,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究前馈3D重建模型在视角跨度分布偏移下的失效模式：在固定输入图像数量下改变角度跨度，发现跨度增大导致重建退化，出现表面覆盖不完整与几何缺乏观测支撑两类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建模型可高效地从稀疏图像估计几何，但其预训练特性使其易受超出训练分布的偏移影响；需要明确这些失效模式，才能判断其在无约束成像场景中的可靠部署条件。

Method: 将视角变化作为受控分布偏移：固定稀疏输入图像预算，仅调节输入的角跨度，在多个前馈重建模型上进行对比实验，分析不同跨度下的重建完整性与几何可靠性。

Result: 随视角跨度增大，多个前馈重建模型性能显著退化；大跨度下同时出现表面覆盖不完整，以及几何结构缺乏对应观测影像支撑的问题。

Conclusion: 视角变化可引发超越传统"重建不完整"的失效模式，表明需在挑战模型几何先验的分布偏移条件下评估预训练前馈重建模型。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models enable efficient geometry estimation from sparse images, but their pretrained nature can make them vulnerable to distribution shifts beyond their training data. Identifying these failure modes is important for understanding when such models can be reliably deployed in unconstrained imaging settings. We investigate viewpoint variation as a controlled distribution shift by varying the angular span of sparse image inputs while keeping the input budget fixed. Across multiple feed-forward reconstruction models, we observe substantial degradation as viewpoint span increases, with wide spans producing both incomplete surface coverage and geometry unsupported by the observed imagery. These results reveal that viewpoint variation can induce failure modes beyond conventional reconstruction incompleteness, highlighting the need to evaluate pretrained feed-forward models under distribution shifts that challenge their learned geometric priors.

</details>


### [211] [Revisiting Multi-View Stereo: A Sequence-to-Sequence Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24850)
*Aoxiang Fan,Corentin Dumery,Nicolas Talabot,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将已知相机参数下的MVS重构为sequence-to-sequence任务，用全局Transformer联合预测所有视角几何，并通过ray-map嵌入与统一全局代价体显式注入相机先验，在多个公开基准上超越MVS与前馈重建基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈模型即使给定真值相机参数，仍受重建歧义导致的几何畸变困扰；传统MVS多采用sequence-to-one、仅预测单参考视角深度的范式，视角间几何不一致。作者希望在已知相机参数条件下，融合传统MVS与前馈方法的优势，联合且一致地恢复所有输入视角的几何。

Method: 提出全局Transformer架构，含两个显式利用相机先验的组件：(1) ray-map嵌入，将相机参数注入图像patch token使Transformer具备相机感知能力；(2) 统一全局代价体，替代传统逐视角代价体，跨所有视角联合捕捉3D结构。将MVS从sequence-to-one映射重构为sequence-to-sequence任务，联合预测全部输入视角的几何。

Result: 在多个公开基准上达到state-of-the-art性能，超越MVS与前馈重建两类基线，表明已知相机参数下可有效缓解重建歧义带来的几何畸变。

Conclusion: 通过将已知相机参数的MVS形式化为sequence-to-sequence问题，并以ray-map嵌入与全局代价体显式注入相机先验，在传统MVS与前馈方法之间架起桥梁，实现跨视角一致的几何估计，取得领先精度。

Abstract: Computing accurate geometry from multi-view images is a fundamental problem in computer vision. Recent feed-forward (FF) models jointly estimate 3D geometry and camera parameters, but they typically suffer from geometry distortion caused by reconstruction ambiguity, even when ground-truth camera parameters are supplied. In this paper, we study the multi-view stereo (MVS) problem with known camera parameters and propose a novel approach that bridges conventional MVS and FF methods. Rather than casting MVS as a sequence-to-one mapping that predicts depth only for a single reference view, we reformulate it as a sequence-to-sequence task, akin to FF models, that jointly predicts geometry for all input views. We introduce a global transformer-based architecture with two components that explicitly exploit camera-induced priors: ray-map embeddings that inject camera parameters into image patch tokens, making the transformer camera-aware, and a unified global cost volume that replaces conventional per-view cost volumes to jointly capture 3D structure across all views. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks show our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing both MVS and FF reconstruction baselines.

</details>


### [212] [DTKDP: A Dual Teacher Knowledge Distillation and Pruning Framework for Lightweight Oriented SAR Ship Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24872)
*Yuming Li,Fan Zhang,Alin M. Achim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DTKDP框架，通过双教师知识蒸馏与结构化剪枝，将两阶段旋转SAR舰船检测器（Oriented R-CNN、RoI Transformer）参数量压缩约87.5-91.8%、FLOPs降低约75.6-79.9%，精度基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段有向检测器在SAR舰船检测中定位精度高，但其庞大backbone、特征金字塔、proposal模块与沉重RoI head难以部署；而已有轻量化SAR舰船检测器多为一阶段框架，缺乏proposal级别的精细旋转定位能力。因此需要一种能在保留两阶段精细定位能力的同时大幅压缩模型的方法。

Method: DTKDP在卷积、归一化与线性层中引入可学习门控（gates），实现卷积通道与RoI head神经元的剪枝；提出旋转proposal对齐（RPA），在教师生成的共享旋转proposal空间中蒸馏师生预测；并采用双教师方案，由同构主教师提供分类与回归指导，异构辅助教师补充分类线索。

Result: 在SSDD与RSDD-SAR数据集上，ResNet-50骨干的Oriented R-CNN与RoI Transformer参数量减少87.5-91.8%，FLOPs减少75.6-79.9%；AP50、AP75、mAP50:75、mAP50:95相对变化在-2.38%至+0.65%之间；相比RTMDet-tiny在四指标上提升0.52-27.55%，并持续优于代表性蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: DTKDP为轻量化有向SAR舰船检测提供了可行的精度-效率折中方案，证明通过门控剪枝与双教师旋转proposal蒸馏可在显著压缩两阶段检测器的同时保持精度。

Abstract: Two-stage oriented detectors achieve high localization accuracy in synthetic aperture radar (SAR) ship detection, but their large backbones, feature pyramids, proposal modules, and heavy region of interest (RoI) heads hinder deployment. Existing lightweight SAR ship detectors typically use one-stage frameworks that lack proposal-level refinement for precise rotated localization. This paper presents a dual-teacher knowledge distillation and pruning (DTKDP) framework for lightweight oriented SAR ship detection. DTKDP introduces learnable gates into convolutional, normalization, and linear layers to prune convolutional channels and RoI-head neurons. Rotated proposal alignment (RPA) distills teacher and student predictions in a shared teacher-generated rotated proposal space, while a dual-teacher scheme combines classification and regression guidance from a homogeneous main teacher with complementary classification cues from a heterogeneous auxiliary teacher. Experiments on the SAR Ship Detection Dataset (SSDD) and Rotated Ship Detection Dataset in SAR Images (RSDD-SAR) show that DTKDP reduces the parameters of Oriented Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Oriented R-CNN) and RoI Transformer equipped with ResNet-50 backbones by 87.5-91.8% and their floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 75.6-79.9%. In terms of average precision (AP) and mean average precision (mAP), the resulting Oriented R-CNN-slim and RoI Transformer-slim retain accuracy close to their full-scale counterparts. Relative changes across $\mathrm{AP}_{50}$, $\mathrm{AP}_{75}$, $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:75}$, and $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:95}$ range from a 2.38% decrease to a 0.65% improvement. Compared with RTMDet-tiny, they improve all four metrics on both datasets by 0.52-27.55% and consistently surpass representative distillation methods, demonstrating a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [213] [SPHQuant: Efficient extreme low bit weight quantization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24875)
*Kewei Zhang,Zheng Chen,Haotong Qin,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPHQuant：面向 VLM 的无旋转球坐标极低比特权重量化框架，将 8D 权重向量分解为符号、半径与单位方向，把离群值幅度隔离到半径并对其分配额外精度，配合正方向码本与角参数化微调，并设计硬件友好的 GEMV 内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新一代原生多模态 VLM 以 LLM 为骨干，参数量与自回归解码的内存受限特性使其难以在边缘设备部署。仅权重量化后训练是实用方案，但推向极低比特（2-3 bit）仍困难：无旋转方法在极低比特下受权重离群值影响精度骤降，基于旋转的方法虽能提升精度却引入额外运行时开销。

Method: SPHQuant 采用无旋转的球坐标表示：将每个 8 维权重向量分解为坐标符号、半径和正单位方向，使离群幅度集中于半径、方向保持有界且统计规整。据此对半径分配额外比特以缓解离群导致的精度损失；使用紧凑的正方向码本，并通过角度参数化微调码本条目以维持单位球约束。同时设计硬件友好的 GEMV 内核，使方向码本小到可放入共享内存查表，并高效打包半径比特。

Result: 实验表明 SPHQuant 达到与当前最先进极低比特量化方法相当的性能，在 RTX A6000 上解码吞吐较 QTIP 提升 30.3%。代码将开源。

Conclusion: 球坐标分解为极低比特 VLM 权重量化提供了无旋转、低开销的新路径，在精度与解码效率之间取得良好折中，并具备实际硬件实现可行性。

Abstract: Recent foundation models are moving toward native multimodal Vision-Language Models (VLMs), making VLMs a central form of next-generation foundation models. However, their large language backbones make edge deployment difficult due to high memory footprint and memory-bound autoregressive decoding. Weight-only post-training quantization is a practical solution, but pushing VLMs to extreme low bit-widths remains challenging: existing rotation-free methods suffer from outliers at 2-3 bits, while rotation-based methods improve accuracy at the cost of additional runtime overhead. We propose SPHQuant, a rotation-free spherical weight-only quantization framework for VLMs. Instead of quantizing weights directly in Cartesian coordinates, SPHQuant decomposes each 8D weight vector into coordinate signs, radius, and a positive unit direction. This representation isolates outlier magnitude into the radius while keeping directions bounded and statistically regular. Based on this insight, SPHQuant allocates extra precision to the radius to mitigate accuracy degradation induced by outliers. It further uses a compact positive-direction codebook and fine-tunes codebook entries through angular parameterization to preserve the unit-sphere constraint. We also design a hardware-friendly GEMV kernel that keeps the direction codebook small enough for shared-memory lookup and packs radial bits efficiently. Experiments show that SPHQuant matches the performance of state-of-the-art extreme low-bit quantization methods while improving decode throughput over QTIP by 30.3% on RTX A6000. Code will be released in this https URL .

</details>


### [214] [Generating Chest X-Ray Counterfactuals by Specialising Foundation Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24879)
*Xiaodan Xing,Rajat R. Rasal,Julia A. Meister,Sara Ghorayeb,Galvin Khara,Jessica Schrouff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 specialisation 框架，将预训练非因果生成模型以数据与参数高效方式适配为分布偏移下的因果机制，并据此用潜空间流匹配训练放射学反事实图像生成模型 RadCF，在三个胸片数据集上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景中反事实图像生成受限于标注数据稀缺、数据集间分布偏移，以及预训练生成模型与反事实推断所需因果模型之间的不匹配。已有方法在感知质量、身份保持和因果模型忠实度上有所改进，但难以落地应用。

Method: 提出 specialisation：面向分布偏移，将预训练的非因果生成模型高效适配为因果机制，兼顾数据与参数效率。基于该框架构建 RadCF，采用 latent flow matching（潜空间流匹配）进行反事实图像生成；在三份胸片数据集上覆盖不同分布偏移、数据规模与反事实问题，并涉及高度局部化的干预。

Result: RadCF 与 specialisation 在反事实合理性上优于现有方法，同时具备数据与参数效率；生成的反事实样本可用于检测并缓解下游医学分类器中的捷径学习（shortcut learning）。

Conclusion: 该工作为医疗等分布偏移、标注稀缺场景下的反事实图像生成提供了可复用的适配范式，并展示了反事实数据在下游模型去偏中的应用价值。

Abstract: Counterfactual image generation answers questions about how a subject would have looked under retrospective, hypothetical scenarios. Recent methods have improved perceptual quality, identity preservation and faithfulness to an underlying causal model, but their adoption in healthcare is limited by scarce annotated data, distribution shift between datasets, and mismatches between pretrained generative models and those required for counterfactual inference. We propose specialisation, a data and parameter-efficient framework for adapting pretrained, non-causal generative models into causal mechanisms under distribution shift. Based on this framework, we train a radiology counterfactual image generation model, called RadCF, using latent flow matching. We validate our approach on three chest X-ray datasets spanning different dataset shifts, data volumes, and counterfactual questions, associated with challenging, highly-localised interventions. Our results show that RadCF and specialisation improve counterfactual soundness over existing methods while being data and parameter efficient, and that the resulting counterfactuals can detect and mitigate shortcut learning in a downstream medical classifier. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [215] [SLICEChat: Progressive In-Encoder Token Pruning for Whole-Slide Pathology Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24894)
*Ali Kerem Bozkurt,Baris Cem Bakay,Ibrahim Kulac,Cigdem Gunduz-Demir,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLICEChat 在混合 Mamba-Transformer 切片编码器中嵌入渐进式 token 剪枝，实现十亿像素病理全切片的高效多模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片病理图像（WSI）达十亿像素级，现有切片级多模态大模型需处理数千 patch token 且仅在编码后压缩，导致多模态注意力计算代价高昂，可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出 SLICEChat：混合 Mamba–Transformer 编码器，Mamba 层负责高效长程传播，Transformer 层在序列渐进缩短时保留全局交互；各阶段之间引入语言监督的区域感知剪枝，按受控保留率移除空间连通的低效用区域，在多模态融合前得到紧凑切片表示。

Result: SlideBench VQA 上 TCGA 达 79.84%、BCNB 达 59.09%，优于既有切片级病理 MLLM；WSI-Bench 综合指标最高；显存占用与推理时延在评测模型中具竞争力。

Conclusion: 渐进式 token 剪枝与 Mamba-Transformer 混合编码可在十亿像素 WSI 上实现准确且计算高效的多模态推理，为超长视觉序列的切片级建模提供可行范式。

Abstract: Whole-slide pathology images (WSIs) contain gigapixel-scale visual content, creating a major scalability challenge for slide-level multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing approaches process thousands of patch tokens and typically apply compression only after slide encoding, leaving multimodal attention computationally expensive. We introduce SLICEChat, a slide-level MLLM that integrates progressive token pruning within a hybrid Mamba--Transformer slide encoder. Mamba layers enable efficient long-range propagation, while Transformer layers preserve global interactions as the sequence is progressively shortened. Between stages, language-supervised, region-aware pruning removes spatially coherent low-utility regions under a controlled keep-rate schedule, producing compact slide representations before multimodal fusion. On SlideBench VQA, SLICEChat achieves 79.84% accuracy on TCGA and 59.09% on BCNB cohorts, outperforming prior slide-level pathology MLLMs, and achieves the highest overall WSI-Bench metrics. It also provides competitive memory usage and the inference latency among the evaluated models. These results demonstrate accurate and computationally efficient multimodal reasoning over gigapixel WSIs.

</details>


### [216] [PixelDiT2: Representation-Grounded Pixel Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24919)
*Yongsheng Yu,Wei Xiong,Yichen Sheng,Shiqiu Liu,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelDiT2：用冻结的预训练视觉基础模型为像素空间扩散提供逐patch表征引导（representation grounding），解耦表征学习与像素生成，在ImageNet 256上600 epoch达FID 1.46、512上680 epoch达FID 1.48。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型图像质量已接近潜空间扩散，但收敛更慢、最终质量仍落后。作者认为关键在于缺少显式表征先验：潜空间扩散在紧凑结构化隐空间中去噪，而像素扩散必须同时从原始RGB空间学去噪友好表征与像素生成。

Method: 提出PixelDiT2，端到端像素空间扩散模型。核心是representation grounding：使用冻结的预训练视觉基础模型在去噪全过程提供逐patch表征指导，使像素扩散Transformer专注像素生成；不引入自编码器，避免潜空间重建瓶颈。

Result: ImageNet 256×256：600 epoch后FID 1.46；512×512：680 epoch后FID 1.48。

Conclusion: 通过冻结视觉基础模型提供显式表征引导，可在无自编码器/无隐空间重建瓶颈的前提下解耦表征学习与像素生成，缩小像素扩散与潜空间扩散在收敛速度与最终质量上的差距。

Abstract: Recent advances in pixel-space diffusion models have narrowed the image quality gap with latent-space diffusion, but still converge more slowly and lag behind in final image quality. We argue that a key reason is the lack of an explicit representation prior: unlike latent diffusion, which usually denoises in a compact and structured latent space, pixel diffusion needs to learn denoising-friendly representations and pixel generation simultaneously from raw RGB space. To address this problem, we propose PixelDiT2, an end-to-end pixel-space diffusion model designed to decouple representation learning from pixel generation without introducing an autoencoder or latent reconstruction bottleneck. We propose representation grounding that uses a frozen pretrained vision foundation model to provide explicit per-patch representation guidance throughout denoising, allowing the pixel diffusion transformer to focus more on pixel generation. On ImageNet-256x256, PixelDiT2 achieves an FID of 1.46 after 600 epochs; at 512x512 resolution, PixelDiT2 achieves an FID of 1.48 after 680 epochs.

</details>


### [217] [Anatomy-Decomposed Chest Computed Tomography (CT) Projections as Scalable Supervision for Bone Suppression in Chest Radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24937)
*Mrunmay Angaitkar,Piyush Kumar,Aarjav Satia,Pranav Rao,Ashish Mittal,Manoj Tadepalli,Preetham Putha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于数字重建X光片(DRR)的框架，用胸部CT合成像素级配对的组件图像(骨、非肺软组织、肺)，训练模型进行骨/肺抑制，并扩展到非配对组件级DRR翻译，在多数据集上验证骨抑制对下游检测的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片中骨骼重叠会掩盖异常，而配对的骨抑制训练数据稀缺，监督学习难以开展，需要一种能自动生成配对监督信号的替代方案。

Method: 新骨分割算法将CT分解为骨、非肺软组织、肺三部分并分别投影，加权组合得到组件图像精确求和等于完整DRR，形成像素配对的合成监督；模型预测目标组件再由减法恢复剩余部分；进一步在真实胸片输出上构建目标域，做非配对、组件级DRR翻译，缩小外观差距同时保留解剖细节。

Result: 下游检测实验显示骨抑制带来收益，且增益集中在骨重叠显著的异常上；相比开源DRR引擎，未修改的DRR在真实感与标签相关解剖保真上相当，翻译后的DRR在FID、肺野锐度与源CT解剖一致性上最优。

Conclusion: DRR合成配对监督可有效迁移到真实胸片的组件抑制任务，无需真实配对数据；组件级非配对翻译进一步提升真实感；方法适用于医学影像域，但未见与物理AI/机器人/3D重建核心方向的关联。

Abstract: Bone overlap can obscure abnormalities in chest radiographs, while scarce paired training data limit supervised bone suppression. We address this challenge with a digitally reconstructed radiograph (DRR) framework that converts chest computed tomography (CT) into paired supervision for component suppression. A novel bone segmentation algorithm enables CT decomposition into bone, non-lung soft-tissue, and lung components, which are projected separately. Their weighted combination yields synthetic radiographs with pixel-registered component images that sum exactly to the full DRR. Models trained on these data suppress bone or lung components by predicting the target component and recovering the remainder by subtraction, transferring to real radiographs without real paired training data. As an extension, their outputs on real radiographs provide target domains for unpaired, component-wise DRR translation, reducing the appearance gap while retaining anatomical details. Across multiple public datasets, downstream detection experiments demonstrate the utility of bone suppression, with gains concentrated on abnormalities with substantial bone overlap. Compared with open-source DRR engines applied to the same CTs, our unmodified DRRs achieve comparable realism and preservation of label-relevant anatomy, while translated DRRs achieve the best Fréchet inception distance (FID), lung-field sharpness, and agreement with source-CT anatomy among the evaluated methods. Models and inference code: this https URL Translated projections: this https URL .

</details>


### [218] [GAE: Learning a Geometry-Native Latent Space for 3D-Consistent World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24981)
*Jiahao Lu,Minghao Yin,Wenbo Hu,Hengyu Liu,Wang Zhao,Sai-Kit Yeung,Ying Shan,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何原生自编码器（GAE），将几何基础模型特征重参数化为紧凑的几何原生隐空间，该隐空间可联合解码外观、深度、相机与点图，作为感知与生成的共享接口；在固定生成器与训练协议的条件下替换隐空间，可同时提升视觉质量与3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成器能产出逼真帧，但难以保持一致的3D场景；作者认为这不仅是建模问题，更是表征问题：生成器演化出以表观为中心的隐空间，而感知模型在富含语义、编码跨视角结构的几何空间中恢复几何。因此需要一种几何原生的共享表征。

Method: 不把几何作为额外输出，而是将几何基础模型的特征重参数化为紧凑隐空间用于生成，实现为几何原生自编码器（GAE），其隐变量可联合解码为表观、深度、相机和点图；在此基础上使用标准条件流（conditional flow）支持多种生成任务。通过控制变量比较（固定生成器与训练协议，仅替换隐空间）验证效果。

Result: 在RealEstate10K与DL3DV上，FVD分别下降12.7%与23.1%；在RealEstate10K上相机轨迹误差减半，同时视觉质量与独立测量的3D一致性均得到改善。

Conclusion: 隐空间是几何一致生成的核心，且几何原生隐空间可作为感知与生成之间的共享接口，为统一的几何-外观表征提供可行路径。

Abstract: We present a compact geometry-native latent space as a shared foundation for perception and generation. Visual generators can produce photorealistic frames without preserving a consistent 3D scene. We argue that this is not only a modeling problem but also a representation problem: generators typically evolve appearance-centric latents, while perception models recover geometry in a semantically rich space that encodes cross-view structure. Rather than adding geometry as another output, we reparameterize a geometry foundation model's features into a compact latent space for generation. We realize this shift with the geometry-native autoencoder (GAE), whose latent is jointly decodable to appearance, depth, cameras, and point maps. With this state, a standard conditional flow supports diverse generation tasks. In controlled comparisons that hold the generator and training protocol fixed, replacing the latent with GAE improves both visual quality and independently measured 3D coherence: FVD falls by $12.7\%$ and $23.1\%$ on RealEstate10K and DL3DV, and camera-trajectory error is halved on RealEstate10K. Together, these results show that the latent space is central to geometry-consistent generation and can serve as a shared interface between perception and generation.

</details>


### [219] [WorldCrafter: Consistent Video World Model with Implicit 3D-aware Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24984)
*Wangbo Yu,Kunhao Liu,Wenbo Hu,Shenghai Yuan,Chaoran Feng,Haiyang Zhou,Yukun Huang,Yiran Wang,Wang Zhao,Yingmin Luo,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldCrafter 提出一种可相机查询的隐式 3D 感知记忆视频世界模型：让请求视点决定多视角证据如何压入生成器的 token 预算，联合训练记忆编码器与位姿条件读出模块，实现单图/文本驱动的流式场景探索，在长时一致性与相机控制精度上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽支持交互式探索，但在长时程与跨视角下难以持续遵守先前的观测证据，缺乏有效的长期几何记忆机制。

Method: 构建相机可查询的隐式 3D 感知记忆。记忆编码器与位姿条件读出模块和视频生成器联合训练，在去噪前将历史观测整合为固定数量的目标视点专属 token，不依赖显式深度对应；再结合近期时序上下文与少步蒸馏实现流式探索。

Result: 在静态与动态场景实验中，长时程一致性与相机控制精度显著提升，同时在分钟级探索中保持视觉质量。

Conclusion: 以视点驱动的 token 级记忆压缩是提升视频世界模型长期 3D 一致性的有效路径，支持单图/文本的流式场景探索。

Abstract: Video world models enable interactive exploration of dynamic environments, yet struggle to respect prior observations over long horizons and across viewpoints. We present WorldCrafter, a video world model that learns a camera-queryable implicit 3D-aware memory for this purpose. The key insight is to let the requested viewpoint shape how multi-view evidence is compressed into the video generator's limited token budget. Trained jointly with the video generator, a memory encoder and pose-conditioned readout module integrate historical observations into a fixed set of target view-specific tokens before denoising, without explicit depth-based correspondences. By combining this memory with recent temporal context and few-step distillation, WorldCrafter enables streaming scene exploration from a single input image or text prompt. Experiments across static and dynamic scenes show substantial gains in long-horizon consistency and camera-control accuracy while preserving visual quality during minute-scale exploration.

</details>


### [220] [VideoGen-Agent: Reinforcing Video Generation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24997)
*Binxu Li,Haoyi Duan,Yuhui Zhang,Yaohui Zhang,Zihao Lin,Kaituo Feng,Suozhi Huang,Xiangyi Li,Yu Li,Chunyuan Li,Shilong Liu,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoGen-Agent：通过多任务智能体强化学习训练的多模态智能体，协调增强、生成、验证工具完成视频生成；并提出VABench基准（600条提示）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以满足需要专门知识、特定身份、物理一致性或有序事件的提示。作者希望让智能体学会调用外部工具来弥补基础生成器的能力缺口。

Method: 构建覆盖六类任务、类别均衡的数据集，训练共享策略：先用教师生成轨迹做监督微调建立工具使用行为，再用强化学习精调；通过多轮交互协调增强、生成与验证工具；设计类别感知混合奖励，评估工具调用有效性、任务适配工具选择与生成视频质量。

Result: 在VABench上，VideoGen-Agent较其基座文生视频模型提升19.1分（56.5→75.6）；仅升级生成工具即可达86.1分，无需额外智能体训练；人类评估中84.3%的对比偏好升级后配置。

Conclusion: 验证了跨视频生成任务学习工具使用的可行性，且训练后的智能体能随生成工具的进步持续受益。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generative models have enabled high-fidelity, temporally coherent video generation. However, these models often struggle to satisfy prompts requiring specialized knowledge, specific identities, physical consistency, or ordered events. In this paper, we present VideoGen-Agent, a multimodal agent trained through multitask agentic reinforcement learning to use external tools for video generation. The agent coordinates augmentation, generation, and verification tools through multi-turn interactions, using the prompt and intermediate observations to guide its decisions. We train a shared policy on a category-balanced dataset spanning six tasks. Supervised fine-tuning on teacher-generated trajectories establishes tool-use behavior, which is then refined through reinforcement learning. A category-aware hybrid reward evaluates tool-call validity, task-appropriate tool use, and generated video quality. We further introduce VABench, a held-out benchmark of 600 prompts covering procedural knowledge, single- and multi-entity identity preservation, physical consistency, scene composition, and multi-shot temporal structure. On VABench, VideoGen-Agent improves over its base text-to-video generator by 19.1 points, from 56.5 to 75.6. Upgrading the generation tools further raises the score to 86.1 without additional agent training. Human raters prefer the upgraded configuration over the strongest standalone baseline in 84.3% of comparisons. These results support learning tool use across video-generation tasks and show that the trained agent can benefit from subsequent advances in generation tools.

</details>


### [221] [GameHorizon Suite: Multi-Horizon Data and Evaluation in Gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25001)
*Yiran Wang,Xingyilang Yin,Junfu Pu,Guangzhi Wang,Kaifeng Li,Mingyu Ouyang,Huiqiang Sun,Lingen Li,Cheng Cheng,Wangbo Yu,Honghao Chen,Xiaodong Cun,Chi-Man Pun,Zhiguo Cao,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GameHorizon 统一数据与评测套件，含自动化标注流水线、5000 小时/21 款游戏的 AAA 游戏数据集，以及离线+在线可复现基准，评测 47 个模型逾百万次调用，揭示任务难度层级与模型能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频游戏数据集与基准要么覆盖游戏范围窄、要么缺少语言指令、要么依赖高方差在线 rollout，无法在多时间尺度上统一衡量模型的视觉理解、指令分解、目标规划与精细动作控制能力。

Method: 构建 GameHorizon Suite：1) GameHorizon-Annotator，可扩展的自动化多时间尺度指令标注流水线；2) 基于该流水线构建 GameHorizon-Data，首个大规模 AAA 游戏数据（5000 小时、21 款游戏、100 名人类专家玩家，时序对齐的视频、动作与多层级指令）；3) GameHorizon-Bench，含可复现离线评测（数千标准化问题，3 类主任务+诊断变体）与分步在线测试。

Result: 对 47 个模型进行超过一百万次调用评测，呈现出清晰的任务难度层级，并在不同模型家族间显示出显著能力差异；在线赛道可检验离线分数是否反映真实 gameplay 能力，并将失败定位到长时程中的具体步骤。

Conclusion: GameHorizon 为跨时间尺度、跨模型家族的 gameplay 能力评测提供了标准化标尺，数据集、标注器与基准将开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Modern video games provide a measurable testbed for AI models, combining abilities of visual understanding, instruction decomposition, goal planning, and precise action control over multiple temporal horizons. Existing datasets and benchmarks, however, either cover a narrow range of games, lack language instructions, or rely on high-variance online rollouts. To address these challenges, we introduce GameHorizon, a unified data and evaluation suite that measures gameplay capabilities at different horizons for diverse model families. GameHorizon Suite consists of three components. First, GameHorizon-Annotator is a scalable and automated annotation pipeline for multi-horizon instructions. Second, utilizing the pipeline, we construct GameHorizon-Data, the first large-scale AAA gameplay dataset with temporally aligned videos, player actions, and multi-horizon instructions. It comprises 5,000 hours of recordings from 21 games, collected by 100 human expert players. Third, we build GameHorizon-Bench with reproducible offline and stepwise online testing. The offline track enables reproducible evaluation using thousands of standardized questions organized into three primary tasks and a series of diagnostic variants, while the online track tests whether offline scores reflect actual gameplay capabilities and localizes failures to specific steps within long-horizon gameplay. Based on our GameHorizon Suite, we evaluate 47 models through more than one million model invocations, revealing a meaningful hierarchy of task difficulty and pronounced differences in model capabilities. Our work can provide a standardized yardstick for evaluating gameplay capabilities across horizons and model families. We will release our dataset, annotator, and benchmark to facilitate future research.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [CHOREO: Every Humanoid Skill as a Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22274)
*Ziyi Sun,Jingwen Chen,Yuxi Wang,Xiuze Xia,Long Cheng,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Yujun Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHOREO提出免训练的组合框架，将异构人形技能统一为可执行运动轨迹表示SkillMotion，实现异构技能组合与多动作任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人技能虽丰富，但因强化学习、运动模仿、生成建模等来源的表示、接口与控制器不兼容而形成孤岛，难以组合复用。

Method: 观察到任何技能最终都可表示为可执行运动轨迹，CHOREO将各能力转化为SkillMotion统一表示（含运动状态、接触、语义、边界条件），并通过直接续接、三次Hermite混合或经校验的桥接运动进行组合，无需重训练源模型、测试时也不更新模型。

Result: 在MuJoCo的Unitree G1上组织来自异构来源的2950个准入SkillMotion资产，在130个多动作任务上取得95.4%序列成功率，其中八动作序列成功率93.8%。

Conclusion: 可执行轨迹为人形预训练能力的积累与组合提供了可扩展接口，但验证限于仿真环境，真实机器人迁移待检验。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics have produced diverse skills through reinforcement learning, motion imitation, and generative modeling. Yet these capabilities remain siloed because they are built around incompatible representations, interfaces, and controllers. We present CHOREO, a framework for training-free composition of heterogeneous humanoid skills. Our key observation is that, regardless of how a skill is learned, it can ultimately be expressed as an executable motion trajectory. Based on this observation, CHOREO converts each capability into SkillMotion, a unified representation that combines motion states, contacts, semantics, and boundary conditions. Skills are composed through direct continuation, cubic Hermite blending, or validated bridge motions, without retraining source models or updating models at test time. On Unitree G1 in MuJoCo, CHOREO organizes 2,950 admitted SkillMotion assets derived from heterogeneous sources and achieves 95.4\% sequence success across 130 multi-action tasks, including 93.8\% success on eight-action sequences. These results demonstrate that executable trajectories provide a scalable interface for accumulating and composing pretrained humanoid capabilities.

</details>


### [223] [D3DWA: Adaptive Weight and Prediction-Horizon for Dynamic Window Approach via Dueling Double Deep Q-Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22276)
*Zahra Jooyandeh,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出D3DWA，用Dueling Double DQN在每控制步从连续导航状态自适应选择DWA评价权重与预测时域，保留DWA轨迹生成与碰撞检测；8个仿真环境（含未见过布局）全部到达目标，真机3种配置全部完成，含权重单变量变体超时的受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DWA广泛用于局部导航，但参数通常在导航前固定。预测时域应随局部自由空间变化：开阔区域宜长时域以高效运动，狭窄/杂乱区域宜短时域以保留可行运动。因此需联合自适应评价权重与时域。

Method: D3DWA：基于Dueling Double Deep Q-Network（D3QN）的自适应DWA框架，在每个控制步根据连续导航状态联合选择DWA评价权重与预测时域，同时保留DWA原有的轨迹生成与碰撞检测模块作为底层执行器。

Result: 在8个仿真环境（包括未见过布局）中D3DWA均到达全部目标；真机实验完成全部3种测试配置，其中受限场景下仅调权重的变体超时。

Conclusion: 联合自适应评价权重与预测时域优于仅调权重，验证了时域自适应对受限空间导航的必要性。

Abstract: The Dynamic Window Approach (DWA) is widely used for local navigation, but its performance depends strongly on parameters that are typically fixed before navigation. In particular, the appropriate prediction horizon can vary with local free space: longer horizons support efficient motion in open areas, whereas shorter horizons help preserve feasible motions in narrow or cluttered regions. This paper proposes D3DWA, an adaptive DWA framework based on a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network (D3QN), which jointly selects the DWA evaluation weights and prediction horizon from a continuous navigation state at every control step while retaining DWA's trajectory generation and collision checking. In eight simulated environments, including unseen layouts, D3DWA reached every goal. Real-robot experiments further showed that D3DWA completed all three tested configurations, including a constrained case in which the weights-only variant timed out. These results demonstrate the benefit of jointly adapting the evaluation weights and prediction horizon. Additional material is available at this https URL

</details>


### [224] [DeViGrasp: Robust Visual Mobile Grasping for Quadruped Manipulators under Degraded Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22278)
*Liang Zhou,Jiaming Su,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出移动抓取退化视觉基准 DeViGrasp-Bench 与教师-学生框架 DeViGrasp-Net，通过特权教师监督与可靠性感知时序目标估计提升四足机械臂在视觉退化下的抓取鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机械臂全身控制策略依赖不稳定的机载视觉，现有方法多在较可靠观测下开发，未系统考察遮挡、分割掩码丢失、深度噪声与目标定位抖动对抓取推理与目标跟踪的影响。

Method: 构建 DeViGrasp-Bench，包含可控视觉退化、已见/未见物体、多难度等级与复杂地形，评价任务成功率、执行效率与动作平滑度。提出 DeViGrasp-Net 教师-学生框架：特权教师以物体、机器人、末端执行器与任务状态为条件关注离线抓取候选；可部署学生通过 Target Hold Memory 与 Temporal Memory Attention 融合双视角分割深度观测及当前、记忆、恢复三种目标假设。

Result: 在各退化等级、未见物体与复杂地形上优于 VBC，并在所有评估退化等级上超过适配的 DQ-Net；Difficult 设置成功率 62.3%，分别较 VBC 与 DQ-Net 提升 16.1 与 4.3 个百分点；Hard 设置对 DQ-Net 领先扩大至 10.5 个百分点。消融验证抓取感知监督与可靠性感知时序记忆的互补增益。

Conclusion: 显式建模视觉退化并以教师-学生加时序记忆方式融合目标假设，可显著增强四足移动机械臂的抓取鲁棒性；该基准为退化视觉下的移动抓取提供了系统评估平台。

Abstract: Quadruped manipulators enable mobile grasping in complex environments, yet their whole-body control policies remain vulnerable to unreliable onboard visual perception. Existing methods are typically developed under relatively reliable observations and have not systematically examined how occlusion, segmentation-mask dropout, depth noise, and target-localization jitter affect grasp reasoning and target tracking. To address this gap, we introduce DeViGrasp-Bench, a benchmark for mobile grasping under degraded vision that incorporates controlled visual degradations, seen and unseen objects, multiple difficulty levels, and complex terrains, and evaluates task success, execution efficiency, and action smoothness. We further propose DeViGrasp-Net, a teacher--student framework that combines state-conditioned grasp reasoning with reliability-aware temporal target estimation. The privileged teacher attends to offline grasp candidates conditioned on object, robot, end-effector, and task states, while the deployable student fuses dual-view segmented-depth observations with current, memory, and recovery target hypotheses through Target Hold Memory and Temporal Memory Attention. DeViGrasp-Net outperforms VBC across degradation levels, unseen objects, and complex terrains, and surpasses an adapted DQ-Net across all evaluated degradation levels. Under the Difficult setting, it achieves a success rate of 62.3\%, improving upon VBC and DQ-Net by 16.1 and 4.3 percentage points, respectively; under the Hard setting, its margin over DQ-Net increases to 10.5 percentage points. Ablation studies confirm the complementary benefits of grasp-aware supervision and reliability-aware temporal memory.

</details>


### [225] [ORDER: A Fictitious-World Benchmark for Domain-Adaptive Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22285)
*Sai Krishna Reddy Sathi,Anuj Tiwari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ORDER基准：基于虚构物理的自洽合成语料（342,069 token）以规避预训练污染，包含知识测试ORDER-BENCH（500题）与组合空间排序任务ORDER-SPATIAL，并在仿真iiwa7机械臂上验证"知识测试分数≠真实规划质量，ORDER-SPATIAL才是预测指标"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练适配新领域存在评估污染问题：若语料与模型既有知识重叠，性能提升无法区分"新学习"与"已有知识"。该问题在知识密集、任务轻（KHTL）的机器人场景（药品分发、危化品处置、设施专属规程）尤为关键——物理任务简单但规则专有且安全攸关，而实机测试昂贵甚至危险。因此需要一个不可能出现在任何预训练数据中的虚构物理世界作为干净评估载体。

Method: 构建ORDER（Ontology-driven Decision-making for Embodied Reasoning）：1) 342,069 token的虚构世界合成语料，定义自洽且无法被预训练覆盖的物理规律；2) ORDER-BENCH，500题知识测试；3) ORDER-SPATIAL，更难的对象安全操作排序组合任务，含熟悉场景与全新场景；4) 持续预训练小模型；5) 接入机器人流程，增加技能适配（skill-adaptation）阶段以生成可执行计划；6) 在仿真iiwa7机械臂上完成感知到执行的闭环，并含human-in-the-loop纠错；7) 对照GPT-4.1（含检索访问同一规则集）进行对比。

Result: 未适配的GPT-4.1在ORDER-SPATIAL上低于随机水平（Kendall's tau = 0.441），其先验与虚构物理主动冲突。持续预训练后小模型在熟悉与全新场景上均显著提升，表明是真实世界模型归纳而非记忆。知识测试高分模型常无法产出有效可执行计划，需技能适配阶段；适配后小型全离线模型在完整感知-执行闭环上超越带检索的GPT-4.1（tau = 0.848 vs. 0.606）。ORDER-SPATIAL表现而非知识测试准确率预测真实计划质量。

Conclusion: 用虚构物理语料可干净剥离预训练污染，验证持续预训练带来的是可迁移的世界模型归纳能力；同时揭示知识问答准确率与具身规划质量脱节，组合空间排序任务才是预测真实部署表现的指标，为KHTL机器人部署提供了评估范式与技能适配必要性证据。

Abstract: Adapting language models to new domains via continual pre-training raises a basic evaluation problem: if the training corpus overlaps with what the model already knows, performance gains cannot be cleanly attributed to new learning rather than pre-existing knowledge. This matters most for knowledge-intensive, task-light (KHTL) robot deployments - pharmaceutical dispensing, hazardous-material handling, facility-specific protocols, where the physical task is simple but the governing rules are proprietary and safety-critical, and where extensive live testing is costly or unsafe. We introduce ORDER (Ontology-driven Decision-making for Embodied Reasoning), a benchmark built on a fictitious world: a 342,069-token synthetic corpus defining a self-consistent physics that cannot appear in any model's pre-training data. ORDER pairs a 500-question knowledge test (ORDER-BENCH) with a harder compositional task, ORDER-SPATIAL: ordering objects for safe manipulation across both familiar and entirely novel scenes. GPT-4.1 without adaptation scores below chance on ORDER-SPATIAL (Kendall's tau = 0.441), showing its priors actively conflict with the invented physics. After continual pre-training, small models improve substantially on both familiar and novel scenes alike evidence of genuine world-model induction rather than memorization. We then carry this through to a robot pipeline: models that answer the knowledge test well often cannot produce valid, executable plans without a further skill-adaptation stage, after which small, fully offline models outperform GPT-4.1 even when GPT-4.1 is given retrieval access to the same rules (Kendall's tau = 0.848 vs. 0.606), on a full perception-to-execution loop demonstrated on a simulated iiwa7 arm with human-in-the-loop correction. Throughout, ORDER-SPATIAL performance, not knowledge-test accuracy is what predicts real plan quality.

</details>


### [226] [OJOx: Specification-Conditioned Demonstrations for Embodied AI in Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22289)
*Mohamed Dawod*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"规格条件化演示"(specification-conditioned demonstration)这一数据范式，并实现 OJOx 采集接口：将外部设计几何送入头显、锚定到物理工作区、以立体透视渲染给演示者，并与全身和手部运动同步录制；通过一次 33 组件砌墙实验验证记录与物理场景的一致性，数据可接入现有类人重定向流程并在 Unitree G1 仿真中回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模第一人称与全身人类演示已成为具身智能的主要数据源，但其只记录"人感知到什么、做了什么"，缺少赋予动作目的的外部规格(specification)。在建筑等场景中，技能指向的是设计模型中定义、但当前物理环境中尚不存在的目标构型；砌砖工可迁移的能力不是某面墙的几何，而是依据规格实现新几何的能力。因此需要一种把"感知状态—外部设计意图—行为"三者同步记录的数据接口，以检验策略能否学会朝向训练经验中不存在的规格行动。

Method: 1) 形式化"规格条件化演示"：同步记录演示者感知的物理状态、外部设计给出的意图状态、以及连接二者的行为。2) 实现 OJOx 采集接口：将设计几何传输至头显，锚定到物理工作空间，渲染进演示者的立体透视视图，并与全身及手部运动同步录制。3) 在一次完全仪器化的砌墙会话中采集数据（33 个组件，且规格在工作过程中动态变化）。4) 用独立配准的外部相机对捕获记录与真实物理场景做一致性校验。5) 验证数据与现有人形重定向基础设施的兼容性，并重定向回放至 Unitree G1 仿真。

Result: 完成一次全仪器化会话记录（33 组件墙体，规格边施工边变更）；经独立配准的外部相机核对，录制内容与物理场景一致；采集数据保持与现有人形重定向流程兼容，可重定向并在 Unitree G1 仿真中回放。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种面向建筑等领域的数据接口，使具身策略的评测从"模仿已演示动作"扩展到"朝训练中未出现的规格行动"；为规格条件化的具身学习提供了可复用的采集与兼容回放范式。

Abstract: Large-scale egocentric and whole-body human demonstrations are becoming a primary source of data for embodied intelligence. They record what people perceive and do, but rarely the external specification that gave an action its purpose. In construction that omission is consequential: skilled work is directed at project-specific configurations defined in a design model - configurations not yet present in the environment being observed. A mason's transferable competence is not the geometry of one wall but the ability to realise a new geometry from a specification. We introduce the specification-conditioned demonstration: a synchronised record of the physical state a demonstrator perceives, the intended state supplied to them by an external design, and the behaviour connecting the two. We present OJOx, a capture interface that realises this for construction - delivering design geometry to a headset, anchoring it in the physical workspace, rendering it into a demonstrator's stereo passthrough view, and recording that view synchronously with whole-body and hand motion. We report one fully instrumented session - a 33-component wall laid against a specification that changes while the work proceeds - and check the record against the physical scene through an external camera registered independently of the capture. Recorded sessions remain compatible with existing humanoid retargeting infrastructure and replay onto a Unitree G1 in simulation. The result is a data interface for testing whether embodied policies can learn not merely to imitate demonstrated actions, but to act toward specifications absent from their training experience.

</details>


### [227] [When Does Test-Time Physical Diagnosis Pay? A Frozen Policy Buys Evidence It Never Reads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22299)
*Zhengshu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出机器人物理诊断需满足六个有序经验条件，并在受控环境中端到端成立，但迁移至未见机制后在“证据使用”环节失效：冻结解码器不改变选择，而仅用轨迹增量的线性模型可恢复正确选择，说明证据有信息量却未被使用；失败集中在最丰富证据层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人面对陌生物理条件时通常先收集“什么变了”的证据再做适应，但这种诊断是否真正改善行为缺乏检验标准。作者希望把“有效诊断”拆解为一串可验证的经验条件，以便定位诊断链条具体在哪一环断裂，而非仅凭恢复精度或总体性能好坏下结论。

Method: 提出六个有序经验条件：有意义的参考、物理条件的可辨识性、所获证据的使用、决策价值、相对固定替代方案的选择价值、安全实现。在受控环境与公开环境中对该链条逐环测试；通过冻结解码器在需要完整轨迹的决策上是否改变选择来检验证据使用；再用仅含轨迹增量的线性模型检验轨迹本身的信息量。

Result: 在受控环境中链条端到端成立；迁移到未见机制后在证据使用环节断裂：完整轨迹决策上冻结解码器选择不变，而仅用轨迹增量的线性模型在未参与拟合的机制上恢复了正确选择，表明轨迹信息量充足但未被利用。失败集中在最丰富证据层级，这些决策降至随机水平，而低成本证据即可解决的决策仍正确，这一分化被总体准确率掩盖；在公开环境中链条可在其他环节失效。

Conclusion: 成功识别物理条件既不能保证证据被使用，也不能保证适应有效；评测应定位诊断链条的断点，而非依赖恢复精度或聚合性能指标。

Abstract: When a robot faces unfamiliar physical conditions, a common approach is to collect evidence about what changed and adapt. For such diagnosis to improve behavior, six ordered empirical conditions must hold: a meaningful reference, identifiability of the physical condition, use of the acquired evidence, decision value, selection value over a fixed alternative, and safe realization. We test this chain in controlled and public environments. It holds end to end in our controlled environments. After transfer to unseen mechanisms, however, it breaks at evidence use. On decisions requiring the full trace, the frozen decoder does not change its choice. A linear model using only trace increments recovers the correct choice on mechanisms excluded from fitting, showing that the trace is informative but unused. The failure is concentrated at the richest evidence level: those decisions fall to chance, while decisions settled with lower-cost evidence remain correct, a split hidden by aggregate accuracy. The same chain can fail at other links in public environments. Successful physical identification therefore guarantees neither evidence use nor useful adaptation; evaluation should identify where the chain breaks rather than rely on recovery accuracy or aggregate performance alone.

</details>


### [228] [EditWM: Event-Decomposed World Modeling with Incremental Correction for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22317)
*Junjie Yang,Qingwei Zeng,Youyou Li,Zicheng Ding,Ziyi Shi,Shuqi Shen,Hongliang Lu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EditWM 将驾驶世界模型分解为"正常演化"与"事件驱动增量修正"，在紧凑视觉特征空间中以冻结的轨迹条件预测器为基座，用带门控的修正解码器产生有界特征更新，并借候选特定交叉注意力把修正后的未来特征接入轨迹评分，在 NAVSIM navtest 上实现 5.35% 未来特征 MSE 下降与 91.05 EPDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景的可预测性并不均匀：多数时刻是常规演化，少数时刻由事件引发的偏差才需要纠正。统一的世界模型把两者混在一起预测，既浪费容量也易在关键事件处失真，因此需要显式区分"常态预测"与"选择性修正"。

Method: 两阶段分解式世界模型。(1) 轨迹条件正常预测器给出基础未来特征，随后冻结作为残差基座；(2) 修正解码器对比该预测与观测历史、规划动作，输出有界特征增量，其贡献由学习到的门控调节；(3) 修正后的未来特征通过候选特定交叉注意力参与轨迹评分，将世界建模与规划选择耦合。推理时仅用历史与当前观测、自车状态和候选轨迹，不需未来信息。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest 全部 12,146 个场景上，以专家轨迹为条件的评测显示未来特征 MSE 较 Normal 基线下降 5.35%，且 83.54% 的场景获得改善；官方 EPDMS 评测器上取得 91.05/100。

Conclusion: 结果说明将既有预测部分冻结、只学习事件驱动修正是一种有效且经济的世界模型范式，修正后的未来表征接入规划后能同时提升未来特征预测质量与轨迹选择表现。

Abstract: World models support autonomous driving by predicting the scene evolution associated with candidate trajectories. Driving dynamics differ in predictability, motivating a distinction between regular evolution and event-induced deviations that call for selective correction. We propose EditWM, a world model that decomposes future prediction into normal evolution and event-driven incremental correction in compact visual feature space. A trajectory-conditioned normal predictor provides the base forecast and is then frozen for correction learning. A correction decoder compares this forecast with observation history and planned actions, producing a bounded feature update whose contribution is regulated by a learned gate. The corrected future features condition trajectory scoring through candidate-specific cross-attention, linking world modeling to plan selection. At inference, EditWM uses only past and current observations, ego state, and candidate trajectories. Across all 12,146 NAVSIM navtest scenes, expert-trajectory-conditioned evaluation shows a 5.35\% reduction in future-feature MSE over Normal, with improvements in 83.54\% of scenes. The system achieves 91.05 EPDMS on a 100-point scale using the official EPDMS evaluator. These results demonstrate improved future-feature prediction and competitive trajectory selection when corrected future representations are integrated into planning.

</details>


### [229] [Embedding Physics Priors in Robot Learning: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22319)
*Mattia Piccinini,Lucas Schulze,Alice Plebe,Matteo Saveriano,Thomas Beckers,Yuan Gao,Oleg Arenz,Baha Zarrouki,Dingrui Wang,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Jan Peters,Johannes Betz,Gastone Pietro Rosati Papini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统梳理"物理嵌入机器人学习"（physics-embedded robot learning），提出统一分类法（物理引导的输入/数据/表征、物理编码的模型架构、物理约束训练损失），覆盖动力学学习、轨迹规划、预测、控制与估计及开源生态，并总结挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动方法在视觉与NLP上成果显著，但机器人面临数据稀缺、真实交互复杂、可靠性要求高等瓶颈。将物理先验嵌入学习算法可提供机器人特有的归纳偏置，有望提升泛化性、可解释性与样本效率。然而相关文献在术语、方法与应用域上高度碎片化，难以整体评估，因此需要一篇统一视角的综述。

Method: 综述性研究：以"物理嵌入方式"为轴建立统一分类法——(1) 物理引导的输入、数据与表征；(2) 物理编码的模型架构；(3) 物理约束/物理信息的训练损失函数。在此分类框架下，跨先验类型、机器人应用与模型族（从单层感知机到生成式基础模型）系统回顾机器人动力学学习、轨迹规划、预测、控制与状态估计方法，并对应梳理开源软件生态。

Result: 构建了覆盖物理先验、机器人任务与机器学习模型的统一分类体系；系统归类并对比了动力学学习、规划、预测、控制、估计等方向的代表性方法；梳理了相关开源软件生态；识别出若干关键开放挑战并给出未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 物理先验是尤其契合机器人领域的归纳偏置，是对数据驱动学习的补充而非替代，为构建更具泛化性、数据高效且可信赖的机器人系统提供了路径。

Abstract: The rapid progress of artificial intelligence is reshaping robotics and accelerating the adoption of learning-based approaches. While purely data-driven methods have achieved remarkable success in computer vision and natural language processing, robotics remains constrained by limited data, complex real-world interactions, and the need for reliable operation. These challenges have motivated the exploration of physics-embedded robot learning, which embeds physics priors into learning algorithms. By encoding the underlying physical laws and constraints, physics priors can complement limited data with robotics-specific inductive biases, potentially improving generalization, interpretability, and sample efficiency. However, the literature on physics-embedded robot learning remains fragmented across terminology, methodologies, and application domains, making it difficult to assess this growing body of work. This survey reviews physics-embedded robot learning across a broad range of physics priors, robotics applications, and machine learning models, from single-layer perceptrons to generative foundation models. We adopt a unified taxonomy that classifies existing approaches according to their physics embedding: physics-guided inputs, data, and representations; physics-encoded model architectures; and physics-informed training loss functions. Building on this taxonomy, we review methods for robot dynamics learning, trajectory planning, prediction, control, and estimation, together with the corresponding open-source software ecosystem. We identify key open challenges, and outline promising future research directions. Overall, we argue that physics priors provide a particularly relevant robotics-specific inductive bias, complementing rather than replacing data-driven learning, and paving the way toward more generalizable, data-efficient, and trustworthy robotic systems.

</details>


### [230] [ReliCAD: From Uncertain LLM Generation to Reliable Parametric CAD Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22325)
*Peng Zheng,Xintong Dong,Chuanyang Li,Jiaxin Jing,Chuqi Han,Hailong Shen,Yanzhi Song,Zhouwang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReliCAD 将 LLM 的概率式生成转化为可靠的参数化 CAD 建模：通过显式设计意图建模生成约束感知参数化指令，调用 CAD 内核求解，并用运行时证据做验证-反馈迭代修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的随机生成与 CAD 的确定性需求存在根本矛盾，导致可靠性、设计意图保持与几何有效性三方面不足；现有方法依赖大规模标注数据、缺乏对设计意图的显式建模、且未充分利用 CAD 内核的确定性能力。

Method: 显式设计意图建模（把用户需求转为结构化设计规范，显式建模几何关系、拓扑依赖与特征构建顺序）→ 生成约束感知的参数化指令 → 通过 Agent-ready API 调用 CAD 内核完成几何构建与约束求解 → 记录运行时证据，以验证-反馈机制检查模型与设计规范的一致性，实现错误定位与迭代修复。

Result: 在公开 HistCAD 生成数据集及自建多粒度 CAD 编辑数据集上显著优于基线，达到 99.8% 有效率与 0.8753 IoU。

Conclusion: ReliCAD 提供了一条可验证、可修复、可泛化的自然语言交互 CAD 建模路径，核心思路是用结构化规范与内核约束求解约束 LLM 的自由度。

Abstract: Large language models have shown considerable potential for natural-language-driven parametric CAD modeling. However, a fundamental contradiction exists between their probabilistic generation and the deterministic requirements of CAD modeling, resulting in limitations in reliability, design-intent preservation, and geometric validity. Existing methods typically rely on large-scale annotated datasets, lack explicit modeling of design intent, and underutilize the deterministic capabilities of CAD kernels. To address these limitations, we propose ReliCAD, a unified framework that transforms uncertain LLM generation into reliable parametric CAD modeling. Through explicit design-intent modeling, ReliCAD converts user requirements into structured design specifications and explicitly models geometric relations, topological dependencies, and feature construction order. It then generates constraint-aware parametric instructions and invokes the CAD kernel through an Agent-ready API to perform geometric construction and constraint solving. ReliCAD further records runtime evidence and employs a verification-feedback mechanism to assess consistency between the generated model and the design specifications, enabling error localization and iterative repair. Experiments on the public HistCAD generation dataset and our multi-granularity CAD editing dataset demonstrate that ReliCAD significantly outperforms baseline methods, achieving 99.8\% validity rate and 0.8753 IoU. ReliCAD provides a verifiable, repairable, and generalizable approach to natural-language-interactive CAD modeling.

</details>


### [231] [AffordanceWAM: Affordance-Aware Joint World-Action Modeling for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22332)
*Jiadi You,Qize Yu,Yue Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhide Zhong,Yuran Wang,Bowen Ping,Jiaqi Liang,Zhenhao Shen,Haodong Yan,Yinchuan Li,Ruihai Wu,Xiaojuan Qi,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordanceWam 将“可供性”作为人与机器人视频之间共享的交互接口，在预训练视频扩散 Transformer 上以 World/Action 双专家与掩码联合自注意力统一预测未来 RGB、标量可供性、可供性热图与连续动作，实现无需人类动作标注的人到机器人迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作标注的机器人视频监督信号直接但采集昂贵、多样性差；第一视角人类视频交互多样却缺少动作标签且存在具身与外观差异。因此需要一个跨具身共享的中间表示来连接视觉预测与动作生成，并充分利用两类数据。

Method: 提出 AffordanceWam：基于预训练视频扩散 Transformer，用独立参数化的 World Expert 与 Action Expert，通过 Masked Joint Self-Attention 耦合，在统一 flow-matching 目标下联合预测未来 RGB 观测、Scalar Affordance 场、Affordance Heatmap 与连续机器人动作；以对象为中心的可供性表示将视觉预测锚定到任务相关物体与交互区域。人类视频监督三条未来世界流，机器人轨迹额外汇入动作监督。

Result: 在 RoboCasa、CALVIN ABC→D 与真实机器人操作上均稳定优于仅 RGB 与仅机器人数据基线；在固定机器人监督下，RoboCasa 性能随可供性标注人类视频规模单调提升。

Conclusion: 可供性可作为视觉-语言-动作学习与人到机器人迁移的有效共享接口，使生成式世界模型在缺少机器人动作标签时仍可获取多样化人类交互知识。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires predicting how a scene will evolve, identifying where interactions are feasible, and determining how to act. Action-labeled robot videos directly supervise control but are costly and limited in diversity, whereas egocentric human videos capture diverse interactions but lack robot actions and differ in embodiment and appearance. We introduce AffordanceWAM, an affordance-aware generative World Action Model that represents object-centric spatiotemporal affordance through Scalar Affordance and Affordance Heatmap, within the generated future World. This representation grounds visual prediction in task-relevant objects and interaction regions for action generation, and provides shared interaction targets across human and robot videos. Built on a pretrained video diffusion Transformer, AffordanceWAM uses separately parameterized World and Action Experts, coupled through Masked Joint Self-Attention, to jointly predict future RGB observations, Scalar Affordance fields, Affordance Heatmaps, and continuous robot actions under a unified flow-matching objective. Human videos supervise all three future-World streams, whereas robot trajectories additionally provide action supervision, enabling transfer without human action labels or retargeting. Experiments on RoboCasa, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation demonstrate consistent gains over RGB-only and robot-data-only baselines. Under fixed robot supervision, RoboCasa performance improves monotonically as affordance-annotated human video scales. These results support affordance as an effective interface for both vision-language-action learning and human-to-robot transfer.

</details>


### [232] [Active Spatial Inspection for Effective and Efficient Embodied Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22385)
*Wenbin Wang,Xiang Bai,Yizhao Wang,Hang Sun,Dong Ren,Jie Qin,Qingquan Li,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出空间显式引导的主动探索框架 ACE，通过证据锚定的感知与暴露度引导的移动，构建空间可分辨的决策范式，缓解探索中"过早终止"与"过度延续"的矛盾，在导航与问答探索任务上显著超越已有最优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身探索框架依赖空间粗略、间接的提示线索与方向评估来引导智能体行为，难以判断线索充分性以及是否需要进一步检查，因而导致过早终止或过度探索，降低任务成功率与效率。

Method: 从空间显式视角重构具身探索，提出 ACE（空间检查引导的主动探索）框架，将证据锚定的感知与暴露度引导的移动耦合，建立空间可分辨的决策范式。感知端融合细粒度定位与聚焦验证，把提示性线索转化为精确的决策性视觉证据；移动端通过前瞻性优先化与回溯性抑制进行方向判断，筛选有前景方向以推进高效空间进展。

Result: 大量实验显示，ACE 相比此前最先进基线在导航任务成功率上提升 18.0%，在问答式探索效率上提升 10.3%。

Conclusion: ACE 通过空间显式证据与暴露度驱动的移动策略，有效缓解防止过早终止与避免无谓延续之间的张力，推进了具身探索的高效与有效。

Abstract: Achieving high task success and efficiency remains a central pursuit in embodied exploration. Existing frameworks typically guide agent behavior through a spatially coarse and indirect assessment of suggestive cues and directions, yet such designs may struggle to judge cue sufficiency and the need for further inspection, leading to early termination or excessive continuation and ultimately reducing task success and efficiency. This work rethinks embodied exploration from a spatially explicit standpoint and introduces the spatial-inspection-guided ACtive Exploration (ACE) framework coupling evidence-grounded perception with exposure-informed movement and establishing a spatially resolved decision paradigm. Evidence-grounded perception strengthens inferential validity by integrating granular localization with focused verification, turning suggestive cues into precise decisive visual support. Exposure-informed movement promotes directional judgment through prospective prioritization and retrospective suppression, selecting promising directions for efficient spatial progress. ACE alleviates the tension between preventing early termination and avoiding unnecessary continuation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACE achieves 18.0% higher navigation task success and 10.3% higher exploration efficiency for question answering than prior state-of-the-art baselines, advancing effective and efficient embodied exploration.

</details>


### [233] [Behavior Trees for Robotic Systems: An Empirical Study on Practices and Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22404)
*Razan Ghzouli,Jennifer Horkoff,Daniel Strüber,Rebekka Wohlrab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一项混合方法实证研究：结合某汽车公司的技术行动研究（TAR）与34名机器人从业者的问卷，考察行为树（BT）在实际机器人系统中的采纳情况、设计/集成决策与工具痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年BT已成为协调机器人任务的主流行为模型，但缺乏来自学术界与工业界的从业者实证证据；证据稀缺导致实践随意化，阻碍软件复用、维护与演化，也使研究社区易产出与实践脱节的指南与工具。

Method: 混合方法研究：在汽车企业开展技术行动研究（technical action research），并对34名机器人从业者进行问卷调查；围绕架构与语言选择、ROS集成、粒度决策、库使用与规划算法等主题归纳观察到的模式。

Result: BT提升团队沟通与机器人决策逻辑的可理解性，价值超出任务协调本身；但从业者面临大量非平凡的设计与集成决策，缺乏充分指南与工具支持。具体包括：架构/语言选择与ROS集成中的模式化决策、多因素粒度决策、对现有库喜忧参半的体验（实现难题与文档缺口）、BT节点最优排序难以判断；可扩展性观点尚无定论，现有库与实践局限阻碍更广泛采纳。

Conclusion: 给出跨切面观察与启示：为采纳BT的从业者提供设计/集成参考，为研究者指出需推进BT采纳实证理解的方向。

Abstract: Over the last decade, behavior trees (BT) have become one of the dominant behavior models for coordinating missions of robotic systems. Yet empirical evidence on BT adoption in real-world contexts remains limited, especially regarding practitioners experiences and practices. Without practitioner-grounded evidence from both academic and industrial settings, the research community risks developing guidelines and tools that are plausible in principle but only partly aligned with real challenges. This scarcity of evidence leads to ad-hoc practices, impeding software reuse, maintenance, and evolution. This paper reports a mixed-methods study combining a technical action research investigation at an automotive company with a survey of 34 robotics practitioners. Our results indicate that BTs improve team communication and the understandability of robotic decision-making logic, reflecting BTs practical value beyond mission coordination. At the same time, practitioners face multiple non-trivial design and integration decisions, complicated by the lack of adequate guidelines and tool support. These decisions span architectural and language choices when implementing BTs and integrating them within ROS, for which we report observed patterns. Additionally, practitioners faced multi-factor granularity decisions and mixed experiences with current libraries. For the most used BT libraries, they reported implementation challenges and documentation gaps. Regarding planning algorithms, practitioners find deciding optimal BT node ordering challenging. Views on scalability remained inconclusive, and limitations in current libraries and practices hinder broader adoption. We conclude with cross-cutting observations and implications for both practitioners adopting BTs in robotic systems and researchers aiming to advance empirical understanding of BT adoption.

</details>


### [234] [VLPSA: Vision-Language-Poisson-Safe Actions for Full-Body Safety of Learned Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22462)
*Meg Wilkinson,Emily Fourney,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLPSA安全过滤框架，在线合成Poisson安全函数并双分辨率布尔组合成CBF，通过CBF-QP实现对VLA策略全身（含抓取物体）的实时避碰，无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略泛化能力增强，但对碰撞避让缺乏安全保证，尤其在训练分布之外的环境中。现有安全过滤方法难以在杂乱、动态场景中兼顾全身安全与实时性。

Method: 从感知数据在线合成Poisson Safety Functions（PSF），构造Control Barrier Function；以CBF-QP安全滤波器约束全身及被夹持物体（视为末端连杆延伸）；用布尔CBF组合实现关键任务区域的高空间分辨率与双分辨率并行，保证实时性。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO上优于对比安全滤波基线，避碰率由基础π0.5策略的23.1%提升至91.2%，任务成功率亦超出基线；在Franka FR3真实平台上于杂乱场景、动态障碍与人为干扰下实现实时全身安全操作。

Conclusion: VLPSA为VLA策略提供无需重训练的全身安全过滤，兼顾空间分辨率与实时性，并在仿真基准与真实机器人上验证有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable increasingly general-purpose robotic manipulation, but such learned policies do not provide safety guarantees for collision avoidance---especially in environments outside of training distributions. This work presents Vision-Language-Poisson-Safe Actions (VLPSA), a safety filtering framework that provides full-body safety for VLA policies in cluttered and dynamic environments without retraining. VLPSA synthesizes Poisson Safety Functions (PSF) online from perception data, yielding a Control Barrier Function (CBF) that is enforced through a CBF-QP safety filter over the full body and any grasped object, treated as an extension of the final robot link. To enable real-time deployment while maintaining fine spatial resolution in critical task regions, VLPSA combines dual resolutions of this PSF using Boolean CBF compositions. We evaluate VLPSA on SafeLIBERO against safety-filtering baselines, where it achieves the highest collision avoidance rate among the evaluated methods, increasing collision avoidance from 23.1% for the base $\pi_{0.5}$ policy to 91.2% while surpassing its task success rate. We further deploy VLPSA on a Franka FR3 in cluttered scenes with dynamic obstacles and human interference, demonstrating real-time full-body safety during manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [235] [Tracker-Free Robotic Ultrasound Calibration with a Spherical-Marker Phantom and Threshold-Free Center Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22483)
*Kyoungmo Koo,Guangshen Ma,Xueding Wang,Mark Draelos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无外部追踪器的机器人超声标定框架：用球形标记模体+无阈值图像处理定位球心，多姿态观测估计US-to-EE变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声标定依赖复杂模体或外部3D追踪器，难以实现准确、自动化的标定。

Method: 基于球形标记模体与高自动化感知流程；无阈值图像处理方法依据各US图像灰度分布定位球形特征，避免手工阈值与逐系统重调；利用多姿态球标志观测直接估计US到末端执行器的变换，无需外部追踪或机器人基座系下球心先验。

Result: 12折交叉验证：单标记标定球心精度1.75±0.51 mm、精密度0.85±0.11 mm；三标记为1.72±0.51 mm与0.84±0.12 mm。三组重建中单标记配准后点到面MAE：锥体0.41±0.35 mm、三棱柱0.50±0.41 mm，与三标记的0.40±0.35 mm、0.48±0.40 mm接近。

Conclusion: 无需外部追踪、无需机器人基座系下球心先验的自动化机器人超声标定可行，单标记即可达到与多标记相当的精度，具有跨成像设置的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound (US) calibration is essential for accurately relating US images to the robot coordinate system, but accurate and automated calibration remains challenging because existing methods often require complex phantoms or external 3D trackers. In this work, we develop a tracker-free robotic US calibration framework using a spherical-marker phantom and a highly automated perception pipeline for sphere-center localization. The proposed threshold-free image-processing method localizes the spherical feature based on each US image's intensity distribution, eliminating hand-tuned intensity thresholds and improving robustness across imaging settings without per-system retuning. Multi-pose observations of the spherical fiducial are then used to estimate the US-to-EE transformation without external tracking or prior localization of the sphere center in the robot base frame. We validate the proposed framework on a robotic US platform through repeated sphere scans and further assess the calibrated system using geometrically distinct phantoms with known CAD models. Across 12 cross-validation folds, single-marker calibration achieved sphere-center accuracy and precision of $1.75\pm0.51$ mm and $0.85\pm0.11$ mm, respectively, compared with $1.72\pm0.51$ mm and $0.84\pm0.12$ mm for three-marker calibration. Across the three reconstruction sets, the single-marker calibration yielded pooled post-registration point-to-surface MAE$\pm$SD values of $0.41\pm0.35$ mm for the cone and $0.50\pm0.41$ mm for the triangular prism, closely matching the three-marker results of $0.40\pm0.35$ mm and $0.48\pm0.40$ mm, respectively.

</details>


### [236] [Layered e-skin for Shear Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22493)
*Qingzheng Cong,Alexis W. M. Devillard,Abu Bakar Dawood,Xinxin Zhang,Wen Fan,Neri Niccolò Dei,Cem Suulker,Kaspar Althoefer,Etienne Burdet,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双层堆叠FSR阵列指尖触觉传感器，利用层间压力中心(CoP)相对位移测量剪切力，结合物理力矩平衡与CNN-GRU模型实现三轴力估计、接触运动跟踪与滑移检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FSR阵列仅能感知法向压力，难以估计剪切力与滑移，限制了机器人在抓取与操作中的接触状态感知能力。需要一种结构简单、可在指尖集成并同时提供高分辨率压力图与剪切力信息的触觉方案。

Method: 在柔性点阵弹性体隔层上堆叠两层FSR阵列：剪切载荷使隔层变形，导致两层CoP产生相对位移。用基于物理的力矩平衡将层间CoP位移映射为剪切力，并训练CNN-GRU端到端模型补偿载荷相关压缩与接触重分布等非线性。以双层信号为输入进行三轴力回归，并开展机械臂操作实验对比单层基线、跟踪接触运动与检测滑移事件。

Result: 双层输入模型得到Fx的R²=0.914、Fy的R²=0.944，持续优于单层基线。操作实验中，深层跟踪机械臂施加的平移与旋转，浅层跟踪接触面滑移；两层总压力响应之差的瞬态变化在测试线索中滑移检测表现最佳。

Conclusion: 双层FSR阵列可超越常规法向力感知，同时实现三轴力估计、接触运动跟踪与滑移事件检测，为机器人指尖提供低成本多功能触觉感知路径。

Abstract: This paper presents a stacked two-layer force-sensing resistor (FSR) array designed for robotic fingertips that combines high-resolution pressure mapping with shear-force estimation. A compliant lattice elastomer spacer converts shear loading into a measurable inter-layer displacement, producing relative center-of-pressure (CoP) shifts between layers. A physics-based moment balance links inter-layer CoP displacement to shear force, while an end-to-end CNN--GRU model captures nonlinear effects from load-dependent compression and contact redistribution. This model, with both layers as input, achieves coefficients of determination $R^2 = 0.914$ for $F_x$ and $R^2 = 0.944$ for $F_y$, consistently outperforming single-layer baselines for shear-force estimation. Robotic manipulation experiments show that, for contact-motion tracking, the deep layer tracks the translation and rotation imposed by the robot arm, whereas the superficial layer tracks the slip at the contact surface. Transient changes in the difference between the total pressure responses of the two layers provide the best slip-event detection performance among the tested cues. These results demonstrate that two-layer FSR arrays can provide three-axis force estimation, contact-motion tracking, and slip-event detection beyond conventional normal-force sensing.

</details>


### [237] [Latent Policy Steering: An Efficient and Flexible Framework for Cross-Embodiment Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22521)
*Yiqi Wang,Mrinal Verghese,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Policy Steering (LPS)，一个 embodiment-agnostic 的跨本体迁移框架：先在多本体数据上用光流预训练图像世界模型，再在目标本体少量数据上微调，测试时在世界模型潜空间搜索最优动作序列来引导任意基础策略，仅用 50 条演示即显著提升 Diffusion Policy 与 Pi0.5 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视觉运动策略的性能高度依赖大规模高质量演示数据，但真实机器人数据采集成本高昂。已有的大规模机器人与人类数据集因具身差异（embodiment gap）和动作空间不匹配而难以直接利用。因此，如何复用其他具身形态的经验来帮助目标具身的学习（cross-embodiment transfer）成为扩展机器人学习规模的关键问题。

Method: LPS 分两阶段：(1) 具身无关预训练——在多种具身数据上训练基于图像的世界模型，以光流为监督学习"世界如何响应运动"的共享视觉动力学；(2) 目标具身微调——用目标机器人的动作数据微调该世界模型。测试时进行潜空间策略引导：在世界模型的潜空间中搜索那些与微调数据分布接近的动作规划，从而把基础策略（无需重训）导向更优动作。该框架与策略无关，可灵活适配不同基座策略。

Result: 在 Robomimic 仿真与真实世界评测中，仅在未见过的目标具身上使用 50 条演示，LPS 将 Diffusion Policy 的平均性能相对提升 16%（仿真）与 62%（真机），将 Pi0.5 相对提升 8%（仿真）与 14%（真机）。

Conclusion: 通过学习跨具身共享的视觉动力学并在测试时有效利用稀缺的目标具身数据，LPS 提供了一种策略无关、可插拔的跨具身迁移方案，显著缓解了机器人学习中的数据瓶颈，为超越单机器人数据采集的规模化学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The performance of learned robot visuomotor policies depends heavily on the size and quality of their training data, yet collecting high-quality demonstrations remains costly for robots in the real world. Although large-scale robot and human datasets are increasingly available, embodiment gaps and mismatched action spaces make them difficult to leverage directly. Cross-embodiment transfer, reusing experience from other embodiments to improve learning on a target embodiment, is therefore crucial for scaling robot learning beyond per-robot data collection. In this work, we find that efficient transfer can be achieved by learning from what is shared across embodiments, the visual dynamics of how the world responds to motion, and by effectively exploiting the scarce target-embodiment data at test time. The proposed framework, called Latent Policy Steering (LPS), implements an embodiment-agnostic pretraining phase, which trains an image-based World Model (WM) with optical flow across diverse embodiments. The resulting WM is finetuned on the target embodiment with robot actions. It then steers the base policy toward better actions by searching in the WM's latent space for plans that stay close to the finetuning data. LPS is a policy-agnostic framework: it can flexibly accommodate different policies without having to retrain them. In Robomimic and real-world evaluations, LPS improves the average performance of Diffusion Policy relatively by 16% and 62%, and Pi0.5 by 8% and 14%, with only 50 demonstrations on an unseen target embodiment.

</details>


### [238] [FRAMES: Failure Recovery And Monitoring of Embodied Skills for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22538)
*Ajay Vikram Periasami,Xinyuan Luo,Haoyu Li,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRAMES 提出面向 Unitree G1 人形机器人的失效感知监督框架，位于 CEER 全身控制器之上，由 Planner、Monitor、Recovery 三个智能体及 Memory 模块组成；在 MuJoCo 中 100 次试验的监测模块评测达到 94.0% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 规划器虽能分解自然语言指令并选择可复用技能，但技能选对并不保证物理执行成功。人形 loco-manipulation 中长时程任务对错误高度敏感，接近、抓取、搬运、放置任一步失败都会使后续计划失效，因此需要执行级的失效监督与恢复。

Method: FRAMES 在 CEER 全身控制器之上构建监督层：Planner Agent 通过参数化中层技能选择子任务；基于 VLM 的 Monitor Agent 利用时序多视角观测与结构化的机器人状态、接触证据评估每个技能；检测到失效即终止当前技能并向 Recovery Agent 提供有依据的反馈。框架还包含复用历史技能经验的 Memory Module，以及基于深度与分割的几何 grounding。

Result: 仅独立评测监测模块：MuJoCo 中 100 次试验（5 类任务，50 次失败、50 次成功）。监测器检出 48/50 失败，正确接受 46/50 成功，总体准确率 94.0%。端到端完整恢复闭环的评测仍在进行中。

Conclusion: 结果初步支持监测组件的有效性，但完整恢复回路尚未端到端验证，且仅在仿真中进行，缺少真机演示与系统级收益证据，整体仍属早期工作。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) planners can decompose natural-language instructions and select reusable robot skills, but choosing the correct skill does not guarantee successful physical execution. This gap is especially important in humanoid loco-manipulation, where errors during approach, grasping, transport, or placement can invalidate the remainder of a long-horizon plan. We present FRAMES, a failure-aware supervisory framework for the Unitree G1 humanoid that operates above the CEER whole-body controller. A Planner Agent selects subtasks through parameterized mid-level skills, while a vision-language-model-based Monitor Agent evaluates each skill using temporal multi-view observations and structured robot and contact evidence. Detected failures stop the active skill and provide grounded feedback to a Recovery Agent. The framework further includes a Memory Module for reusing prior skill experience, and geometric grounding via depth and segmentation. We independently evaluate the monitoring module of the framework in MuJoCo using 100 trials comprising 50 failed and 50 successful executions across five tasks. The monitor detects 48 of 50 failures, correctly accepts 46 of 50 successful executions, and achieves 94.0% overall accuracy. These results provide initial evidence for the monitoring component, while end-to-end evaluation of the complete recovery loop remains ongoing.

</details>


### [239] [React When You Need To: Event-Triggered Asynchronous Inference for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22587)
*Yansong Wu,Huaqing Li,Tianding Hou,Lingyun Chen,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出事件引导的动态推理策略，根据场景变化自适应调整 VLA 模型的推理间隔，兼顾动作一致性与实时响应，真实环境平均成功率 95%，超最强基线 55 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型通常预测动作块（action chunk），执行期间难以对环境变化做出反应；现有异步推理方法虽提升响应性，但依赖固定的推理间隔，无法在场景突变时及时重规划、在场景静止时又浪费算力。

Method: 提出事件引导的动态推理策略：以前一次推理以来观测到的场景变化为“事件”，据此自适应调节下一次推理的时间间隔（推理间隙），从而在运动一致性与及时响应之间取得平衡。

Result: 在静态与动态的真实世界场景中均表现最优，平均成功率 95%，超出最强基线 55 个百分点。

Conclusion: 事件驱动的自适应推理间隔可有效提升 VLA 在动态环境中的反应性与成功率，代码将在接收后开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly predict action chunks, limiting their ability to react to environmental changes during execution. Existing asynchronous inference methods improve reactivity but typically rely on a fixed inference gap. In this paper, we propose an event-guided dynamic inference strategy that adapts the inference gap according to scene changes observed since the previous inference. Thereby, it simultaneously preserves motion consistency and prompt reactivity. Across static and dynamic real-world settings, our method consistently performs best, averaging 95% success and exceeding the strongest baseline by 55 percentage points. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [240] [REBOOT: From Failure to Recovery - A Dataset and Benchmark for Precision Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22591)
*Nana Yaw Owusu Ofori-Ampofo,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Joseph Thekinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REBOOT 提出首个以“失败”为一等信号的机器人操作基准，涵盖 18 个精密装配任务、2,160 条演示，按五个共享阶段分解并配以专家恢复轨迹与阶段级/失效模式标注，用于阶段级评估与失效归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习策略的失败具有特征性（不确定状态下停滞、接触对齐漂移、精密任务毫米级偏差），但训练数据以成功演示为主、真实基准多将性能简化为二值成功率，既缺少恢复监督，也无法分析失败发生位置。作者希望把失败作为一等信号来构建数据集与评测。

Method: 构建 REBOOT 基准：18 个精密装配任务共 2,160 条演示，每任务分解为 Align(pick)、Engage(pick)、Transport、Align(place)、Engage(place) 五个共享阶段；在各阶段注入失败并配以专家恢复轨迹使系统回到有效延续状态；标注旋转对称性、贴合间隙精度层级、装配方向（install-remove 配对）；失败片段标注阶段与类别化失效模式；数据含四视角同步 RGB-D 与阶段级成功/失败条件的自然语言描述；一半为专家演示，一半为按模仿学习策略 rollout 失败分布采样的恢复演示；以阶段级完成率评测 action-chunked transformer、diffusion 与 π0-FAST 策略。

Result: 阶段级完成率揭示了二值评估所掩盖的模型特异性失效点：不同策略（transformer / diffusion / π0-FAST）在不同阶段与失效模式上表现差异明显。

Conclusion: 将失败结构化、阶段化并配以恢复轨迹，可为机器人策略提供恢复监督与细粒度失效归因；REBOOT 作为面向 off-nominal 轨迹的基准，支持超越终端成功率的阶段级评测。

Abstract: Robot learning policies fail in characteristic ways: they stall in uncertain states, drift during contact-rich alignment, and miss targets by millimetres in precision tasks. Yet training datasets consist largely of successful demonstrations, while real-world benchmarks often reduce performance to binary success.
