This limits both supervision for recovery and analysis of where failures occur. We introduce REBOOT (Recovery Episode Benchmark for Off-nominal Trajectories), the first robot manipulation benchmark designed around failure as a first-class signal. REBOOT contains 2,160 demonstrations across 18 precision assembly tasks, each decomposed into five shared phases: Align(pick), Engage(pick), Transport, Align(place), and Engage(place), enabling phase-level evaluation beyond terminal success. Failures are introduced across phases and paired with expert recovery trajectories that return the system to a valid continuation state. Tasks are annotated with rotational symmetry, engagement-clearance precision tier, and assembly direction through matched install-remove pairs. Failure episodes are labeled by phase and categorical failure mode, enabling attribution to kinematic stage and tolerance violation. Data includes synchronized RGB-D observations from four viewpoints and grounded natural-language descriptions of phase-level success and failure conditions. Half the dataset contains expert demonstrations; the other half contains recovery demonstrations sampled to reflect failures observed in imitation-learned policy rollouts. We benchmark action-chunked transformer, diffusion, and $\pi_0$-FAST policies using phase-level completion rates, revealing model-specific failure points hidden by binary evaluation. Dataset and code: this https URL

</details>


### [241] [Characterizing Wildlife Response to Biomimetic and Conventional Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22594)
*Huy Pham,Levi Cai,Yogesh Girdhar,Daniela Rus,Zach J. Patterson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个对比传统螺旋桨AUV、仿生海龟鳍肢AUV与潜水员对鱼类行为干扰的野外数据集与基准，发现两者均引起可测行为变化，未校正时仿生AUV干扰略小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV可替代潜水员进行可扩展的海洋生态数据采集，但机器人可能惊扰本地鱼群、造成与其他监测手段显著不同的行为差异，削弱其作为科学工具的价值。作者假设采用非捕食性动物的形态与行为（仿生）可降低生物的不利反应，因此需要定量比较不同平台造成的干扰。

Method: 在加勒比珊瑚礁生物多样性热点区域开展野外实验，采集三类干预下的场景图像：常规推进器驱动AUV、受海龟启发的鳍肢驱动仿生AUV、以及潜水员。使用计算机视觉方法对图像中的鱼类进行定位，并量化比较其行为变化，进行多组间统计比较与多重比较校正，同时给出一组基准。

Result: 两种AUV均导致可测量的鱼类行为变化；经多重比较校正后机器人间差异不再显著，但点估计总体偏向仿生AUV（干扰更小）；潜水员样带中的鱼类反应大于AUV样带，但该探索性对比样本量小且存在若干混淆因素。

Conclusion: 作为首个已知的对比数据集与基准，本研究初步量化了仿生机器人用于野外生态监测时对动物行为的影响，为后续更大规模、可解析微弱效应的研究及面向降扰的机器人设计改进提供了依据；受操作与实验条件限制，结论仍属初步。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are a promising alternative to divers for scalable collection of natural ocean ecology data. However, these robots may disturb local fauna and cause drastic behavioral differences compared to other monitoring techniques, decreasing their value as scientific tools. A promising prospect is to make AUVs that are more biomimetic, with the hope that taking on the form and behavior of a non-predatory animal may reduce adverse responses. We present the first dataset comparing fish disturbance in response to a conventional thruster-driven AUV, a sea turtle inspired flipper-driven AUV, and a diver. Experiments were conducted at a biodiversity hotspot in a Caribbean reef, and images of the scene were analyzed using computer vision to localize and study fish behavior change. Both AUVs cause measurable changes in fish behavior. Although no between-robot differences remain significant after correction for multiple comparisons, point estimates generally favor the biomimetic AUV, motivating larger studies capable of resolving modest effects and further design changes to optimize for disturbance. We also observe larger responses during diver transects than during AUV transects, although this exploratory comparison is based on a small diver sample with several confounds. While conclusions must be taken as preliminary due to operational and experimental limitations, this study provides the community with a first known dataset and benchmarks to quantify the behavioral impacts of biomimetic robots for ecological monitoring in the wild.

</details>


### [242] [VT-Bridge: Bridging Pretrained Foundation VLAs to VTLAs via Lightweight Residual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22606)
*Yansong Wu,Tuo Yang,Rongping Zhao,Lingyun Chen,Xiao Chen,Junnan Li,Fan Wu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-Bridge用轻量残差适配器将预训练VLA迁移为VTLA，仅需≤50条视触觉演示、0.98M参数，跨三种VLA主干在四类富接触任务上平均成功率从11.7%提升至62.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VTLA在富接触操作中优于VLA，但训练需大量视触觉数据与算力。作者希望避免从零训练或改动预训练VLA架构，降低数据与训练门槛。

Method: VT-Bridge采用统一的轻量残差适配器结构，跨不同VLA主干（π0、π0.5、SmolVLA）复用，配合主干特定权重，在机器人执行频率上对动作进行残差精修；只需微调VLA主干并训练0.98M参数适配器，每任务最多50条视触觉演示。

Result: 在四个富接触操作任务、三种VLA主干上验证一致性有效；平均任务完成率由仅做任务级VLA微调的11.7%提升到62.9%。

Conclusion: VT-Bridge展示了在富接触操作中对多种VLA架构的广适用性、有效性与低门槛特性，为VTLA的高效构建提供了可行的残差适配路径。

Abstract: Vision-Tactile-Language-Action (VTLA) models have demonstrated clear advantages over Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in contact-rich manipulation. However, developing VTLA models is severely constrained by the massive amounts of vision-tactile data and computational resources required. To address this bottleneck, we propose VT-Bridge, a lightweight residual adaptation strategy that bridges pretrained foundation VLAs to VTLAs. Rather than training a VTLA model from scratch or modifying the original architecture of a pretrained VLA, VT-Bridge employs an identical lightweight residual-adapter architecture across VLA backbones and uses backbone-specific weights to refine actions at the robot execution frequency. This design substantially lowers the data and training barriers. Specifically, it requires up to 50 vision-tactile demonstrations per task to fine-tune a VLA backbone and train a 0.98M-parameter residual adapter. Experiments with three representative VLA backbones ($\pi_0$, $\pi_{0.5}$, and SmolVLA) across four contact-rich manipulation tasks further demonstrate its consistent effectiveness across VLA architectures. On average, VT-Bridge raises the task completion rate from 11.7% with task-level VLA fine-tuning alone to 62.9%. Together, these findings demonstrate the broad applicability, effectiveness, and accessibility of VT-Bridge for contact-rich manipulation. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [243] [Becoming a Fruit Ninja: Real-Time Probabilistic Kinodynamic Planning for Manipulator Projectile Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22608)
*Lucas Chen,Austin Garrett,Andrew Niu,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRUITNINJA：GPU 批量增长的 anytime 采样规划器，在拦截流形上生长精确三次边并在动态约束下并行搜索到达时间，实现毫秒级抛接拦截与切片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抛接拦截是困难的动态操作问题：机械臂需在毫秒内到达下落物体轨迹上的某一点，且切割任务还固定了接触时刻的刀片速度与朝向，要求规划在严格执行器限制下求解机器人状态与到达时间的子集。

Method: 提出 anytime 采样规划器，在 GPU 上分批向拦截流形生长搜索树；每条边为精确三次曲线，其旅行时间通过并行搜索对照机械臂动力学确定，从而保证每条边满足执行器约束；规划按物体位置不确定性与到达时间的风险感知目标排序。

Result: 在标定实时仿真中、使用 Franka Research 3 对比六种基线：开放场景切割成功率 96.7%（最佳基线 68.3%），五障碍场景 68.3%（最佳基线 35.0%）。

Conclusion: GPU 并行批量采样结合动力学精确可行边与风险感知排序，可显著提升高动态拦截任务的实时成功率，但验证仍限于标定仿真。

Abstract: Projectile interception is a challenging dynamic manipulation problem. Intercepting a thrown object with a robot arm requires reaching a point on the object's path as the object passes through it. Slicing also fixes the blade's velocity and orientation at contact. The goal is therefore a subset of the states of the robot and arrival times that moves as the object falls, and the arm must reach it within its actuator limits in milliseconds. We present FRUITNINJA, an anytime sampling-based planner that grows a tree on the GPU in batches toward the interception manifold. Each edge is an exact cubic whose travel time is found by a parallel search against the arm's dynamics, so every edge satisfies the actuator limits. Plans are ranked by a risk-aware objective over the uncertainty in the object's position and the arm's arrival time. We evaluate on a Franka Research 3 against six baselines in a calibrated real-time simulator, where FRUITNINJA cuts 96.7% of tosses in the open and 68.3% among five obstacles, versus the best baseline's 68.3% and 35.0% respectively.

</details>


### [244] [From Documented Strengths to Force Limits: Material-Informed Robotic Insertion for Construction Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22609)
*Lin He,Yanyi Chen,Haofei Sun,Lingyao Li,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE提出一种将文档中的材料证据转为机器人插入作业轴向承载能力估计的系统：由LLM从检索到的文本与表格中抽取抗拉/抗压强度并标注来源，再用响应模型插值离线有限元解，把强度与装配条件映射为轴向载荷能力，用于设定力上限或判定过盈配合是否可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配插入中，材料属性与装配条件多变，接触力过大易损伤构件，过小则无法完成插入；工程文档虽含材料与工况知识，但难以直接转化为特定装配的载荷限值。研究动机是把"文档解读"与"力学计算"解耦，实现可追溯到来源、可用的承载力估计。

Method: SAGE（Source-grounded Assembly Gating and Execution）将LLM限定为从检索到的段落与表格中抽取抗拉/抗压强度并记录出处；随后由响应模型对离线有限元解进行插值，将强度与装配条件转换为轴向载荷承载能力。对正间隙配合，估计值直接作为策略的轴向力上限；对过盈配合，则与实测支撑需求比较以决定是否准入。

Result: 主基准上，SAGE将平均承载力误差由直接LLM估计（同一证据）的80.65%降至10.74%；不做重新拟合时，在16个额外几何体上平均误差仍为8.00%。在给定支撑释放模型下，62次计分仿真中正确分类59次，且错误均为保守方向。在xArm6实机演示中，以材料文档为输入，13次试验中9次完成物理插入。

Conclusion: 将文档解释交由LLM、将受力计算交由显式力学模型，可获得准确的承载力估计与可追溯的插入决策，验证了"来源约束+物理模型"混合架构在机器人装配中的有效性。

Abstract: Insertion is a fundamental operation in robotic construction assembly, where variations in material properties and assembly conditions make it difficult to select contact forces that complete the task without exceeding the assembly's capacity. Although construction documents encode engineering knowledge about materials and their conditions, translating this knowledge into load limits for a specific assembly remains difficult. This paper presents SAGE (Source-grounded Assembly Gating and Execution), a system that converts documented material evidence into capacity estimates for robotic insertion. SAGE restricts a large language model (LLM) to extracting tensile and compressive strengths from retrieved passages and tables and records their sources. A response model then interpolates offline finite element (FE) solutions to convert these strengths and the assembly conditions into axial load capacity. For fits with positive clearance, the estimated capacity sets the policy's axial force limit; for interference fits, it is compared with measured support demand to determine admission. On the primary benchmark, SAGE reduces mean capacity error from 80.65\% for direct LLM estimates based on the same evidence to 10.74\%. Without refitting, the mean error remains 8.00\% on 16 additional geometries. Under the assigned support release model, SAGE correctly classifies 59 of 62 scored simulation runs, with only conservative errors. In recorded xArm6 demonstrations, SAGE takes material documents as input and completes physical insertion in 9 of 13 trials. These results show that assigning document interpretation to the LLM and force calculation to an explicit mechanical model produces accurate capacity estimates and traceable insertion decisions.

</details>


### [245] [HIGenNTO: Scalable Humanoid Interaction Generation via Noise-Space Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22611)
*Lalit Jayanti,Kashu Yamazaki,Yuto Shibata,Kotaro Amaya,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HIGenNTO通过优化预训练文本条件运动模型的初始噪声，在稀疏时空与场景约束下合成人形机器人-场景交互运动参考，并成功在Unitree G1上部署执行四类接触丰富任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的人形交互运动参考难以获取：动捕在遮挡与近距离接触下退化，重定向又会引入接触与几何不一致。作者希望绕开这些瓶颈，直接从高层任务描述生成物理可执行的交互运动。

Method: 不训练新模型，而是对预训练文本条件运动模型的初始噪声做优化（test-time noise optimization），在稀疏时空约束与场景约束下同时满足期望接触、避免碰撞、维持稳定支撑，保留运动先验的真实性与时序连贯性；支持从零生成交互运动并以分阶段方式组合长时程行为；任务规范可由编码智能体撰写并编译为prompt、约束与场景程序。

Result: 在机器人-环境与机器人-物体任务上生成的参考运动可被跟踪策略在仿真中执行，并用于训练仅依赖机载感知的深度条件视觉运动策略；在Unitree G1真机上完成四类接触丰富任务部署；编码智能体自动撰写了8个评测任务中的3个及另外4种行为。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从高层任务描述到物理可执行人形交互的可扩展路径，说明以约束引导的运动先验噪声优化可替代动捕/重定向作为接触丰富交互的参考来源，并可与自动任务生成、真机策略训练形成闭环。

Abstract: Humanoid robots can acquire complex skills by imitating kinematic humanoid motion references, yet reliable references for contact-rich interactions remain difficult to obtain: motion capture deteriorates under occlusion and close physical contact, while retargeting introduces additional contact and geometric inconsistencies. We present HIGenNTO, a framework that synthesizes humanoid-scene interaction motion references by optimizing the initial noise of a pretrained text-conditioned motion model under sparse spatiotemporal and scene constraints. The same formulation satisfies desired contacts, avoids collisions, and maintains stable support while retaining the prior's realism and temporal coherence, generating interaction motions from scratch and composing long-horizon behaviors stage-wise. Across robot-environment and robot-object tasks, HIGenNTO produces motions that can be executed by tracking policies in simulation and used to train depth-conditioned visuomotor policies operating solely from onboard sensing. We deploy these policies on a Unitree G1 across four contact-rich tasks. Finally, the task specifications themselves can be written by a coding agent, which proposes interaction tasks and compiles them into prompt, constraint, and scene programs, authoring three of our eight evaluated tasks and four further behaviors. Together, these results establish a scalable path from high-level task descriptions to physically executable humanoid interactions.

</details>


### [246] [Simultaneous Forward and Inverse Human-in-the-Loop Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22630)
*Kyeongwon Park,Steven H. Collins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SFIHILO，将前向模型与逆奖励学习同步进行，用主动查询高效推断个体奖励并直接优化控制策略，无需额外用户试验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人在环辅助场景中，执行控制策略、测量生/生理指标与采集用户反馈的实验成本高，限制了查询次数与优化迭代数；同时用户真正在意的结果及其个体与情境差异往往未知，需要样本效率极高的个性化奖励推断方法。

Method: SFIHILO引导一个前向模型，从控制策略预测用户结果；用该模型做主动查询以加速逆奖励学习；随后在不增加用户试验的前提下优化最终控制策略。查询策略以同时降低前向与逆信念的不确定性为目标，并指向一个区域性偏好边界，使这一同步学习过程获得稳健信息。

Result: 仿真实验表明，SFIHILO在用户异质性、结果维度、精度要求、噪声水平与非平稳性等条件下均有效；相比互信息类方法，所提主动查询策略显著提升逆学习的样本效率，同时保持前向模型精度。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了推断潜在人类目标的能力，可支撑更具迁移性与有效性的自然人机交互，为低成本个性化辅助控制提供可行路径。

Abstract: Subjective user experience is important to human-robot interaction, but the outcomes users value, and how those preferences vary across individuals and contexts, are often unknown. While inverse learning approaches using human data can help identify user rewards, in many assistive settings the experimental costs of executing a control policy, measuring biomechanical or physiological outcomes, and collecting user feedback often limit the number of queries and optimization iterations. Here, we present Simultaneous Forward and Inverse Human-In-the-Loop Optimization (SFIHILO), which efficiently infers individual-specific reward functions from preferences over human outcomes and identifies a final control policy that maximizes the learned reward. SFIHILO bootstraps a forward model to predict user outcomes from control policies, uses this model for active querying to accelerate inverse reward learning, and then optimizes the final control policy without additional user trials. We score candidate policies by their expected reduction in uncertainty across both forward and inverse beliefs, targeting a regional preference boundary to robustly inform this simultaneous learning process. In simulation, we show that SFIHILO was effective across user heterogeneity, outcome dimensionalities, precision requirements, noise levels, and nonstationarity; compared with mutual information approaches, the proposed active querying strategy significantly improved sample efficiency in inverse learning while preserving forward model accuracy. This approach demonstrates the potential to infer latent human goals, enabling more transferable and effective human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [247] [Density-Driven Area Coverage for Nonholonomic Multi-Robot Systems with Safety Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22668)
*Julian Martinez,Kooktae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将密度驱动最优控制(D2OC)与基于反馈线性化前视点的控制障碍函数(CBF)安全滤波器结合，直接在机器人物理输入上施加安全约束，并推导了考虑前视几何、机器人足迹与控制周期内运动的动态安全裕度，在仿真与Robotarium多机器人实验中验证了安全性与覆盖性能的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D2OC可用于将多机器人团队按非均匀空间密度分布部署，但直接应用于非完整约束机器人时，安全约束施加在参考运动上，而实际运动由物理输入决定，导致安全认证与物理实际间隙之间出现不匹配（参考轨迹安全但实际物理间隙可能被违反）。

Method: 将D2OC与CBF安全滤波器通过反馈线性化前视点(look-ahead point)耦合，使安全约束与执行器饱和约束在同一控制层中共同处理；推导了显式考虑前视点几何、机器人足迹尺寸以及单个控制周期内运动量的安全裕度(safety margin)。

Result: 仿真表明该方法在保持所需物理间距的同时，覆盖性能与传统参考跟踪方法相当，而后者虽满足参考运动上的安全却会违反实际物理间距；在Robotarium上多台非完整机器人实验中实现了安全执行并驱动团队趋向目标空间分布。

Conclusion: 直接在物理输入层面施加安全约束可消除密度驱动多机器人覆盖中安全认证与物理运动之间的不匹配，为多机器人覆盖控制提供了安全性与任务性能兼顾的实用框架。

Abstract: Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) provides a principled approach to distributing multi-robot teams over non-uniform spatial distributions. Applying D2OC to nonholonomic robots, however, creates a gap between safety constraints imposed on a reference motion and the physical inputs that determine the actual robot motion. We address this issue by enforcing the safety constraint directly on the robot's physical inputs while preserving the density-driven coverage objective. The proposed framework combines D2OC with a control barrier function safety filter through a feedback-linearizing look-ahead point, allowing safety and actuator limits to be considered together during control. We further derive a safety margin that accounts for the look-ahead geometry, robot footprint, and motion during each control interval. Simulation results show that the proposed method maintains the required physical separation while achieving coverage performance comparable to a conventional reference-tracking approach, which can satisfy safety on the reference motion yet violate the corresponding physical clearance. Experiments on multiple nonholonomic robots in the Robotarium further demonstrate safe execution while driving the robots toward the desired spatial distribution. These results show that enforcing safety directly on the physical inputs can eliminate the mismatch between safety certification and physical robot motion in density-driven multi-robot coverage.

</details>


### [248] [AquaWorld: Structure-Consistent Underwater World Generation for Robot Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22670)
*Bin Peng,Tiandong Zhang,Ruidong Wang,Min Luo,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaWorld：以共享地形结构驱动的语言条件水下世界生成框架，联合生成地形、资产布局、任务与流场，提供1万余资产与CFD监督的残差平均流场预测，支持水下航行器与仿生机器鱼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人仿真需要地形、场景组成、任务与流场相互一致的环境；独立随机化会破坏这些耦合关系，导致仿真环境不真实、策略迁移性差。现有生成方法缺乏共享结构参考，难以同时保证几何、任务与流体动力学的一致性。

Method: 提出AquaWorld世界生成框架：以语言条件规划生成3D地形及共享结构参考，进而以随机变化方式引导资产放置、任务定义与入流参数设定；构建超10,000个水下兼容资产库；用CFD监督的残差模型预测可复用的、以地形为条件的平均流场；兼容常规水下航行器与仿生机器鱼。并在24组配对地形上对比结构一致随机化与独立随机化。

Result: 在24对配对地形上，结构一致随机化的跨因素一致性显著优于独立随机化；等预算策略训练对比中，结构一致随机化的验证成功率比独立随机化高21%；独立物理实验中，仿真训练的视觉导航策略在真实水箱试验中成功率95%，且未更新感知或控制模块。

Conclusion: AquaWorld 提供了一条生成多样化水下环境的实用路径，在保留流场仿真与机器人学习所需结构关系的同时实现随机化，并以真机零微调迁移验证了其仿真-现实有效性，对水下具身智能与仿真环境生成有直接价值。

Abstract: Underwater robot simulation requires diverse environments in which terrain, scene composition, tasks, and currents remain mutually consistent. We present AquaWorld, a world-generation framework that preserves these relationships through shared terrain structure. A language-conditioned plan generates 3D terrain and shared structural references that guide asset placement, task definition, and inflow specification under stochastic variation. The framework incorporates over 10,000 underwater-compatible assets, predicts reusable terrain-conditioned mean-flow fields through a CFD-supervised residual model, and supports conventional underwater vehicles and bio-inspired robotic fish. On 24 paired terrains, structure-consistent randomization produces substantially better cross-factor consistency than independent randomization. In a matched-budget policy-training comparison, structurally coherent randomization achieves a validation success rate 21% higher than independent randomization. In separate physical experiments, a simulation-trained visual navigation policy succeeds in 95% of physical tank trials without updating its perception or control modules. Overall, AquaWorld provides a practical way to generate varied underwater environments while retaining the structural relationships needed for flow simulation and robot learning.

</details>


### [249] [SHAFT: A Slack-Compensating, Helical-Buckling-Attenuating Flexible-Shaft Transmission for Lightweight Multi-DoF Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22677)
*Tomoya Takahashi,Moses Gladson Selvamuthu,Richiro Tadakuma,Kazutoshi Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SHAFT柔性轴传动机构，通过近端张紧器补偿松弛、抑制螺旋屈曲，显著提升远程驱动机械臂的传动效率与角度精度，并构建轻量6自由度机械臂样机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量化机械臂需在人类生活环境安全作业，远程驱动（线驱动/Bowden索）可将电机移至基座以降低惯量与尺寸，但现有方案或增加质量（滑轮换向）或摩擦损耗大；柔性轴可兼顾质量与摩擦，却在关节弯折松弛时因螺旋屈曲产生角度传动误差。

Method: 提出SHAFT（Slack-compensating, Helical-buckling-Attenuating Flexible-shaft Transmission）机构：在传动近端设置张紧器补偿关节弯折产生的松弛，抑制螺旋屈曲，从而在不增加臂段运动质量的前提下降低角度传动误差并提升效率。基于该机构搭建含电机与张紧器旋转模块的6-DoF臂+1-DoF夹爪，臂模块质量280 g。

Result: 在包含四个90°弯折的传动路径中，相较无张紧器的柔性轴传动，效率提升约30%，角度传动误差降低约65%；并完成6-DoF臂加1-DoF夹爪的样机制作，臂模块重280 g。

Conclusion: SHAFT为一种新型远程驱动系统，可实现轻量化与高效率兼顾，有助于加速机器人在人类环境中的安全部署。

Abstract: Lightweight and slim manipulators enable safe operation in human living environments. Proximal actuation using remote transmission mechanisms, such as wire-driven or Bowden cables, effectively reduces inertia and arm size by relocating motors near the base and transmitting torque to distal joints. Existing approaches either increase mass through additional components, such as pulleys for direction changes, or suffer from reduced transmission efficiency due to friction losses. Flexible shaft transmission avoids both mass increase and excessive friction losses, but faces increasing angular transmission error due to helical buckling caused by slack generated at joint bending. To address this problem, we propose SHAFT: a Slack-compensating, Helical-buckling-Attenuating Flexible- shaft Transmission mechanism. This mechanism compensates for slack through a proximal tensioner, improving the angular transmission error and efficiency of flexible shaft transmission without increasing the moving mass of the arm section. In a transmission path containing four 90-degree bends, the proposed mechanism demonstrated approximately 30% higher efficiency and approximately 65% lower angular transmission error compared to a flexible shaft transmission without a tensioner. Using this mechanism, we fabricated a 6-Degree-of-Freedom (DoF) arm with a 1-DoF gripper manipulator consisting of a rotary module housing motors with a tensioner, and a 280 g weight for the arm module. The proposed manipulator represents a novel remote actuation system for achieving lightweight construction with high efficiency, contributing to the acceleration of safe robot deployment in human environments.

</details>


### [250] [A Direct Rigid Transmission 2-DoF Wrist Extension for Tendon-Driven Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22681)
*Yujie Pang,Sadman Sakib,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CRAFT-Wrist：一种安装在机械臂与 CRAFT 灵巧手之间的同心 2 自由度腕部扩展模块，在不改动手部的前提下提供局部手部姿态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作在受限空间中需要手部朝向的局部控制；无腕部时，这一朝向只能依赖机械臂多关节协调运动，导致末端轨迹复杂、可控性差。

Method: 同心式 2-DoF 腕部结构，夹装在机械臂与 CRAFT Hand 之间；用两个 XC430-T240BB-T 舵机经短刚性传动分别驱动左右（radial–ulnar）与前后的旋转；并测量不同腕姿态下的手指-电机负载。

Result: 原型在带载条件下实现双轴驱动，实测工作空间为径向-尺侧偏转 ±20°、前后方向屈曲 +80°、伸展 −18°；完成抓取、钉锤敲击与黑板擦拭演示。

Conclusion: 通过低成本同心 2-DoF 腕部为灵巧手补充局部朝向自由度，简化了受限空间内的末端轨迹，并公开演示与装配说明。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation in confined spaces requires local control of hand orientation. Without a wrist, a dexterous hand must obtain this local orientation through coordinated motion of the robot arm, often involving several joints and a more complex end-effector path. We present CRAFT-Wrist, a concentric 2-DoF wrist extension that mounts between a robot arm and the CRAFT Hand without modifying the hand. Two XC430-T240BB-T servos drive sideways and front-back rotation through short rigid transmissions. Our initial hardware prototype actuates both axes under load, achieving a demonstrated workspace of $\pm20^{\circ}$ in radial--ulnar deviation (left--right) and a front-back range of $+80^{\circ}$ in flexion and $-18^{\circ}$ in extension. We characterize the resulting finger-motor loading across several wrist postures and demonstrate how the wrist supplies local orientation in grasping, nail hammering, and blackboard wiping. Demonstrations and assembly instructions are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [251] [StateMem: Single-State Residual Memory with Adaptive Inference for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22684)
*Wenzhuo Li,Qiongfeng Shi,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StateMem 提出一种单状态残差记忆框架，用预测误差驱动低秩残差更新持久记忆 token，并自适应路由缓存前缀，在 LIBERO、RoboMemArena 与真实机器人任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略主要依赖当前观测，历史信息保留不足；MemoryVLA 等记忆增强方法依赖外部记忆库的显式存取，带来开销且不够紧凑。作者希望以更轻量、无需训练的方式为 VLA 引入有效的记忆机制。

Method: 提出单状态残差记忆框架 StateMem：用预测误差驱动持久记忆 token 的低秩残差更新；对缓存前缀进行自适应路由，训练-free 控制器在线调整路由阈值；cache 复用时用快速修正补偿过时的前缀特征。

Result: LIBERO 平均成功率 97.6%，相对全刷新降低平均 VLM 前缀刷新率 20.25%；RoboMemArena Occlusion 类别中取得单 VLA 方法最佳，TSR 21.8%、CSR 44.3%；六个真实操作任务平均成功率 +21%。

Conclusion: StateMem 以单状态残差记忆替代外部记忆库，在保持较高成功率的同时显著降低前缀刷新开销，并在遮挡记忆场景与真实任务上取得增益，说明紧凑的记忆表示对长时程操作有效。

Abstract: Memory-dependent robotic manipulation often requires later actions to use information from earlier interactions. Existing vision-language-action (VLA) policies primarily rely on current observations, limiting historical information retention. Memory-augmented VLAs, such as MemoryVLA, address this limitation with external memory banks but require explicit storage and retrieval. To address these limitations, we propose StateMem, a single-state residual memory framework for VLA policies that uses prediction error to update a persistent memory token through low-rank residuals and to adaptively route cached prefixes. A training-free controller adjusts the routing threshold online, while fast correction compensates for stale prefix features during cache reuse. We evaluate StateMem on LIBERO, RoboMemArena, and real-world manipulation tasks. On LIBERO, StateMem achieves an average success rate of 97.6% and reduces the average VLM prefix refresh rate by 20.25% relative to full refresh. In the Occlusion category of RoboMemArena, StateMem achieves the best performance among single-VLA methods, reaching 21.8% Task Success Rate (TSR) and 44.3% Cumulative Success Rate (CSR). Across six real-world manipulation tasks, it achieves +21% in average success rate.

</details>


### [252] [Decentralized Multi-Robot Exploration with Probabilistic Peer Intent and Multi-hop Plan Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22726)
*Saurbh Singh Jamwal,Nived Chebrolu,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出概率性同伴意图（PPI），将同伴轨迹转化为连续的空间意图表示并融入本地MCTS动作评估，同时研究多跳传播同伴意图对去中心化多机器人探索的影响，并在三台真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在通信受限的大规模环境中，集中式探索不实用，而现有基于MCTS的去中心化方法（如DMCE）仅通过与直接通信范围内的机器人交换路点序列来获取同伴意图，信息利用受限、表达离散。

Method: 以DMCE为基础，将同伴规划轨迹转换为连续的空间概率性意图表示（PPI），嵌入本地MCTS的动作评估；并额外研究通过多跳中继将计划传播到直接通信范围之外的效果。

Result: 在多种仿真环境和不同团队规模下的实验表明，PPI与多跳传播均能改善去中心化探索，二者相对收益取决于环境结构与团队规模；并在三种不同环境类型中的三台真实机器人上完成实机部署。

Conclusion: 概率化、连续化的同伴意图建模及跨跳传播是提升通信受限下去中心化多机器人探索协作效率的有效途径，且已具备实机可行性。

Abstract: Efficient coordination under limited communication remains a key challenge in decentralized multi-robot exploration. While centralized approaches benefit from global information sharing, they are often impractical in large-scale or communication-constrained environments. Existing Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-based approaches, such as Decentralized Monte Carlo Exploration (DMCE), enable decentralized planning by taking peer intent into account. This peer intent is obtained by communicating sequences of planned waypoints with robots within direct communication range. In this work, we extend this idea by introducing Probabilistic Peer Intent (PPI), which converts peer trajectories into a continuous spatial representation of predicted intent and incorporates it into local MCTS action evaluation. We additionally study the effects of sharing peer intent beyond direct communication range by propagating plans over multiple hops. Experiments across multiple simulated environments and team sizes show that PPI and Multi-hop propagation can each improve decentralized exploration, with their relative benefits depending on environment structure and team size. We also demonstrate the real-world deployment of our method on three robots operating in different environment types.

</details>


### [253] [BEACON: Belief-Enabled Adaptive CONtrol for Imitation Learning under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22730)
*Moonyoung Lee,Soumojit Bhattacharya,George Kantor,Oliver Kroemer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用贝叶斯信念表征隐藏状态（当前最优估计+剩余不确定性）来条件化扩散策略，替代原始观测历史，从而在部分可观测的机器人操作任务中缓解状态别名问题，实现无需显式模式切换或奖励塑形的探索-利用自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务常含不可直接观测的隐藏状态，需通过序列物理交互推断。在部分可观测设定下，将模仿学习策略直接条件化于近期原始观测历史会导致状态别名（相同观测对应不同隐藏状态），同一输入收到冲突的动作标签，性能下降。目标是赋予策略基于历史的消歧能力。

Method: 将扩散策略条件化于隐藏状态的结构化贝叶斯信念表征，该表征同时给出当前最可能的单点状态估计与残余不确定性。用紧凑的结构化输入替代原始历史，使策略能依据信念不确定性在探索与利用行为间隐式调制，无需显式模式切换或奖励塑形。在两个具有定性不同信念表征的域上验证：连续信念（触觉传感下的玉米秆夹爪对齐）与离散类别分布（插销门开启）。

Result: 在两个域中，信念条件化策略显著优于仅观测基线，并接近特权真值（ground-truth）性能；消融实验表明策略在推理时会依据信念不确定性调整探索行为。

Conclusion: 以贝叶斯信念作为扩散策略的条件输入，可有效解决部分可观测操作中的状态别名问题，兼顾连续与离散信念表示，并自发产生不确定性驱动的探索-利用权衡，为历史感知的模仿学习提供了结构化、紧凑的替代方案。

Abstract: Robot manipulation tasks often involve hidden state information that cannot be directly observed and must be inferred through sequential physical interactions. In such partially observable settings, conditioning an imitation learning policy directly on the recent raw observation history leads to poor performance. This is due to state aliasing, wherein identical observations may arise from different hidden states, and the policy receives conflicting action labels for the same input. To enable history-aware disambiguation capability, we propose conditioning a diffusion policy on a structured representation of the hidden states using Bayesian belief that exposes both the current most likely state estimate and the remaining uncertainty. This representation replaces raw history with a structured, compact input, enabling the policy to implicitly modulate between exploratory and exploitative behaviors based on belief uncertainty, without explicit mode switching or reward shaping. We evaluate across two domains with qualitatively different belief representations: a continuous belief for cornstalk gripper alignment via tactile sensing, and a discrete categorical distribution for latched door opening. In both domains, the belief-conditioned policy substantially outperforms the observation-only baseline and approaches privileged ground-truth performance, with ablations illustrating that the policy adapts its exploration behavior depending on the belief uncertainty at inference time.

</details>


### [254] [Rapidly-Iterating Grid-Based Near-Optimal Kinodynamic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22733)
*Michael Moncton,Eric Frew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RDG算法，利用状态空间网格分解实现常数时间复杂度的节点选择、动力学传播与图修正，使kinoDynamic运动规划可通过快速迭代获得渐近近优解，在10 DOF环境中成功率与解质量优于SST和DIRT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kinoDynamic运动规划需在满足动力学约束下求高质量轨迹，已有具最优性保证的稀疏算法（SST、DIRT）迭代效率低、解质量与成功率在高维复杂环境中不足。

Method: 基于状态空间网格分解（grid decomposition）的快速迭代kinoDynamic网格（RDG）算法：在网格上完成节点选择、动力学传播与图修正，使各子模块复杂度相对树节点数为常数；用覆盖球序列归纳证明渐近近最优性与概率完备性；并将各子系统与常见最近邻搜索方法在探索偏置生成上对比。

Result: 在最高10 DOF的复杂kinoDynamic规划仿真环境中，RDG的平均最终解质量分别超过SST和DIRT最多104%与40%，并在SST与DIRT均失败的高维用例中保持100%成功率。

Conclusion: 网格化状态空间分解可显著加速kinoDynamic规划的迭代过程，在保留渐近近最优与概率完备保证的同时提升解质量与成功率，为高维动力学约束规划提供高效方案。

Abstract: This paper develops the Rapidly-iterating kinoDynamic Grid (RDG) algorithm, an asymptotically near-optimal kinodynamic motion planning algorithm that produces high quality solutions through rapid iteration. The algorithm leverages a state space grid decomposition to perform node selection, dynamics propagation, and graph revision in constant time complexity with respect to the number of nodes in the trajectory tree. Through a covering ball sequence induction proof, the algorithm is shown to be asymptotically near-optimal and probabilistically complete. Different subsystems of the algorithm are evaluated against common nearest-neighbor search-based methods at generating exploration bias. The RDG algorithm is evaluated through simulated trials in complex, kinodynamic motion planning problem environments up to 10 DOF relative to similar sparse, kinodynamic planning algorithms with optimality guarantees, SST and DIRT. The RDG algorithm outperforms both SST and DIRT in mean final solution quality by up to 104% and 40% respectively. Additionally, RDG maintained a 100% success rate, even on a 10-DOF test case where both SST and DIRT did not.

</details>


### [255] [Distributed Infrastructure Sensors and Cues for Robotic Fire-Fighting and Safety System on a Lunar Base](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22786)
*Alexis Munguia,Eryc Rodriguez,Drake Russ,Jekan Thangavelautham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种"环境嵌入智能"的月球栖息地应急消防原型：在舱内每几平方米贴一张薄陶瓷QR码，码内编码局部楼层地图、传感器安全阈值与分步处置流程；低功耗漫游车数秒内解码后查询邻近温-气传感簇并立即执行本地响应，无需全局地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期载人月球栖息地若发生火灾或有毒泄漏，需在数分钟内处置压力容器，而穿舱外服的航天员与存在通信延迟的地面支持都无法保证该响应速度，因此需要把快速响应能力下沉到栖息地本体，实现无地面介入的自治应急。

Method: 采用被动式信息嵌入方案：以薄陶瓷QR码作为耐候信息载体，按约每2平方米一张的密度分布式部署；每张码存储局部楼层地图、传感器安全上下限和分步危险处置程序。低功耗漫游车（agent）在数秒内解码单张码，读取邻近温度-气体传感器簇数据，基于阈值与流程做出即时动作，全程无需构建或查询全局地图。

Result: 在代表性栖息地布局（约每2平方米一个码）的仿真中，该快速响应消防方案简化了任务执行，并避免了高资源消耗与不确定结果。由于知识分散在无源铭牌上，损伤被局部化，程序更新只需更换单张码，且对处理器算力需求极低。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一种去中心化、低算力、可局部维护的月球栖息地应急安全技术原型，用被动陶瓷码+漫游车替代延迟的地面支持与全局建图。当前主要为仿真验证的原型，缺乏真实硬件演示与实测数据，且文中存在重复段落，尚需实际环境验证其鲁棒性与响应时效。

Abstract: There are renewed efforts to build a lunar base and house a team of astronauts on the Moon for extended periods. However, important challenges remain, such as a rapid-response fire-hazard management system. In the event of a fire or toxic leak inside an early crewed lunar habitat, a single pressurized cylinder must be handled within minutes. However, suited astronauts and radio-delayed support from Earth cannot guarantee such response times. We describe a prototype fire-fighting and emergency response safety technology that embeds intelligence in the habitat itself: using thin ceramic QR codes affixed every few meters, each carries a local floor map, safe sensor limits, and step-by-step hazard responses. A low-power rover decodes a plate in a matter of seconds, polls the adjoining temperature-gas sensor cluster, and acts immediately, eliminating the need for a global map . In a representative layout, the interior is divided into roughly one code per two square meters. Simulations show that rapid-response firefighting both simplifies the task and avoids costly resource use and indeterminate outcomes. Because knowledge is spread across passive plates, damage is localized, procedures can be updated by replacing a single code, and processor demands remain minimal. However, important challenges remain, such as a rapid-response fire-hazard management system. In the event of a fire or toxic leak inside an early crewed lunar habitat, a single pressurized cylinder must be handled within minutes. However, suited astronauts and radio-delayed support from Earth cannot guarantee such response times. We describe a prototype fire-fighting and emergency response safety technology that embeds intelligence in the habitat itself: using thin ceramic QR codes affixed every few meters, each carries a local floor map, safe sensor limits, and step-by-step hazard responses.

</details>


### [256] [Task-Oriented Co-Design and Optimization of Geared Actuators for Robotic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22795)
*Xuanyu Huang,Jianqiang Dong,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向任务的多足机器人执行器软硬件协同设计框架，采用代理模型加速电机评估、分层混合变量优化，联合优化额定输出扭矩密度与任务性能，并在二自由度跳跃腿上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多足机器人执行器多按部件级指标（扭矩、功率密度）优化，缺乏任务导向；电机、减速器、传感器的系统级协同设计因机械-电气-电磁耦合与高计算成本而难以实现，单部件改进未必带来特定任务性能提升。

Method: 1) 用代理模型加速电机评估，支持耦合设计空间的全局探索；2) 分层混合变量优化：离散枚举与连续维度/实值指标搜索结合，每次评估前对连续指标取整以选择剩余离散取值；3) 联合优化额定输出扭矩密度与任务性能，为每个硬件候选确定Bezier参数化关节扭矩曲线；4) 通过执行器制造与二自由度跳跃腿实验验证。

Result: 样机实测质量下额定输出扭矩密度达35.7 N·m/kg，较广泛使用的商用齿轮关节执行器高约60%，且轻18.6%；同等台架条件下，在两倍额定扭矩时跳跃高度提高12.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一条从任务需求到执行器设计与控制的系统化路径，证明任务导向的软硬件协同设计可同时提升扭矩密度与任务级性能。

Abstract: Different tasks performed by legged robots impose distinct torque and speed requirements on actuators. Existing robotic actuators are generally optimized at the component level for metrics such as torque or power density, without explicit task guidance. System-level optimization across components such as motors, gearboxes, and sensors is challenging because of the high computational cost and coupling among mechanical, electrical, and electromagnetic behaviors. Consequently, improvements in individual components may not translate into better robot performance in a specific task. To this end, we present a systematic optimization framework for task-oriented co-design of actuator hardware and control. First, surrogate models are employed to accelerate motor evaluation and support global exploration of the coupled design space. Then, a hierarchical mixed-variable optimization strategy is adopted, combining discrete enumeration with continuous search over dimensions and real-valued indices. These indices are rounded to select admissible values for the remaining discrete choices before each evaluation. Within this search, rated output torque density and task performance are jointly optimized, with Bezier-parameterized joint torque profiles determined for each hardware candidate. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated through actuator fabrication and experiments on a two-degree-of-freedom jumping leg. Based on its measured mass, the fabricated prototype achieves a nominal rated output torque density of 35.7 N m/kg, approximately 60% higher than that of a widely used commercial geared joint actuator, while being 18.6% lighter. Under matched bench conditions, it achieves 12.0% greater jump height at twice-rated torque. Together, these results demonstrate a systematic route from task requirements to actuator design and control.

</details>


### [257] [Expert-Play Contouring Control: Faster-than-Demonstration Planning from Slow Expert and Fast Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22798)
*Seunghoon Cho,Wonsuhk Jung,Sundhar Vinodh Sangeetha,Shreyas Kousik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EPCC将"超越示教速度执行"建模为动力学感知控制问题：用慢速专家示教训练隐式轨迹生成器，将其预测重参数化为与时间无关的"等值线（contour）"表示任务进展；再用快速非专家 play 数据训练世界模型，部署时由规划器在世界模型中优化动作，最大化沿专家等值线的进展并惩罚偏离预期任务演化。三个视觉运动操作任务上吞吐量平均达 IL 基线 2.0×，在交互敏感动力学任务上达最强加速基线 2.2×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家示教只规定"做什么"，不规定"能做多快"；模仿学习继承示教的时间节奏，而直接放大动作速度会因机器人-物体动力学改变而失败。作者希望利用廉价、快速的非专家 play 数据来补充快速执行所需的动力学覆盖，从而在保持任务意图的前提下突破示教速度上限。

Method: EPCC 分三部分：(1) 用慢速专家示教训练隐式轨迹生成器，把预测轨迹重参数化为时间无关的任务进展等值线，剥离时间信息、只保留任务意图；(2) 用快速非专家 play 数据训练世界模型（WM），建模快速动作的动力学后果；(3) 部署时规划器在世界模型上优化动作，目标为最大化沿专家等值线的进展，并惩罚偏离预期任务演化（contour tracking + dynamics-aware penalty）。通过等值线表示把"任务意图"与"执行速度"解耦。

Result: 在三个视觉运动操作任务上平均，EPCC 吞吐量约为 IL 基线的 2.0×；在物体动力学对交互敏感的任务上，吞吐量为最强加速基线的 2.2×。分析显示性能增益集中在"更快执行会改变机器人-物体演化"的场景，验证了动力学感知设计的必要性。

Conclusion: 示教提供任务意图，play 数据提供将该意图更快执行所需的动力学覆盖；这一简洁原则可有效实现超越示教速度的机器人操作执行。属于动力学感知控制/机器人学习方向的方法类工作。

Abstract: Expert demonstrations often specify what a robot should do, but not how fast it can do it. Imitation Learning (IL) inherits demonstration timing, while directly accelerating the learned motion can fail when faster execution changes the robot-object dynamics. We study faster-than-demonstration execution as a dynamics-aware control problem and introduce Expert-Play Contouring Control (EPCC), which combines slow expert demonstrations with fast, non-expert play. Expert demonstrations train a latent trajectory generator whose predictions are reparameterized into a time-independent contour of successful task progression, while play trains a world model (WM) of fast-action outcomes. At deployment, our proposed planner uses the WM to optimize actions that makes maximize progress along the expert-derived contour while penalizing deviation from the intended task evolution. Averaged across three visuomotor manipulation tasks, EPCC achieves a $2.0\times$ the throughput of the IL baseline, including $2.2\times$ that of the throughput of the strongest acceleration baseline on a task with interaction-sensitive object dynamics. Our analysis shows that the gains concentrate where faster execution changes robot-object evolution. Together, our results highlight a simple yet effective principle: demonstrations provide task intent, while play data provides the dynamic coverage needed to execute that intent faster.

</details>


### [258] [ARCGym: Benchmarking Deep Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Robotic Colonoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22803)
*Guanglin Ji,Martina Finocchiaro,Kenny Erleben,Hang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARCGym：面向柔性结肠镜（磁驱动与近端插入式）的开源强化学习环境与基准，含5个CT重建结肠、临床子任务与统一成功指标，并提出深度管腔对齐+管腔可见性奖励以应对遮挡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型自主结肠镜导航仿真主要针对全驱动胶囊机器人，无法反映长而柔性的临床结肠镜在接触丰富条件下的导航特性，缺乏临床来源的可变形解剖与统一评测基准。

Method: 构建开源强化学习环境ARCGym：支持胶囊与柔性内窥镜多类机器人（本文聚焦磁驱动tip actuation与临床常用近端平移驱动）；用5个CT重建结肠覆盖典型临床场景，定义临床意义的导航子任务与统一成功指标；提出结合深度管腔对齐与管腔可见性分数的奖励函数以改善遮挡下的学习。

Result: 跨任务、机器人与解剖结构的实验表明：磁驱动与近端插入式柔性机器人的自主导航均仍具挑战性，近端插入驱动仍是开放问题。

Conclusion: ARCGym提供了可复现的柔性结肠镜自主导航仿真基准，揭示柔性近端插入驱动导航尚未解决，为后续接触丰富、遮挡条件下的学习方法研究提供平台。

Abstract: Simulations for learning-based autonomous colonoscopic navigation focus mainly on fully actuated capsule robots, failing to capture the contact-rich navigation of long and flexible clinical colonoscopes. We present the Autonomous Robotic Colonoscopy Gym (ARCGym), an open-source reinforcement learning environment and benchmark for image-based navigation in clinically derived deformable colon anatomies. ARCGym supports multiple types of colonoscope robots, spanning capsule robots and flexible endoscopes, with this work focusing on flexible endoscopes including magnetic-driven tip actuation and clinically used proximally translational actuation. This work includes five CT-reconstructed colons representing typical clinical scenarios, a set of clinically meaningful navigation subtasks, and unified success metrics. We introduce a reward combining depth-based lumen alignment with a lumen-visibility score to improve learning under occlusions. Experiments across tasks, robots, and anatomies show that autonomous navigation remains challenging for both magnetic-driven and proximal-insertion flexible robots, with proximal-insertion actuation remaining an open problem.

</details>


### [259] [Kinematic Interface for the Wild: Modular Bimanual Loco-Manipulation Capture from 360$^{\circ}$ Cameras Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22809)
*Benjamin Yang,Weiying Wang,Shenggao Li,Keming Yan,Sasha Wilkinson,Zelin Wang,Yip Fun Yeung,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KIWI 是一种仅使用现成相机的可穿戴数据采集套件，通过将 360 度相机的后置镜头用于房间级定位与双手配准、前置镜头用于操作录制，实现无需专用追踪硬件的房间尺度双手操作数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMI 风格的手腕相机既要录制操作又要进行场景定位。现有手持设备多依赖面向工作空间的在线定位（视野易被手和物体遮挡）或额外追踪硬件，导致房间尺度双手采集往往需要为操作者或场景加装设备。

Method: 核心思想是把定位与录制两个任务分给 360 度相机的两个镜头：后置镜头朝向房间，构建共享度量地图，将双手以及可选的头戴相机注册到同一坐标系，不依赖工作空间共视；前置镜头录制操作过程，并用离线 IMU 融合弥补前置镜头跟踪丢失。通过快拆底板，相机模块可安装到筷子夹爪、平行夹爪、手腕支架或机器人法兰上。

Result: 在六段双手录制中，后置+前置镜头组合仅有 0.1% 的查询帧定位失败；仅用前置镜头的双手特征对齐在 24.8% 的帧上失败并完全丢失一段录制。与评估基准标记相比，定位误差保持在 4.5 mm 以内。仅用四路手腕流恢复的位姿即可重建出场景的 3D 高斯泼溅。

Conclusion: KIWI 证明了仅用消费级相机即可实现房间尺度、无标记、双手操作的精确数据采集与定位，其位姿精度足以支撑 3D 高斯泼溅级别的场景重建，可作为具身操作数据采集的低成本通用方案。

Abstract: A wrist-mounted camera for UMI-style data collection must do two jobs: record the manipulation and localize in the scene. Most handheld devices localize online from workspace-facing views crowded by hands and objects, or add dedicated tracking hardware. Room-scale bimanual capture therefore still tends to instrument the operator or the scene for accurate localization. We present KIWI (Kinematic Interface for the Wild), a capture kit whose only electronics are off-the-shelf cameras. Our core system splits the two jobs across the two lenses of a 360-degree camera. The rear lens faces the room and builds a shared metric map that registers both hands, and an optional head camera, in one frame without workspace co-visibility; the front lens records the manipulation, and offline IMU fusion bridges front-lens tracking loss. Through our quick-release plate, the camera module attaches to chopstick grippers, parallel-jaw grippers, hand-wrist mounts, or robot flanges. Across six bimanual recordings, combining the rear and front lenses failed to localize only 0.1% of query frames, whereas front-only bimanual feature alignment failed on 24.8% of frames and lost one recording entirely; against evaluation fiducials, localization error stayed within 4.5 mm. KIWI's recovered poses were sufficiently consistent for the four wrist streams alone to reconstruct the scene as a 3D Gaussian splat.

</details>


### [260] [Commonsense-Grounded Path Planning from Abstract Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22813)
*Masafumi Endo,Kohei Honda,Ryo Yonetani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRS利用LLM/VLM作为常识知识源，为环境中各区域推导隐含注意事项并两两比较生成常识排序，成本驱动传统搜索得到可行路径；配套1350题基准显示其能发现未言明约束，绕开值得绕行的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数路径规划方法只遵守预先写明的约束，而在人类环境中的机器人还需遵循未明确表述的隐性考虑（如湿滑地板），这类考虑难以预先枚举，但直接影响安全与常识性行为。

Method: 给定抽象指令（如"move carefully"）与各区域视觉观测，用VLM为每个区域生成具体考虑（如"湿地板滑、值得绕行"）；再用LLM对区域两两比较"哪个更该避开"，得到区域常识排序；将排序转化为代价，交由传统搜索算法求解，保证输出始终是有效路径。构建含3个环境、1350个问题、5条指令×3个抽象层级的基准。

Result: 实验显示CoRS能发现未言明的考虑，绕开值得绕行的区域、穿过其余区域；近期的LLM-based规划器不具备该行为。

Conclusion: 将LLM/VLM的常识判断外化为区域排序并嵌入经典搜索，是一种兼顾常识推理与路径有效性的可行范式，但代价推理链与真实机器人验证仍有待补充。

Abstract: We present \emph{commonsense ranked search} (CoRS), a novel path planner that turns an abstract instruction into a route that follows commonsense. While existing methods respect the considerations written down in advance, a robot working among people must follow those left unstated too, as with a wet floor that a worker avoids without being told. CoRS leverages large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) as commonsense knowledge to reason about these latent considerations in its planning. Given an abstract instruction (\emph{e.g.}, ``move carefully'') and visual observations of each region in the environment, CoRS derives a consideration for each region, as in ``this wet floor is slippery and worth a detour.'' It then compares the considerations between regions to see which of the two the robot should avoid more, as in ``the crowd is worse than the wet floor.'' These judgments sort the regions into a commonsense ranking, whose costs drive a conventional search that always returns a valid route. We build a benchmark for planning under latent considerations, with three environments, 1350 problems, and five instructions at three levels of abstraction. Experiments show that CoRS discovers the unstated considerations and goes around the ones worth a detour while crossing the rest, a behavior that recent LLM-based planners do not achieve.

</details>


### [261] [Whole-Body UMI: Transferring UMI Manipulation Skills to Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation via Real-Time Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22829)
*Yuxuan Nai,Leixin Chang,Liangjing Yang,Shuo Yang,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WB-UMI：一个与任务无关、实时、以末端执行器为条件的全身运动生成器，通过共享末端执行器接口解耦全身协调学习与任务语义学习，仅用原生UMI数据和重定向运动捕捉训练，在G1人形机器人上实现四类任务的闭环迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身操作演示采集主要依赖遥操作，成本高、难以规模化。UMI提供了可扩展的数据采集范式，但仅有末端执行器轨迹无法确定全身协调，不足以支撑全身演示采集。

Method: 通过共享末端执行器接口解耦全身协调学习与任务语义学习：扩散策略从原生UMI演示中学习，WB-UMI则独立地从重定向的运动捕捉数据学习，任务特定数据采集阶段无需身体追踪器或成对的图像—全身演示；真实部署时使用异步分层架构整合扩散策略、运动生成器与全身控制器，并加入延迟补偿和实测状态反馈。

Result: 在G1真实机器人上完成四个任务的实时闭环迁移：关抽屉90%、货架取放80%、抛球30%、Loco-PnP 40%。

Conclusion: 该分层架构可有效将原生UMI技能迁移至人形全身操作，验证了末端执行器条件化运动生成作为可扩展全身演示采集接口的可行性，但高动态任务（抛球、移动操作）成功率仍偏低。

Abstract: Collecting whole-body demonstrations for humanoid manipulation mostly relies on teleoperation, which is costly and hard to scale up. The Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) provides a scalable data collection paradigm, but end-effector trajectories alone underdetermine humanoid whole-body coordination, which is insufficient for whole-body demonstration collection. Therefore, we introduce Whole-Body UMI (WB-UMI), a task-agnostic, real-time and end-effector conditioned motion generator that decouples whole-body coordination learning from task semantics learning through a shared end-effector interface. A diffusion policy learns from native UMI demonstrations, while WB-UMI learns independently from retargeted motion capture, requiring no body trackers or paired image--whole-body demonstrations during task-specific data collection. In real deployment, an asynchronous hierarchy integrates the diffusion policy, motion generator, and a whole-body controller with latency compensation and measured-state feedback. Real-robot experiments on G1 support real-time closed-loop transfer across four tasks, achieving 90% success in drawer closing, 80% in shelf pick-and-place, 30% in ball toss, and 40% in Loco-PnP, which shows the effectiveness of this hierarchy in transferring native UMI skills to humanoid whole-body manipulation.

</details>


### [262] [ForceRFT: Refining VLA Actions through Force-Guided Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22840)
*Yichen Wang,Chaoyang Zhang,Xuqi Su,Jun Ma,Haiyue Zhu,Xiaocong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ForceRFT：冻结的示范训练 SmolVLA 先验生成力条件动作块，轻量残差 actor 依据执行期腕部力/力矩及其时间变化做末端位姿残差修正；人类纠正监督残差 actor，自主段验证转移训练 twin critics 并做价值引导更新，自举限制在自主段内。真机插销、套环、擦白板成功率优于示范训练与残差模仿基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力条件 VLA 策略仅靠示范训练时，恢复行为受示范覆盖限制且无法从部署结果中学习；人类纠正模仿虽补充恢复样本，但其目标只是局部动作匹配，未显式优化任务回报。

Method: 冻结的示范训练 SmolVLA 先验输出力条件动作块；轻量残差 actor 用执行动作块期间采集的腕部力/力矩反馈精修单个末端位姿指令；决策时刻的腕部 wrench、其时间变化及所选基础运动共同条件化残差修正与价值估计。人类纠正监督残差 actor，经验证的自主转移训练 twin critics 并做价值引导更新；bootstrapping 仅限自主片段，避免 TD 信用跨越人类干预边界。

Result: 在插销插入、ring-on-peg 装配、白板擦拭三项真机任务上，自主成功率高于所评估的示范训练基线与残差模仿基线；与残差模仿的对比支持价值引导的残差优化，插销插入消融显示直接使用执行期腕部反馈的收益。

Conclusion: 将人类纠正的监督学习与自主结果的价值学习结合，并在残差层面用执行期力反馈条件化，可在接触丰富任务中提升自主成功率；关键在于自举边界约束与结构化力反馈而非单纯示范扩充。

Abstract: Force-conditioned vision-language-action (VLA) policies can respond to contact, but when trained solely on demonstrations, their recovery behavior may be limited by demonstration coverage, and they do not learn from deployment outcomes. Human corrective imitation provides additional recovery examples, but its objective matches local action targets without explicitly optimizing task return. We present ForceRFT, a force-guided residual reinforcement learning framework that learns contact-dependent corrections from human supervision and autonomous task outcomes. A frozen, demonstration-trained SmolVLA-based prior generates force-conditioned action chunks, while a lightweight residual actor refines individual end-effector pose commands using wrist feedback acquired during chunk execution. The decision-time wrist wrench, its temporal change, and the selected base motion condition both residual correction and value estimation. Human corrections supervise the residual actor, while verified autonomous transitions train the twin critics and support value-guided updates to the same actor. Bootstrapping is restricted to autonomous segments, preventing TD credit from crossing human-intervention boundaries. Real-robot experiments on plug insertion, ring-on-peg assembly, and whiteboard wiping show higher autonomous success rates than the evaluated demonstration-trained and residual-imitation baselines. Comparisons with residual imitation support value-guided residual optimization, while plug-insertion ablations indicate the benefit of direct execution-time wrist feedback.

</details>


### [263] [Physical-Touch Observability from Wrist Wrench in Granular Scooping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22852)
*Hongyi Lin,Song Zhang,Xubo Liu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出"物理接触可观测性(physical-touch observability)"概念,验证机器人铲装任务中腕部六轴力/力矩(F/T)信号是否携带最终采集体积的信息,且该信息依赖于正确配对的"动作-地形"交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挖掘与土方作业是具身智能的重要真实部署场景,但装载与铲掘仍依赖熟练人工操作,使人员与设备暴露于作业风险。预接触RGB-D感知仅能揭示表面几何,无法反映交互过程中出现的阻力、压实、工具啮合与载荷传递等隐藏物理状态,因此需要探索交互过程中的力觉感知能否提供补充信息。

Method: 基于6,700次真实机器人铲装、覆盖67种地形,在留出地形(terrain-held-out)测试下,将当前铲斗的腕部F/T信号与预接触预测、配对破坏对照(仅动作、跨地形错配F/T)进行对比;并使用人工设计的信号汇总特征在多种模型架构上重复验证。

Result: 在回顾性的60%序列边界处,正确配对的F/T将平均绝对误差相对Action-only降低14.2%,相对跨地形错配F/T降低21.9%;工程化信号汇总在不同模型架构下复现该结果。

Conclusion: 腕部力/矩不仅是低层反馈信号,更可作为任务级感知模态,使具身机器人能够在交互中推断隐藏物理状态,为挖掘等接触丰富任务的响应感知型自主性提供基础。

Abstract: Mining and earthmoving are important real-world deployment settings for embodied intelligence. Autonomous transport and driving systems have improved substantially, but loading and scooping still often depend on skilled human operators, exposing personnel and equipment to operational risk. For robotic scooping, pre-contact RGB-D sensing reveals surface geometry but not the resistance, compaction, tool engagement, or load transfer that emerge during interaction. We test whether the current scoop's six-axis wrist force/torque (F/T), or wrist wrench, contains information about final collected volume, and whether that information depends on the correctly paired action-terrain interaction. We call this property physical-touch observability. Using 6,700 real-robot scoops across 67 terrains, we evaluate correctly paired current-scoop F/T against pre-contact prediction and correspondence-breaking controls under terrain-held-out testing. At the retrospective 60% sequence boundary, correctly paired F/T reduces mean absolute error by 14.2% relative to Action-only and by 21.9% relative to cross-terrain mismatched F/T. Engineered signal summaries reproduce the result across model architectures. Together, these findings position wrist wrench not merely as a low-level feedback signal, but as a task-level perceptual modality through which embodied robots can infer hidden physical states during interaction, providing a foundation for response-aware autonomy in mining and other contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [264] [SmoLSTM: A Compact Vision-Language-Action Model with Recurrent Memory that Persists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22854)
*Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Parsa Mastouri Kashani,Connor Gäde,Matthias Kerzel,Philipp Allgeuer,Cornelius Weber,Stefan Wermter,Jae Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoLSTM 将冻结的 256M SmolVLM 主干与矩阵记忆 LSTM 控制层耦合，把观测 token 与动作 query 统一在整段 episode 不重置的因果流中，以 O(1) 递归状态替代加长观测窗口，在 LIBERO-Mem 上取得 85.1% 子目标覆盖率与 77.5% 全任务成功率（仅 0.04B 可训练参数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型多仅依赖当前观测预测动作，在物体遮挡或视觉上完全相同的物体任务中缺乏 episode 历史而变得歧义。常见对策是加长观测窗口，但这使时间视界变成超参数，且单步计算成本随窗口长度增长。作者希望用递归状态来承载 episode 记忆，使存储与推理成本与 episode 长度解耦。

Method: 提出 SmoLSTM：冻结的 256M 参数 SmolVLM 主干 + 矩阵记忆 LSTM 控制层；观测 token 与动作 query 统一为单一因果流，且在整个 episode 中从不重置，递归状态存储复杂度为 O(1)。动作头采用 flow matching，每个控制步预测 10 步末端执行器位姿增量与夹爪命令的动作块。单一策略在 140 个任务、7,461 条演示数据上联合训练，并在留出初始状态上评测。消融实验为每控制步重置递归状态。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Mem 上，子目标覆盖率达 85.1%，全任务成功率 77.5%，可训练参数仅 0.04B，优于该基准自带的物体中心基线及近期基于记忆的方法；每控制步重置递归状态会使全任务成功率降至 7.0%。同一模型在标准 LIBERO 上平均成功率为 79.6%。

Conclusion: 递归状态可有效替代加长观测窗口，为遮挡与视觉相同物体场景提供低成本 episode 记忆；消融证明策略确实依赖跨决策传递的上下文，而非单步观测。方法以极小可训练参数量取得领先性能，但证据主要来自仿真基准，尚缺真实机器人部署验证。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models often predict actions from only the current observation, which can leave tasks involving object occlusion or visually identical objects ambiguous without episode history. The usual countermeasure, widening the observation window, turns the horizon into a hyperparameter and lets per-step cost grow with it. We instead capture the episode in a recurrent state. SmoLSTM couples a frozen 256M-parameter SmolVLM backbone to a matrix-memory LSTM control layer in which observation tokens and action queries are unified in a single causal stream that is never reset throughout the entire episode. Recurrent-state storage is therefore O(1) in episode length. A flow-matching action head predicts chunks of 10 end-effector pose deltas and gripper commands at each control step. Our single policy, trained jointly on 7,461 demonstrations across 140 tasks and evaluated on held-out initial states, performs best, reaching 85.1% subgoal coverage and 77.5% full-task success on LIBERO-Mem with 0.04B trainable parameters, surpassing both the benchmark's own object-centric baseline and recent memory-based approaches. Resetting the recurrent state at every control step reduces full-task success to 7.0%, showing that the trained policy relies on context carried between decisions. The same model achieves 79.6% average success on standard LIBERO.

</details>


### [265] [PileBelief: Persistent Physical State for Interaction-Driven World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22858)
*Hongyi Lin,Song Zhang,Haiquan Liu,Yang Liu,Jinhua Zhao,Xiaobo Qu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PileBelief：面向部分可观测挖掘任务的交互驱动持久世界模型，通过物理先验+形变记忆+响应记忆实现多步地形变化与动作后果预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挖掘场景中每次挖斗都会重塑地形并影响后续动作，但局部观测无法揭示地表以下的支撑与物料状态；现有仅基于当前观测的世界模型难以维持跨交互的物理证据。

Method: 提出 PileBelief：观测条件化的物理先验 + 世界地址化形变记忆 + 物理响应记忆；采用动作对齐读取与门控残差修正地形变化与结果预测；部署权重固定下，已完成交互更新"实测信念"，假设动作推进独立"想象状态"。

Result: 相比仅用当前观测的基线，五步联合预测误差降低10.8%，离线动作选择后悔值降低65.5%；在 Newton/MPM 仿真与真实挖掘数据集上地形变化与铲斗体积预测均有提升。

Conclusion: 持久物理信念是机器人持续重塑环境中世界模型的有效表示，可在土壤内部状态未知时仅凭局部观测完成多步预测与候选动作排序。

Abstract: World models allow robots to anticipate action consequences before execution. This capability is especially valuable in excavation, where each scoop reshapes the terrain and affects subsequent actions. Local observations, however, cannot fully reveal the underlying support and material conditions. We present PileBelief, an interaction-driven persistent world model for partially observed excavation that retains physical evidence beyond the visible surface. It combines an observation-conditioned physical prior with world-addressed deformation memory and physical-response memory. Action-aligned reads and gated residual corrections refine terrain-change and outcome predictions. With deployment weights fixed, completed interactions update measured belief, while hypothetical actions advance a separate imagined state. Compared with a current-observation-only baseline, PileBelief reduces five-step joint prediction error by 10.8% and offline action-selection regret by 65.5%. Experiments on Newton/MPM and real excavation datasets further demonstrate improved terrain-change and bucket-volume prediction. Our method enables multi-step prediction and candidate-action ranking from local observations, even when the underlying soil state is unknown. These results identify persistent physical belief as a useful representation for world models of environments that robots continually reshape.

</details>


### [266] [A Reconfigurable Dual-Opposition Architecture for Single-Hand Assembly and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22871)
*William Su,Yunosuke Nakamura,Yixiao Wang,Yitong Li,Mingrui Yu,Huanan Qi,Boyuan Liang,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可重构双对置（dual-opposition）灵巧手架构，用四个独立驱动手指组成两个虚拟手指对置，配合可重构手掌实现单手中的双手持物与相对运动控制，完成抓取、对齐、紧固、压装等手内装配任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手内装配需在单手内同时保持对两个分离零件的抓取并控制其相对运动，传统灵巧手难以兼顾同时双物抓取与手内操作，因此需要新的机构与运动学架构来支持双物抓取协调操作。

Method: 设计四指独立驱动、组织成两个虚拟手指对置的可重构手掌架构；建立手指与手掌运动学模型及以物体尺寸为条件的工作空间公式；表征指关节运动与手掌跟踪性能；通过仿真策略训练（含外部扰动微调）与硬件演示评估手内装配（抓取、对齐、紧固、压装）与单物旋转，并做消融实验。

Result: 仿真中直径40–230 mm球体连续旋转平均成功率98.6%，LEAP Hand为73.8%；外部扰动微调后多方向扰动下成功率92.8%，LEAP Hand为45.2%；硬件演示了仿真训练策略迁移到不同尺寸物体的手内旋转；消融实验表明手指外展/内收与手掌重构对成功装配至关重要。

Conclusion: 该可重构双对置架构可在单手内同时支持两个分离零件的装配操作与单物体的协调操作，是一种兼具手内装配与手内操作能力的灵巧手设计。

Abstract: In-hand assembly is constrained by the need to maintain grasps on two separate parts while controlling their relative motion within a single hand. To enable both in-hand assembly and manipulation, we present a reconfigurable dual-opposition architecture. Specifically, to support simultaneous grasping of two parts and coordinated in-hand manipulation, four independently actuated fingers are organized into two virtual finger (VF) oppositions, with their relative configuration controlled by a reconfigurable palm. To describe hand motion and simultaneous two-object grasping configurations, a kinematic model of the fingers and palm and an object-size-conditioned workspace formulation are built. To further evaluate motion performance and assembly capability, finger-joint motion and palm tracking are characterized, and in-hand assembly is demonstrated through tasks involving grasping, alignment, fastening, and pressing. Ablation experiments further demonstrate the importance of finger abduction/adduction and palm reconfiguration for successful in-hand assembly. In simulation, the proposed hand achieves a mean continuous sphere rotation success rate of 98.6% over diameters of 40-230 mm, compared with 73.8% for the LEAP Hand. After policy fine-tuning with external disturbances, the proposed hand achieves 92.8% success under disturbances from multiple directions, compared with 45.2% for the LEAP Hand. Hardware demonstrations further show in-hand rotation of objects of different sizes using policies trained in simulation. Together, these results show that the proposed architecture supports both assembly of two separately held parts and coordinated manipulation of a single object within one hand.

</details>


### [267] [Barrier Certificate Synthesis for Non-Polynomial Robotic Dynamics via Polynomial Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22885)
*Shivam Chaubey,Francesco Verdoja,Shankar Deka,Ville Kyrki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用精确多项式提升将含三角项的非多项式机器人动力学转为带代数约束的多项式增广动力学，在提升域内做联合障碍证书综合并设计采样数据安全滤波器，在协调转弯与平面多旋翼模型上提升认证覆盖率、降低成本并减少闭环干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键机器人要求轨迹在容许控制下保持在给定安全集内，控制障碍证书可证明其中受控不变区域，但平方和（SOS）综合需多项式动力学，无法直接处理三角函数等常见机器人非线性；已有工作或依赖多项式近似/泰勒展开而损失非线性几何精度，或只针对特定框架，未系统评估"提升"带来的收益能否跨综合框架保持。

Method: 采用精确多项式提升（exact polynomial lifting）：用多项式增广状态替换非多项式动力学，并附加提升诱导的代数约束（如 sin/cos 满足的单位圆约束），从而在不做近似的前提下保留非线性几何。在此基础上提出提升域联合障碍证书综合，同时求解证书与状态反馈控制见证量；为应对零阶保持（ZOH）实现，设计采样数据安全滤波器（sampled-data safety filter）。此外将样本引导的逐次障碍（successive-barrier）方法适配到提升表示，以便在两个综合框架间公平比较。验证对象为协调转弯模型与平面多旋翼模型。

Result: 在两种方法上精确保留提升几何均提升了认证覆盖：在相同样本量下，提升域逐次障碍综合以更少障碍证书和更低计算成本获得更高覆盖率；提升域联合障碍综合相比测试中最细的分段分辨率先前方法，覆盖率更高、计算成本更低。闭环实验中安全滤波器在所有评估轨迹上保持可行性与安全性，降低空间保守性，且对两个模型都减少了控制干预量。

Conclusion: 精确多项式提升为含三角函数等非多项式非线性的机器人系统提供了无需近似的障碍证书综合途径，可在保持非线性几何的同时跨综合框架稳定获益，并在闭环中兼顾安全性与低保守性、低干预；采样数据安全滤波器使其可直接用于ZOH数字控制实现。

Abstract: Safe operation of robotic systems requires trajectories to remain within a prescribed safe set under admissible control inputs. Barrier certificates provide such guarantees by certifying a controlled-invariant region within that set. Sum-of-squares optimization offers a systematic way to synthesize such certificates, but its direct application requires polynomial dynamics, excluding common robotic nonlinearities, including trigonometric terms. We address this limitation using exact polynomial lifting, which replaces non-polynomial dynamics with polynomial-augmented dynamics subject to lifting-induced algebraic constraints, preserving nonlinear geometry without approximation. We formulate lifted-domain joint barrier synthesis that computes a certificate with a state-feedback control witness and develop a sampled-data safety filter for zero-order-hold implementation. To assess whether the benefits of lifting persist across synthesis frameworks, we also adapt a sample-guided successive-barrier method to the lifted representation. On coordinated-turn and planar multirotor models, exact lifting improves certified coverage in both methods: at matched sample sizes, lifted successive-barrier synthesis achieves higher coverage with fewer barriers and lower computational cost, while lifted joint barrier synthesis provides higher coverage and lower computational cost than the finest tested piecewise resolution. In closed-loop experiments, the safety filter maintains feasibility and safety across all evaluated trajectories, reduces spatial conservativeness, and requires less intervention for both models.

</details>


### [268] [Stable and Efficient Real-World Online VLA Post-Training via Asynchronous Replay-Anchored Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22888)
*Jiarui Yang,Jiajin Zhang,Bin Zhu,Jingjing Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPolicy：异步rollout+学习的VLA在线后训练框架，critic用回放行为构造chunk级Bellman目标（不预测下一动作），actor用一步flow并复用rollout初始噪声做优势加权条件似然监督；真机1-2小时在线预算下，单任务平均86.3%成功率，五任务联合从52%提升到88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在线后训练需要高效利用机器人交互数据，并在持续采集的经验上实现可靠策略改进；现有方法存在计算开销大、依赖回放覆盖之外的动作价值估计、以及执行策略与学习目标不匹配等问题。

Method: 异步Replay-Anchored Policy improvement框架，rollout与学习并发执行，critic与actor更新都以回放行为为锚定：critic从记录动作学习chunk级价值，不预测下一动作即可构造Bellman目标，降低计算量并减少对回放覆盖外动作价值估计的依赖；一步flow actor复用rollout期间存储的初始噪声，通过优势加权条件似然学习，直接监督执行所用的动作映射。

Result: 真实世界四个单任务+一个五任务联合设置，在线训练预算仅1-2小时，每任务仅10条示范微调起步：单任务快速适应并达平均86.3%成功率；联合多任务从52%提升到88%，同时保持已可靠任务性能并改善较弱能力，总体成功率显著优于基线且所需人工干预更少。

Conclusion: RAPolicy以回放锚定的critic与actor设计实现了稳定、高样本效率的真机在线策略改进，验证了异步rollout-学习并行在VLA在线后训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Online post-training of vision-language-action (VLA) models requires efficient use of robot interaction and reliable policy improvement from continually collected experience. We propose asynchronous Replay-Anchored Policy improvement (RAPolicy), a framework that performs rollout and learning concurrently while grounding both critic and actor updates in replayed behavior. The critic learns chunk-level values from recorded actions and constructs Bellman targets without predicting next actions, reducing computation and dependence on action-value estimates outside replay coverage. The one-step flow actor reuses the initial noise stored during rollout and learns through advantage-weighted conditional likelihood, directly supervising the action mapping used for execution. We evaluate RAPolicy across four single-task settings and one joint five-task setting in the real world, with online training budgets of only 1--2 hours. Starting from policies fine-tuned on just 10 demonstrations per task, RAPolicy rapidly adapts to new single tasks and achieves an average 86.3% success rate. In the joint multi-task setting, RAPolicy improves overall success rate from 52% to 88% while preserving performance on already reliable tasks and improving weaker capabilities. Overall, RAPolicy substantially outperforms the baselines in aggregate task success while requiring fewer human interventions, demonstrating stable policy improvement and high online training efficiency. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [269] [H-VLA: Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Model with Key-Action Reasoning and Motion Planning in a Unified Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22895)
*Xiongfeng Peng,Lu Xu,Yandong Wang,Jiaqian Yu,Zirui Zheng,Yamin Mao,Weiming Li,Inseop Chung,Hyun-woong Cho,Jaewook Yoo,Dongwook Lee,Daehyun Ji,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H-VLA 提出分层 VLA 框架，将高层关键动作推理与低层稠密动作生成解耦，并引入统一相机中心动作空间与两阶段训练，在 SimplerEnv 与 Agilex 真机上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 方法直接将语言与视觉观测映射为稠密动作，削弱了预训练 VLM 的语义推理能力，且在物体位置、场景布局、机器人本体与相机视角变化下鲁棒性差。

Method: H-VLA 分层解耦：Key-Action Model 预测下一步操作子目标（关键动作）；Motion Planning Model 以关键动作为条件生成稠密未来动作；统一相机中心动作空间保证跨数据集、本体与视角的表征一致性；两阶段训练（预训练侧重关键动作推理，微调侧重稠密运动生成）。

Result: SimplerEnv 上 Google Robot visual matching 91%、variant aggregation 84%、WidowX visual matching 81%；Agilex 真机任务在分布内、位置 OOD、场景/物体 OOD 三种设定下较最强基线分别提升 10、47、16 个百分点。

Conclusion: 分层解耦关键动作推理与运动生成，配合统一相机中心动作空间，能有效保留 VLM 语义先验并提升跨空间变化的泛化与真机部署表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation, but many existing methods still rely on direct mappings from language and visual observations to dense actions. This formulation can weaken the semantic reasoning capability inherited from pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which are mainly optimized for visual-linguistic understanding rather than low-level control, and becomes fragile under spatial variations, including changes in object positions, scene layouts, robot embodiments, and camera viewpoints. To address these limitations, we propose H-VLA, a hierarchical VLA framework that decouples high-level key-action reasoning from low-level motion generation. H-VLA combines a Key-Action Model for predicting a key-action as the next manipulation subgoal, a Motion Planning Model for generating dense future actions conditioned on the predicted key-action, and a Unified Camera-Centric Action Space for consistent representation across datasets, embodiments, and viewpoints. We further adopt a two-stage training strategy that emphasizes key-action reasoning during pre-training and dense motion generation during fine-tuning. Experiments show that H-VLA achieves strong performance on SimplerEnv, reaching 91% on Google Robot visual matching, 84% on Google Robot variant aggregation, and 81% on WidowX visual matching. On Agilex real-robot tasks, H-VLA improves over the strongest baseline by 10, 47, and 16 percentage points under in-distribution, out-of-distribution position, and out-of-distribution scene/object settings, respectively.

</details>


### [270] ["Dear LLaVA, Please Drive": A Depth-Aware Vision-Language Agent for Closed-Loop Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22925)
*Sebastian Berger,Katharina Winter,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种参数高效方法，用"命令式学习"（Imperative Learning）范式微调预训练VLM，基于可微几何代价场而非标注轨迹实现无碰撞导航，并用共享VLM主干+LoRA模块统一自然语言驱动的端到端机器人导航流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动机器人导航框架多依赖模仿学习，需大量标注轨迹数据，限制可扩展性与鲁棒性；希望利用VLM的语义理解与泛化能力，同时避免大规模标注轨迹的采集成本。

Method: 1) 用Imperative Learning范式，直接对可微几何代价场（collision cost field）做优化，仅依赖立体深度观测生成无碰撞路径，无需标注轨迹；2) 构建统一端到端导航流程，共享VLM主干，挂载任务特定LoRA模块，串联语义目标选择与低层轨迹规划；3) 更新参数量小于总参数1%。

Result: 在未见环境中取得有竞争力的SPL（Success weighted by Path Length）；真实世界定性实验验证了sim-to-real泛化与稳定路径规划，且未使用真实数据微调。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条在移动机器人上部署VLM智能体的实用路径：以极低参数开销实现高层语义导航，摆脱对大规模标注轨迹数据的依赖。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) provide a compelling foundation for reasoning-driven mobile navigation, offering rich contextual understanding and strong generalization from large-scale pretraining. Most existing navigation frameworks rely on imitation learning and therefore require substantial labeled trajectory data, limiting their scalability and robustness. In this work, we propose a parameter-efficient approach to fine-tune a pretrained VLM for autonomous navigation using an Imperative Learning paradigm. By optimizing against differentiable geometric cost fields rather than labeled trajectories, our model learns to generate collision-free paths exclusively from stereoscopic depth observations. We introduce a unified end-to-end navigation pipeline for natural-language-driven robotic control. This system leverages a shared VLM backbone with task-specific Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules, effectively bridging the gap from semantic target selection to low-level trajectory planning. Our approach achieves competitive Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) in unseen environments while updating less than 1% of the model's total parameters. Qualitative real-world experiments validate sim-to-real generalization and stable path planning without fine-tuning on real-world data. These results highlight a practical approach for deploying VLM-based agents on mobile robots, enabling high-level semantic navigation without the prohibitive requirement for large-scale, labeled trajectory data.

</details>


### [271] [Embodied Snap: Octopus-Inspired Distributed Reach-and-Attach with a Speed-Limited Soft Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22926)
*Linxin Hou,Zhihang Qin,Heyang Zou,Qirui Wu,Peiyi Wang,Muhammad Sunny Nazeer,Yongxin Guo,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"具身弹射控制器"(embodied snap controller),将慢速伺服预加载与快速弹性释放解耦,使软体臂突破直驱腱驱动的速度上限,实现动态reach-and-attach。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体臂的被动吸盘吸附需要精确瞄准与足够接触速度,但带减速器的执行器存在速度上限,仅靠改进轨迹跟踪无法突破。需要一种新的控制分工方式。

Method: 受章鱼生物学启发的分节组织先验,而非复刻章鱼神经系统;一个在三节间共享的学习策略选择预加载量、瞄准、腱松弛与释放时机;策略在硬件验证的循环模型上离线优化,并受实验支持边界约束。

Result: 5个优化种子、400个未见仿真目标:5 cm横向容差下吸附成功率(73±4)%,共享策略在共同评测预算的中位17%处达到集中式控制器的平均最终奖励。硬件尖端速度1.56–1.64 m/s,比直驱腱释放至少高108%。18次开环硬件试验(6个位置)中17次超过1 m/s弹射阈值,9次取回物体,5个位置成功取回。

Conclusion: 控制问题存在实用的职责分工:学习控制负责"准备身体",被动身体力学负责执行动态reach-and-attach所需的快速运动。

Abstract: Reach-and-attach of soft robotic arms with passive suction requires accurate targeting and sufficient contact speed, yet geared actuators can impose a speed limit that improved trajectory tracking alone cannot overcome. This paper proposes an embodied snap controller that separates slow servo-driven preloading from rapid elastic release, enabling a compliant arm to move beyond its direct tendon-driven speed limit. Octopus biology motivates the controller's section-wise organizational prior, rather than reproduction of the octopus nervous system. A learned policy shared across three sections selects preloads, aim, tendon slack, and release timing, determining where, how, and when to load and release the body. The policy is optimized offline using a hardware-validated recurrent model within experimentally supported bounds. Across five optimization seeds and 400 unseen simulated targets, attachment success is $(73\pm4)\%$ at a $5\text{ cm}$ lateral tolerance, and the shared policy reaches the matched centralized controller's mean final reward after a median $17\%$ of the common evaluation budget. Hardware characterization achieves tip speeds of 1.56-1.64 m/s, at least $108\%$ above direct tendon-driven release. In 18 open-loop hardware trials across six placements, 17 exceed the 1 m/s snap threshold and nine retrieve the object, with successful retrieval at five placements. These results demonstrate a practical division of responsibility in the control problem: learned control prepares the body, and passive body mechanics execute the rapid movement needed for dynamic reach-and-attach.

</details>


### [272] [MIRA-PRM: Mission-Informed Reusable Roadmap Planning for Mobile Gas-Sensing Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22954)
*Gongsen Wang,Siyuan Wang,Xinyuan Wang,Yuhan Wen,Feng Yang,Zhen Tian,Hoi Leong Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIRA-PRM：面向移动气体传感巡检的任务信息引导PRM，融合几何/任务流/巡检条件采样、局部自适应连接与证据门控精化；在142,448次规划运行及冻结留出集上实现高成功率与局部修复效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动气体感知巡检需要移动平台可靠地依次到达有序采样位姿，传统PRM/PRM*在有序多目标任务、有限节点预算和局部地图变更下成功率与效率不足，缺少面向任务语义与巡检条件的采样与连接机制。

Method: 提出MIRA-PRM：在概率路线图上引入几何、任务流程（mission-flow）与巡检条件三类条件化采样，配合局部自适应连接策略以及证据门控的精化机制；支持重复查询适应与局部地图修复式增量更新，替代整图重建。

Result: 八个开发战役共142,448次规划运行；有序目标从2增至8时保持100%种子级成功率；40节点上限下97.67%成功率，PRM 67.17%、PRM* 86.50%。局部修复较重建降低事件时间79.9-81.9%、节点增量90.8-94.3%。冻结留出（12图、48任务、每单元3种子）中144次验证成功96次，PRM 82、PRM* 89、LE-HG-PRM 70；配对同成功运行墙钟时间较PRM*降16.89%、较LE-HG-PRM降81.01%，但比PRM慢52.23%，路径长0.54-1.86%。所有有效路径通过完整分段校验与间隙非劣性检验。

Conclusion: 结果支持结构化仿真中面向可靠性的权衡策略（略长路径与相对PRM较高耗时换取成功率与可扩展性），后续需外部地图与物理移动感知平台的验证，尚未提供真机部署证据。

Abstract: Mobile gas-sensing inspection requires a mobile platform to reliably reach ordered sampling poses. We present MIRA-PRM, a mission-informed probabilistic roadmap that combines geometry-, mission-flow-, and inspection-conditioned sampling with locally adaptive connectivity and evidence-gated refinement. Eight development campaigns comprising 142,448 planner runs evaluated mission scaling, repeated-query adaptation, finite node budgets, target distributions, local map repair, ablation, cross-family comparison, and parameter sensitivity. MIRA-PRM maintained 100% seed-level success as ordered targets increased from two to eight, and achieved 97.67% seed-level success under a 40-node ceiling, compared with 67.17% for PRM and 86.50% for PRM*. Local repair reduced event time by 79.9-81.9% and node additions by 90.8-94.3% relative to rebuilding. On a separate frozen holdout of 12 maps, 48 ordered missions, and three seeds per map-mission unit, MIRA-PRM produced 96/144 validated successes, versus 82/144 for PRM, 89/144 for PRM*, and 70/144 for LE-HG-PRM. On paired common-success runs, MIRA-PRM reduced wall time by 16.89% relative to PRM* and 81.01% relative to LE-HG-PRM, but was 52.23% slower than PRM; paths were 0.54-1.86% longer. All validated paths passed independent full-segment checks and clearance non-inferiority. The results support a reliability-oriented trade-off in structured simulations and motivate external-map and physical mobile-sensing validation.

</details>


### [273] [Prescribed-Time Contracting-Boundary Control of a Tendon-Driven Flexible Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22963)
*Yi Lu,Chao Tang,Zhiji Han,Hongdu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向三段拮抗肌腱驱动柔性臂的曲率跟踪，提出"性能塑形"预定时间控制：用笛卡尔曲率表示规避直线位形下弯曲方向不定，构造三次性能边界在规定时间内由初始误差界平滑收缩至非零终端精度界，经静态对称误差缩放与时变行为塑形双重变换将误差映射到固定单位箱，保证边界不变性、预定时间进入终端精度区并渐近收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单段柔性臂在直线位形处弯曲方向未定义，且三对拮抗肌腱存在六自由度冗余驱动，曲率跟踪的显式运动学映射与控制律设计困难；同时应用中往往要求在规定时间内将误差收敛到指定精度，传统渐近或有限时间控制难以同时给出可预设的收敛时间与可保证的误差边界。

Method: 1) 引入笛卡尔曲率表示，避免直线位形处弯曲方向未定义，并建立六肌腱的显式运动学映射；2) 设计三次性能边界，在预定时间内从可行初始误差界平滑收缩至非零终端精度界；3) 基于该边界构造双重变换，由静态对称误差缩放与时变行为塑形将跟踪误差映射到固定单位箱；4) 据此设计控制器，实现边界不变性、预定时间进入终端精度区及后续渐近收敛；5) 用 Python 与 OpenCR-MuJoCo 数值评估，并在两段四通道平台上开展监督式降阶实验验证。

Result: 六组实验均未出现违反预定边界的情况；相比匹配基线，所提控制器将平均终端曲率 RMSE 降低 32.5%，进入终端误差带的时刻相当；数值仿真与降阶平台实验相互补充验证，支持该方法在降阶实验设定下的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的预定时间性能塑形控制可在保证误差边界不变的前提下实现曲率跟踪的定时收敛与终端精度，方法在降阶平台与仿真中可行；但验证局限于单段柔性臂的降阶实验，全尺寸多段系统与更复杂任务下的有效性仍待考察。

Abstract: This study develops a prescribed-time performance-shaping control method for curvature tracking of a single-segment flexible arm actuated by three antagonistic tendon pairs. A Cartesian curvature representation is introduced to avoid the undefined bending direction at the straight configuration and to establish an explicit six-tendon kinematic mapping. A cubic performance boundary contracts smoothly from an initially admissible error bound to a nonzero terminal accuracy bound within a prescribed time. Based on this boundary, a dual transformation combining static symmetric error scaling and time-varying behavior shaping maps the tracking error into a fixed unit box. The resulting controller guarantees boundary invariance, prescribed-time entry into the terminal accuracy region, and subsequent asymptotic convergence. Numerical evaluations with Python and OpenCR--MuJoCo, together with a supervised reduced-order experiment on a two-section, four-channel platform, provide complementary validation. Across six experimental trials, no violation of the prescribed boundary is observed, and the proposed controller reduces the mean terminal curvature RMSE by 32.5% relative to a matched baseline, with comparable terminal-band entry times. These results support the feasibility of the proposed approach in the reduced-order experimental setting.

</details>


### [274] [Transferring the Intelligence of VLMs to Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22966)
*Meng-Hao Guo,Zhe-Han Mo,Jia-Jun Wang,Yi Zhang,Kejin Wang,Yi-Xuan Deng,Jia-Peng Zhang,Yongming Rao,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboDawn 提出一种人类直觉式机器人控制接口，将机器人控制暴露给具备智能体能力的 VLM，仅用离散的平移、旋转和夹爪命令，并以闭环感知-推理-执行方式控制机器人；结合少样本 ICL 示范，实现无需任务专属机器人训练的跨域泛化，在 RoboTwin 2.0 C2R 与 RoboDojo 上取得 SOTA，并迁移到真实 Franka 机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能无缝适应物理与数字世界，暗示数字-现实鸿沟虽存在于具身、环境和任务层面，但智能本身可跨域迁移。因此作者追问：视觉语言模型（VLM）的智能是否能从数字世界泛化到物理世界用于机器人控制。现有机器人策略通常依赖任务专属的机器人数据训练，泛化性与数据效率受限。

Method: 核心是 RoboDawn 接口：用紧凑的离散动作原语（平移、旋转、夹爪）把机器人控制暴露给 agentic VLM，降低动作空间复杂度以匹配 VLM 的离散 token 生成能力。VLM 在闭环中运行：观察当前视觉状态、推理下一步动作、执行、再依据新状态调整后续决策。此外引入 in-context learning 方案，用少量示范将 VLM 对齐到接口使用方式与任务求解策略。
