Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 C2R 与 RoboDojo 上无需任务专属机器人训练即取得强性能。零样本下超越多个在基准专属机器人数据上训练的强策略；单条 ICL 示范带来显著提升并达到 SOTA。RoboTwin 2.0 C2R 成功率从零样本 53.2% 提升到单样本 73.6%，超过强基线 π0.5（46.0%）；RoboDojo 从 35.67% 提升到 47.17%。同一框架迁移到真实机器人，在 Franka 上完成 block-in-basket 与 block stacking。

Conclusion: VLM 的数字世界智能可以在一定程度上迁移到物理世界用于机器人控制，前提是提供人类直觉的紧凑离散接口与少量 ICL 示范。该范式无需任务专属机器人训练即可获得 SOTA 与真实机器人迁移能力，暗示具身智能可以通过接口对齐而非大量机器人数据采集来实现。

Abstract: Humans can seamlessly adapt to both physical and digital worlds, suggesting that while a digital-to-real gap exists in embodiment, environment and task, human intelligence itself may transfer across this gap. This naturally raises a fundamental question: can the intelligence of vision-language models (VLMs) similarly generalize from the digital world to the physical world for robotic control? We investigate this question through RoboDawn, a human-intuitive interface that exposes robotic control to an agentic VLM through a compact set of discrete translation, rotation, and gripper commands. Using this interface, the VLM controls a robot in a closed loop: it observes the current visual state, reasons about the next action, executes it, and adapts subsequent decisions to the resulting state. Furthermore, we introduce an in-context learning (ICL) scheme that uses a few demonstrations to ground the VLM in both interface usage and task-solving strategies. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 C2R and RoboDojo demonstrate that RoboDawn achieves strong performance without task-specific robot training. In the zero-shot setting, RoboDawn outperforms several strong policies trained on benchmarkspecific robot data, while a single in-context demonstration further yields substantial performance gains and establishes state-of-the-art (SOTA) results. On RoboTwin 2.0 C2R, the success rate increases from 53.2% zero-shot to 73.6% one-shot, exceeding the solid baseline {\pi}0.5 (46.0%). Similar gains are observed on RoboDojo, where success rate improves from 35.67% zero-shot to 47.17% one-shot. The same framework also transfers to real-world robots, performing block-in-basket and block stacking on Franka.

</details>


### [275] [An Empirical Study and Open Testbed for Federated Fine-Tuning of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22973)
*Zhekai Duan,Kevin Ziyang Xie,Xinyu Tan,Shikai Geng,Chengxu Zhou,Ramana Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Chris Xiaoxuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统研究预训练 VLA 策略的联邦微调，覆盖 LIBERO 40 个仿真任务与 6 个真实机器人任务（2/3 站点），分析联邦参数范围、聚合算法与分布偏移，并开源 model-/runtime-agnostic 试验台 DecentVLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地采集的机器人示教数据常被丢弃；联邦学习可复用这些分布式示教来学习共享策略，但能否适配大规模预训练 VLA 尚不明确，且缺乏可复现基准与可复用训练框架，导致结果难以比较。

Method: 在 3 个现代预训练 VLA 策略上做联邦微调实证研究：多种联邦参数范围（federated parameter scopes）、3 种聚合算法、分布偏移评测；仿真用 LIBERO 全部 40 个操作任务，真实实验含 2 站点与 3 站点采集示教的 6 个任务；配套开源模型与运行时无关的测试床 DecentVLA。

Result: 得出若干经验结论：联邦参数范围的影响强于聚合算法选择；物理机器人上因跨站点异质性更强，难以达到集中式微调效果；联邦 VLA 在异质数据上可匹配集中式微调，分布偏移下稳健性不低于集中式；部分联邦 + 本地保留的个性化设置可缓解预训练薄弱场景，但不产生可用的全局模型。

Conclusion: 预训练 VLA 的联邦微调可行且具特定优势（异质数据、稳健性、个性化），但真机场景仍落后于集中式微调；开源 DecentVLA 以促进该方向的可复现研究与公平比较。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model to a new robot, environment, or task requires demonstrations that are collected locally and often discarded. Federated learning is a promising approach to exploiting such distributed demonstrations by learning a shared policy. However, whether it can adapt large pretrained VLAs remains an open question, and a lack of reproducible benchmarks for pretrained VLAs and reusable training frameworks makes existing results difficult to compare. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study of federated fine-tuning of three modern pretrained VLA policies on the 40 simulated tasks of the LIBERO manipulation benchmark, and on six real-world tasks in two real-robot experiments, with demonstrations collected across two and three sites, respectively. Our study analyzes the key choices in this setting, spanning multiple federated parameter scopes, three aggregation algorithms, and evaluation under distribution shift. Based on the study, we derive a series of lessons, including the dominance of the federated scope over the choice of aggregation algorithm and the difficulty of matching centralized fine-tuning on physical robots, where cross-site heterogeneity is stronger than simulation captures. We also highlight opportunities for federated VLA learning, such as the ability to match centralized fine-tuning on heterogeneous data, to remain at least as robust as centralized fine-tuning under distribution shift, and to personalize, with each client federating part of the policy and keeping the rest local, which helps where the policy's pretraining is weak but leaves no usable global model. We open-source \decentvla{}, the model- and runtime-agnostic testbed behind the study, to facilitate future research and fair comparisons in federated VLA learning.

</details>


### [276] [A Horizon-slicing Approach to Minimum Obstacle Displacement Planning for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22974)
*Antony Thomas,Giulio Ferro,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni,Michela Robba,Marco Baglietto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文从机器人运动规划角度研究最小障碍物位移规划问题，证明其平面多边形障碍物建模下为NP-hard，提出精确最优公式及可权衡路径长度与位移量的近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当环境中不存在无碰撞路径时，机器人需通过移动可移动障碍物来开辟通路。现有文献缺乏对该问题最优性与计算复杂度的系统刻画，也缺少能在路径长度与障碍物位移量之间灵活权衡的高效方法，因此作者希望建立统一的问题表述并给出可行解法。

Method: 1) 将最小障碍物位移规划（Minimum Obstacle Displacement Planning）形式化为路径规划问题，目标是在移动可移动障碍物后到达目标位置；2) 从计算复杂度角度分析该问题，在多边形障碍物建模下证明其NP-hard性质；3) 给出一个能推广文献中其他相关问题的精确（最优）公式及其最优解；4) 提出一种计算负担更轻的近似算法，其解与最优解的偏差不超过最优代价的某一比例，并支持在路径长度与障碍物位移量之间进行权衡。

Result: 证明该问题在平面多边形障碍物情形下为NP-hard；给出统一的精确最优公式，可推广已有问题；提出近似算法，其代价与最优解相差不超过最优代价的一个分数比例，并能调节路径长度与障碍物位移量的权衡。

Conclusion: 最小障碍物位移规划具有内在计算难度，精确求解代价高；所提近似方法在保证有界近似比的同时显著降低计算量，为实际机器人导航中"推障碍开路"提供了理论与算法基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the Minimum Obstacle Displacement Planning problem from a robot motion planning perspective. The problem involves determining a feasible path to a goal location by displacing movable obstacles when no collision-free path initially exists. We show that this problem is computationally challenging and, in particular, NP-hard when obstacles are modeled as polygons in the plane. Besides an exact formulation of the minimum obstacle displacement problem generalizing other problems in the literature, and the associated optimal solution, this paper proposes an approximate solution that is less intensive from a computational standpoint, and differs from the optimal solution by a fraction of the optimal cost, being able to trade-off between path length and amount of obstacle displacements.

</details>


### [277] [Connectivity-Aware Exploration of Robotic Grasp Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22983)
*Maksim A Kazanskii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究分析成功抓取集合在SE(3)中的多尺度连通结构，指出其具有异质且可复现的连通性，并提出连通性感知采样策略以更高效地探索抓取空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抓取研究多关注从候选位姿中判别单个可行动作，而忽略了成功动作在空间中的组织方式，因此作者希望探究可行抓取在SE(3)中的多尺度结构及其对探索效率的价值。

Method: 基于大规模抓取数据集，刻画成功抓取集合的连通性结构；提出连通性感知采样策略，优先探索连通分量间的潜在桥接、结构性前沿、边界扩展与几何新颖点，并与随机采样、最远点采样在受控重建实验中对比。

Result: 在受控重建实验中，该方法比随机采样与最远点采样更高效地恢复成功抓取集合的连通结构；并进一步验证了在隐藏可行性的条件下所获取的连通性对后续抓取发现的促进，以及结构性经验从已探索物体向未见物体的迁移。

Conclusion: 可行动作的空间组织本身提供了超越单个候选动作可行性的探索信息，支持将结构感知探索作为利用机器人操作中可行动作空间几何的一种手段。

Abstract: Robotic grasping is typically formulated as the problem of identifying successful actions from a space of candidate grasp poses. However, the organization of successful actions within this space has received less attention. We study the multiscale structure of viable robotic grasps in $SE(3)$ and investigate whether this structure can be exploited for more efficient exploration. Using a large-scale grasp dataset, we show that successful grasp sets exhibit heterogeneous and reproducible connectivity structure across objects. We then introduce a connectivity-aware sampling strategy that incrementally explores the currently observed grasp space by prioritizing potential bridges between components, structural frontiers, boundary extensions, and geometric novelty. In controlled reconstruction experiments, the method recovers the connectivity structure of successful grasp sets substantially more efficiently than random sampling and farthest-point sampling. We further evaluate whether connectivity acquired under hidden grasp viability can improve subsequent grasp discovery, and whether structural experience from previously explored objects can be retrieved and transferred to unseen objects. These results suggest that the spatial organization of viable actions provides information relevant to grasp-space exploration beyond the viability of individual candidate actions. More broadly, they motivate structure-aware exploration as a means of exploiting the geometry of viable action spaces in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [278] [Fast and Robust Temporal Logic Planning via ADMM-based Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23037)
*Lukas Pries,Joris Verhagen,Jon Arrizabalaga,Jana Tumova,Markus Ryll,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种快速数值方法，将非凸安全与逻辑约束建模为凸集并集，并用联合可行性图统一编码离散决策；基于ADMM的非凸分裂算法将光滑轨迹优化与非光滑离散约束解耦，实现可靠收敛，平均加速4.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间运动规划在时序逻辑（TL）规范下需要同时满足平滑性、无碰撞与鲁棒满足逻辑/时序任务，这类问题本质是非凸且含离散决策，现有方法求解慢、规模受限。

Method: 将非凸安全与逻辑约束表示为凸集并集，把离散决策编码进联合可行性图；把欧氏投影与近端鲁棒性最大化分别重述为最短路径与最宽路径问题；在此基础上构建基于ADMM的非凸分裂方法，将光滑时空轨迹优化与非光滑离散约束处理解耦。

Result: 在多个基准上表现出一致的可靠收敛，可扩展到大规模运动规划问题；在离散与连续时间逻辑问题上相比当前最优方法平均获得4.7倍加速。

Conclusion: 图结构的凸集并集表示结合ADMM分裂，为TL规范下的连续时间安全运动规划提供了可扩展且收敛稳定的高效求解框架。

Abstract: We present a fast numerical method for safe continuous-time motion planning under Temporal Logic (TL) specifications. The method generates smooth continuous trajectories that remain collision-free while robustly satisfying temporal and logical task requirements. A central component of our method is the formulation of nonconvex safety and logic constraints as unions of convex sets where associated discrete decisions are encoded in a joint feasibility graph. This graph representation allows Euclidean projection onto the feasible set and proximal robustness maximization to be reformulated as shortest- and widest-path problems, respectively. Building on this structure, we develop a nonconvex splitting method based on the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM), which decouples smooth spatio-temporal trajectory optimization from nonsmooth discrete constraint handling within the optimization. The resulting algorithm exhibits reliable convergence across benchmarks and scales to large-scale motion-planning problems, providing a 4.7x average speedup over the state of the art on discrete and continuous-time logic problems.

</details>


### [279] [Anatomy of a Closed-Loop Collapse: A Causal Case Study of a Compressed VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23048)
*Fengze Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对Octo-Base蒸馏策略的闭环失效进行因果解剖：识别出晚期负z残差签名，并通过替换半数训练流为部署分布教师回放恢复性能，说明离线评测门控不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩后的操作策略可通过离线评测却在闭环执行中失败，这一脱节已被先前工作确立，非本文主张。作者旨在对其中一个自然发生的案例给出因果解剖，回答"失败是什么、何处失败、临床签名是什么、标准疗法为何无效"。

Method: 以Octo-Base的8层蒸馏为案例（保留86%参数，离线教师比达0.996/1.000），在WidowX仿真pick-and-place任务上做闭环评测（0/72 vs 教师40/72）。用配对动作轨迹取证定位失败签名；对四种标准疗法在匹配控制下逐一检验（继续训练、域内离线数据、命令级补偿、命令通道钳制症状）；再做最小配对干预——将一半训练流替换为部署分布教师rollout，其余设置全部固定。

Result: 失效是结构性的：早期阶段渐进退化（移动速率90%、抓取55%），而搬运至目标阶段彻底失败（所有训练变体均为0%）。签名为负的、偏晚期、约10倍于修复后幅度的z残差，并在基础蒸馏和两个延续分支中持续存在。四种标准疗法均失败：继续训练与域内离线数据成功率仍为0（后者虽改善边际动作统计）；命令级补偿任意偏移均无恢复；钳制症状可保住抓取但成功率仍在地板。最小配对干预恢复与教师持平（18/36 vs 17/36留出）并消除该签名。

Conclusion: 主张的是存在性而非普遍性。操作层面，离线门控（包括家族自带的验证指标）对压缩策略不足以作为验收测试，几十次闭环试验即可发现其遗漏；失败需要借助闭环诊断与部署分布数据干预才能修复。

Abstract: Compressed manipulation policies can pass offline evaluation while failing in closed-loop execution; this dissociation is established in prior work and is not our claim. We contribute a causal anatomy of one naturally occurring case. An 8-layer distillation of Octo-Base retains 86% of parameters, passes every offline check we applied (0.996 and 1.000 teacher-ratios on the family's own validation metrics), and collapses in closed loop: 0/72 vs. the teacher's 40/72 on a simulated WidowX pick-and-place task. The collapse is structured, not diffuse: early task stages degrade gradually (the student moves the object at 90% of the teacher's rate and grasps at 55%), while transport-to-target fails categorically, at 0% in every training variant. Paired action-trace forensics isolate the signature: a negative, late-heavy $z$ residual, roughly 10x its post-repair magnitude, and persistent across the base distillation and both continuation branches. Four standard therapies fail under matched controls: continued training and in-domain offline data leave success at zero, even though the latter measurably improves marginal action statistics; command-level compensation recovers nothing at any offset, although the same perturbations degrade healthy policies; clamping the symptom in the command channel preserves grasping, yet success stays at floor. A minimal-pair intervention that substitutes half of the training stream with deployment-distribution teacher rollouts, with every other setting held fixed, restores parity with the teacher (18/36 vs. 17/36 held-out), eliminates that signature, and recovers a teacher-like perturbation-response profile. We claim existence, not universality. Operationally, offline gates, including a family's own validation metrics, are insufficient acceptance tests for compressed policies; a few dozen closed-loop trials sufficed to find what they missed.

</details>


### [280] [On the Control of Mobile Ad-Hoc Agent Deployments in Partially Observed Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23060)
*Edwin Meriaux,Louis-Roy Langevin,Shuo Wen,Ndiamé Ndiaye,Gregory Dudek,Antonio Loría*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为未知正交环境中的移动自组网在线部署建立部分可观测协作守卫画廊模型，证明CADENCE以n/2+h-2个智能体实现全覆盖加连通可见性，并用启发式降低实际开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知正交环境中在线部署移动自组网时，需同时保证覆盖与通信连通性，且无法预知环境结构，导致智能体数量与部署顺序的规划缺乏理论最优依据。

Method: 将问题形式化为部分可观测协作守卫画廊问题（Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Gallery Problem），对CADENCE算法给出完整证明，并评估可降低智能体数量与部署时间的部署顺序启发式。

Result: 证明了CADENCE算法在含n个角点、h个孔洞的正交世界中，使用至多n/2+h-2个智能体即可实现全覆盖并维持连通的可见性图；启发式进一步在实践中减少智能体数量与部署时间。

Conclusion: 该工作为受限通信的在线多智能体部署提供了带理论保证的算法与实用优化，可用于移动自组网的覆盖部署。

Abstract: We study the online deployment of mobile ad hoc networks in unknown orthogonal environments, formalized as the Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Gallery Problem. We give a full proof that CADENCE algorithms achieve full coverage while maintaining a connected visibility graph using at most $n/2 + h - 2$ agents in orthogonal worlds with $n$ corners and $h$ holes. We further evaluate deployment-order heuristics that reduce agent count and deployment time in practice.

</details>


### [281] [Splat-CBF: Safe Next-Best-View Control in 3D Gaussian-Splat Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23100)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Splat-CBF 将风险感知控制屏障函数（AVaR of Gaussian field）与基于 Fisher 信息增益的感知屏障函数统一到一个二次规划中，安全为硬约束、感知为带自适应松弛惩罚的软约束，在室内仿真、Isaac Kinova 机械臂与 Ackermann 实车上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在未知环境中导航需同时解决"看向哪里"（主动感知）与"如何移动"（避障），二者目标冲突：最值得观测的区域恰是地图最不确定、碰撞裕度最不可信的区域。现有方法多将感知与安全割裂处理，缺乏统一的硬安全保证与可自适应的感知激励机制。

Method: 提出 Splat-CBF：1) 风险感知 CBF，将高斯场（3DGS 式场景表示）的 Average Value-at-Risk 转化为单一光滑硬约束以表征不确定区域的安全风险；2) 第二个屏障函数奖励靠近规划路径、期望 Fisher 信息增益高的相机朝向；3) 二者在 QP 中求解，安全为硬约束、感知为软约束，松弛惩罚项根据"感知已放松的历史频率"与"机器人距不确定区域的接近程度"自适应调整。

Result: 在室内仿真、Isaac Kinova 机械臂及 Ackermann 驱动实车实验中，与仅安全（safety-only）和仅感知（perception-only）基线相比，机器人导航更快、采集信息更多、在线求解速度更快，且仅在安全要求下才放弃信息性运动。

Conclusion: 通过将风险感知与信息增益统一为 CBF-QP 框架下的硬/软约束对，可在不牺牲安全保证的前提下实现主动感知导航，且具有较高的在线计算效率。

Abstract: Where to look and how to move? A robot navigating an unmapped environment must do both at once, and the two goals pull against each other. The regions most worth observing are the ones the map knows least about, and those are exactly where the robot cannot trust its collision margins. We resolve this tension by introducing Splat-CBF, an active perception control barrier function that steers the camera toward the next best view while collision avoidance is enforced as a hard constraint. Safety is enforced by a risk-aware control barrier function that turns the Average Value-at-Risk of the Gaussian field into a single smooth hard constraint. Perception is enforced by a second barrier that rewards camera orientations with high expected Fisher information gain near the robot's planned path. The two meet in a quadratic program where safety is hard and perception is soft, with a slack penalty that adapts to how often perception has already been relaxed and how close the robot is to an uncertain region. We verify the method in indoor simulations, a Isaac Kinova manipulator and in experiments on an Ackermann-drive robot. Our results assert that robot navigates faster, gathers more information, and runs faster online than safety-only and perception-only baselines, giving up informative motion only when safety requires it.

</details>


### [282] [DiagGen: Agentic Generation of Deformable Assets with Sim-based Diagnostics for Robotic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23103)
*Guanxiong Chen,Yiduo Qu,Qianjun Xia,Pengyu Jing,Yixian Cheng,Bole Ma,Pengzhi Yang,Bingyang Zhou,Ziming Li,Shashwat Suri,Gongbo Sun,Chao Liu,Peter Yichen Chen,Ziqiu Zeng,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiagGen 提出一个智能体框架，通过"生成—仿真—诊断—修复"闭环，从单张野外图像生成可仿真的可形变资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可形变资产生成框架只关注生成本身，物理合理性通常在生成后才被评估，仿真响应未被用作上游错误的修复反馈，导致生成的资产未必可直接用于机器人操作仿真。

Method: DiagGen 构建部件级几何与材料参数，利用基于 VLM 的智能体选择语义信息丰富的区域，在物理仿真器中探测这些区域、观察材料响应，并将有证据支撑的修复线索回传到对应的生成阶段，形成 generate–simulate–diagnose–refine 循环。

Result: 在 40 个资产上的实验表明，诊断能提供有用的修复线索，并适度提升生成可形变资产的质量；与视觉基础模型生成的、可能无法仿真的资产不同，DiagGen 生成的资产可直接放入高保真物理仿真器，用于接触密集型抓取放置任务的规划与仿真。

Conclusion: 将仿真反馈引入生成闭环可提升可形变资产的物理可用性，为机器人操作提供可直接仿真的资产来源。

Abstract: While simulation-ready deformable assets are essential for in-silico robotic manipulation tasks, existing generation frameworks typically assess physical plausibility after generation, leaving an object's simulated response unused as feedback for repairing upstream errors. We present DiagGen, an agentic framework that turns a single in-the-wild image into a simulation-ready deformable asset through a generate--simulate--diagnose--refine loop. DiagGen constructs part-aware geometry and material parameters, then uses a VLM (vision-language model)-based agent to select semantically informative regions, probe them in a physics simulator, observe material responses, and route evidence-backed repair cues to the responsible generation stage. Experiments on 40 assets show that diagnostics provides useful repair cues and can moderately improve the quality of generated deformable assets. Finally, we show that unlike assets generated from visual foundation models which may not be simulatable, DiagGen-generated deformables can be directly dropped into a high-fidelity physical simulator for the planning and simulation of contact-rich pick-and-place tasks. The project's website is this https URL .

</details>


### [283] [Search, Ground, Plan: Functional Sufficiency for Task and Motion Planning under Incomplete Scene Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23113)
*Narendhiran Vijayakumar,Nav Singhal,Girish Varma,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRAB-TAMP：基于基础模型的任务与运动规划框架，通过功能角色与约束检索并验证场景实体，仅在功能充分性成立后规划，在32个场景变体上端到端成功率54.0%，较三种FM-based TAMP基线均值提升25.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言与视觉驱动的TAMP受限于不完整的场景知识，"任务需求理解"与"场景物理可实现性"之间存在鸿沟，容易在错误或缺失对象上进行规划。作者的动机是先确认场景是否具备完成任务所需的功能实体（功能充分性），再启动规划。

Method: 提出GRAB-TAMP：用功能角色、关系与分配约束表示任务；在需求未解析时增量式检索场景，对候选对象做语义、几何、关系三重校验；求解出完整的联合分配（功能充分性）后方进行任务与运动规划。

Result: 在Kitchen、Living Room、Workshop三个域共32个场景变体、200次可行试验中，端到端成功率54.0%，规划目标覆盖率67.3%；相同执行设置下相较三种FM-based TAMP框架均值端到端成功率提升25.7个百分点。提供实现与评测代码。

Conclusion: 显式的功能充分性校验与实体检索/接地可作为规划前置条件，有效缓解场景知识不完整带来的失败，提升FM-based TAMP在真实场景变体下的端到端可靠性。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have expanded task and motion planning (TAMP) to manipulation problems specified through language and visual observations. However, incomplete scene knowledge leaves a critical gap between understanding what the task requires and knowing whether the physical scene can actually realize it. We introduce GRAB-TAMP, an FM-based TAMP framework that searches for scene entities required for task completion, grounds functional roles to valid physical objects, and plans only after a complete joint assignment establishes functional sufficiency. We represent the task through functional roles, relations, and assignment constraints, and incrementally inspect the scene while requirements remain unresolved, verifying candidate objects through semantic, geometric, and relational checks. We evaluate GRAB-TAMP across 32 scene variants spanning Kitchen, Living Room, and Workshop domains. Across 200 feasible trials, our approach achieves 54.0% end-to-end success with 67.3% plan goal coverage. Compared with three FM-based TAMP frameworks under the same execution setting, GRAB-TAMP improves end-to-end success by 25.7 percentage points over the mean baseline. Implementation and evaluation code: this https URL

</details>


### [284] [Verti-WM: A Physics-Aided Exteroceptive World Model for Off-Road Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23118)
*Chenhui Pan,Tong Xu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Verti-WM：面向越野导航的物理辅助外感受世界模型，循环融合冻结 Transformer（刚性地形）与神经符号地形力学模型（可变形地形），用地图高程与语义观测条件化融合，支持六自由度 rollout 用于策略优化，无需仿真器访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野导航强化学习需要大量车辆-地形交互数据，高保真仿真采集成本高。世界模型可替代仿真器 roll-out，但越野世界模型必须以外感受地形信息为条件，仅靠本体感受不足；同时还需同时建模刚性与可变形地形，数据驱动与物理方法各有优势，如何互补是核心挑战。

Method: Verti-WM：物理辅助的外感受世界模型。循环融合两个模块——冻结的 Transformer 处理刚性地形，神经符号地形力学（terramechanics）模型处理可变形地形；在每个预测位姿处从给定地图查询高程与语义观测以条件化融合，实现六自由度 roll-out，供策略优化使用而无须进一步访问仿真器。

Result: 预测误差较数据驱动基线降低 34.6%，较物理基线降低 21.7%；完全在 Verti-WM 内训练的策略任务成功率相当，计算时间较高保真仿真器直接训练减少 23.6 倍。真实世界数据验证：可在学到的真实运动动力学内做策略优化，Verti-4-Wheeler 平台成功率达 80%，而直接 sim-to-real 迁移仅 40%。

Conclusion: 物理辅助的外感受世界模型可有效替代仿真器进行越野导航策略优化，兼具精度与效率，并具备真实世界可行性，显著优于直接 sim-to-real。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for off-road navigation requires extensive vehicle-terrain interaction data, which are costly to collect in high-fidelity simulation. World models offer a promising alternative by replacing simulator roll-outs during policy optimization. However, an off-road world model must condition state transitions on exteroceptive terrain information, which proprioception alone does not provide. This challenge is further amplified by the need to model both rigid and deformable terrain, where data-driven and physics-based approaches offer complementary strengths. We propose Verti-WM, a physics-aided exteroceptive world model that recurrently fuses a frozen Transformer for rigid terrain and a neuro-symbolic terramechanics model for deformable terrain. Elevation and semantic observations queried from a supplied map at each predicted pose condition fusion, enabling six-degree-of-freedom rollouts for policy optimization without further simulator access. Verti-WM reduces prediction error by 34.6% and 21.7% over data-driven and physics-based baselines, respectively. Policies trained entirely within Verti-WM achieve comparable task success rates while reducing computation time by 23.6X relative to direct training in the high-fidelity simulator. We further validate Verti-WM using real-world data, enabling policy optimization within learned real-world kinodynamics and achieving a 80% success rate on the Verti-4-Wheeler platform, compared with 40% for direct sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [285] [Selective Commitment for Language-Guided Object Retrieval under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23131)
*Wonhee Koh,Sushil Samuel Dinesh,Hansol Ko,Shinkyu Park,Eungjoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个闭环语言引导目标检索框架，用联合信念（目标身份/容器关系/存在性）驱动VLM观测更新、共形抓取判定与有限时域信念空间规划，决定继续观测、交互、抓取或弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测条件下按参照容器检索目标时，需在"继续收集证据/交互/执行抓取/主动弃权"之间做权衡；现有方法缺乏将这些决策统一协调的机制，容易盲目抓取或在不确定时误弃权。

Method: 1) 维护持久联合信念，由跟踪目标、未观测目标、目标缺失三类假设组成；2) 用视角条件化的类别式VLM观测更新信念；3) 以共形预测的抓取可用性与机器人可行性决定是否承诺抓取；4) 有限时域信念空间规划选择信息收集动作，形成闭环。

Result: 五个场景下19/25仿真回合成功，最佳任务适配基线为12/25，且是唯一在每个场景至少成功一次的策略；消融显示跨视角记忆在部分遮挡下提升成功率，但完整系统未稳定优于简化变体；真机实验展示闭环重观测与注入抓取失败后的自主恢复，注入视角失败则导致错误弃权。

Conclusion: 验证了在统一框架内协调证据收集与选择性抓取承诺的可行性；自评亦指出系统相对简化变体无一致优势、视角故障下会误弃权，是后续改进方向。

Abstract: Language-guided object retrieval under partial observability requires deciding whether to gather more evidence, interact with the scene, grasp a candidate, or abstain. We present a closed-loop framework that coordinates these decisions for retrieving a target specified in relation to a reference container. The framework maintains a persistent joint belief over target identity, container relation, and presence through tracked-object, unobserved-target, and target-absent hypotheses. View-conditioned categorical VLM observations update this belief; conformal grasp eligibility and robot feasibility govern commitment, while finite-horizon belief-space planning selects information-gathering actions. Across five different scenarios, our proposed method succeeds in 19/25 simulation episodes versus 12/25 for the best-performing task-adapted baseline and is the only evaluated policy to achieve at least one success in each scenario. Ablations show that cross-view memory improves success under partial occlusion, while the full system does not consistently outperform simplified variants. Real-robot trials demonstrate closed-loop re-observation and autonomous recovery from injected grasp failures, while injected viewpoint failures end in false defer. Experimental results demonstrate the feasibility of coordinating evidence gathering and selective grasp commitment within a unified framework for retrieval under partial observability.

</details>


### [286] [Tip Manipulation in Soft Everting Robots via Wall Retraction and Deployable Fingers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23132)
*Nelson Badillo Perez,Niccolo Pagliarani,Matteo Cianchetti,Robert D. Howe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于"壁面回撤"（wall retraction）的软外翻机器人（everting robot）尖端操纵与多工具部署策略：通过基座滚轮独立调节外壁膜流速、尾部卷轴调节内尾生长，解耦机器人长度与膜材料位置，使膜上器件可在近尖端处按需运输、暴露与重新定位；并集成超轻褶皱充气手指（TPU涂层尼龙+气密囊），实现低气压大弯曲与1.9 N阻塞力，完成多物体抓取、三维操作与堆叠、受限空间相机巡检与顺序投送。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软外翻机器人可沿狭长受限路径持续生长行进，但现有主动交互仅局限于固定在尖端（或近尖端）的单一工具，无法按需重新定位，且刚性工具与软体结构难以兼容。作者希望赋予此类机器人"可逆、可重定位的尖端操纵"能力与多工具部署能力。

Method: 1）壁面回撤策略：基座滚轮组件独立计量外壁膜流动，尾部卷轴（tail spool）调节内部尾段生长，协调驱动将"机器人长度"与"膜材料位置"解耦；2）膜集成超轻褶皱充气手指：TPU涂层尼龙制成，内置气密气囊，运输时柔软松弛，充气后高褶皱设计在约50 kPa下弯曲约100°，阻塞力达1.9 N；3）系统层面协调壁面/尾部驱动以完成运输-暴露-重定位流程。

Result: 实现21–550 g、16–200 mm 多种家用物体的自适应抓取；三维物体操作与堆叠；利用环境支撑的伸展（braced extension）；受限空间巡检中的远端相机俯仰扫视；以及受控的顺序载荷投送。手指在低压下获得大弯曲与最高1.9 N阻塞力。

Conclusion: 该工作使软生长机器人具备具身化、可逆的尖端操纵能力，适用于杂乱与曲折环境中的操作与检测任务，突破了此前"单一固定工具"的限制。

Abstract: Soft everting robots can traverse long, confined paths by continuously growing, yet active interaction remains limited to a single tool fixed at or near the tip, unable to be repositioned on demand and difficult to reconcile with the robot's soft body. We introduce a tip-manipulation and multi-tool deployment strategy for soft everting robots based on wall retraction, implemented with a base roller assembly that independently meters membrane flow in the outer wall while a tail spool regulates growth in the internal tail section. Coordinated wall and tail actuation decouples robot length from membrane-material position, enabling membrane-mounted devices to be transported, exposed, and repositioned at selected locations near the distal tip. We pair this capability with ultralight pleated inflatable fingers integrated into the membrane, fabricated from TPU-coated nylon with an internal airtight bladder. The fingers achieve large bending at low pressures (approximately 100 degrees at 50 kPa in high-pleat designs) and generate blocking forces up to 1.9 N, while remaining limp during transport. The system demonstrates adaptive grasping across diverse household objects (21 to 550 g; 16 to 200 mm), three-dimensional object manipulation and stacking, environmentally braced extension, distal camera panning for confined-space inspection, and controlled sequential payload delivery. These results enable embodied and reversible tip manipulation for soft growing robots in cluttered and tortuous environments.

</details>


### [287] [AquaCap: A Training-Free Underwater Embodied Agent with Code-as-Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23133)
*Xiaoshi Li,Yule Xu,Chunghiu Kong,Yizhou Zhou,Yang Liu,Hao Yang,Zihao Huang,Yunxiao Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaCap：免训练"代码即策略"双层智能体框架，用于水下自主导航与操作，含结构化感知、失败感知记忆与闭环重规划，仿真成功率66.43%，并在ROV上完成真实抓取与搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型依赖大规模交互数据，而水下数据采集成本高、样本稀缺，导致现有具身智能方法难以直接应用于水下场景。作者希望在不做任务特定训练、不更新参数的前提下实现水下自主导航与操作。

Method: 提出AquaCap，免训练代码即策略（Code-as-Policy）框架：双层智能体将任务指令与环境观测转化为条件感知的计划与可执行控制程序；结构化感知在退化水下条件下提供语义、几何与可靠性感知的观测；失败感知记忆对失败动作进行诊断，支撑闭环重规划与代码修订，实现无需训练或参数更新的在线自适应。

Result: 仿真环境成功率达66.43%；真实世界实验在ROV上实现自主抓取与物体搬运，包括对受水动力扰动而位移目标的操作。

Conclusion: 代码即策略+失败记忆的免训练范式可绕开水下交互数据瓶颈，在仿真与真实ROV上验证了水下具身导航与操作的可行性；但成功率仍偏低，泛化边界与感知鲁棒性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language-action models have stimulated growing interest in underwater embodied intelligence. However, their reliance on large-scale interaction data limits their applicability underwater, where data collection is costly and scarce. To address this challenge, we present AquaCap, a training-free Code-as-Policy framework for autonomous underwater navigation and manipulation. AquaCap employs a dual-layer agent that translates task instructions and environmental observations into condition-aware plans and executable control programs. Structured perception then provides the agent with semantic, geometric, and reliability-aware observations under degraded underwater conditions. A failure-aware memory diagnoses unsuccessful actions and supports closed-loop replanning and code revision. This design enables online adaptation without task-specific training or parameter updates. AquaCap achieves a 66.43% success rate in simulation. Real-world experiments further demonstrate autonomous grasping and object transport with an ROV, including the manipulation of targets displaced by hydrodynamic disturbances.

</details>


### [288] [Probabilistic Scene Graphs: Hierarchical Representation and Real-time System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23144)
*Waqas Ali,Michele Antonazzi,Timon Homberger,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Patric Jensfelt,Yixi Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出概率场景图PSG，用高斯层级图HGG参数化空间信念，在构图与精化全流程保持不确定性，六个数据集上实现传感器帧率、近恒定内存与SOTA物体精度及零样本图对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图缺乏显式不确定性信念表示，也未在建图和精化操作中传播不确定性；几何通常需先建独立度量地图再挑选，导致不确定性丢失、误差无法回溯到图结构层面。

Method: 将场景图推广为对可能图的后验分布，分解为离散图结构（实体、关系、语义属性）与连续空间状态，二者均维护不确定性；几何由节点直接承载，度量地图可由图导出。以高斯层级图实例化空间定位：每个物体基元用全协方差高斯、服从Normal-Inverse-Wishart信念，并在节点内递归参数化得到几何图。建图管线中，纯图式粗到细对齐通过比较节点信念配准观测，嵌套EM与因子图优化联合精化位姿、物体参数与内部几何。

Result: 在室内RGB-D、室外LiDAR及跨模态部署共六个数据集上，HGG以传感器帧率运行，内存近恒定，取得SOTA物体精度与零样本图对齐性能。

Conclusion: 该框架把不确定性作为一等公民贯穿场景图构建与精化，几何由节点携带并以信念形式递归细化，为机器人提供概率化、可导出的层次化3D表示，且满足实时与轻量内存约束。

Abstract: 3D scene graphs provide semantically rich and hierarchical representations for robot perception. However, existing systems do not maintain uncertainty as an explicit belief or propagate it through the operations that construct and refine the graph. We introduce Probabilistic Scene Graph (PSG), a generalization of the conventional scene graph that represents a posterior over possible graphs, factorized into a discrete graph structure of entities, relations, and semantic attributes, and continuous states that ground them spatially, with uncertainty maintained over both components. Geometry is carried directly by the nodes rather than selected from a separately constructed metric map, so a metric map, where needed, follows from the graph rather than preceding it. We instantiate PSG's probabilistic spatial grounding with hierarchical graphs of Gaussians (HGG): each object primitive is represented by a full-covariance Gaussian under a Normal-Inverse-Wishart belief, and the same parametrization applied recursively within a node yields a geometry graph that resolves its surface at finer resolution. We then build a mapping pipeline that preserves these beliefs throughout graph construction and refinement: a purely graph-based coarse-to-fine alignment registers observations by comparing node beliefs, while a nested Expectation-Maximization and factor-graph optimization jointly refines poses, object parameters, and internal geometry. Across six datasets spanning indoor RGB-D, outdoor LiDAR, and cross-modality deployment, HGG operates at sensor rate with near-constant memory and achieves state-of-the-art object accuracy and zero-shot graph alignment.

</details>


### [289] [Robot World Models Are Not Invariant to How the Actions Are Written](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23252)
*Ahmed Karim,Leon Chlon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 世界模型会隐性继承动作参数化方式（绝对关节目标 vs 相对增量），导致同一轨迹在另一种编码下表现崩塌；该缺陷无法用视觉不变性文献解释，修补需对目标/输出做平均并对尾部不一致施加惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略可用绝对关节目标或相对当前状态的增量两种动作参数化训练，这是机器人学习中的实际工程选择；以动作作为条件的世界模型会静默继承这一选择，但此前无人审计该继承的后果。视觉模型的不变性研究只关注裁剪、抖动、相机位姿，命令的参数化通道未被审查。

Method: 在三个机器人数据集、两种形态上训练潜动力学模型，将同一命令轨迹用另一种参数化书写后输入，测量检索退化、目标条件动作选择成功率，以及两种"同一未来"信念的余弦相似度；给出区分有效重参数化与有损摘要/传感器替换的判别检验（两编码在给定关节输入下互为可重构，R²=0.996）。修补方案比较目标平均（objective averaging）与输出平均：概率可由凹性保证输出平均安全，方向值预测不可用归一化均值，故采用目标平均并加不一致惩罚。

Result: 检索性能跨三数据集两形态退化2.6–13.4倍；目标条件动作选择由53%降至15%；PushT上同一未来的两种信念近正交（cos=0.067，最差-0.377），说明预测器并非平滑退化而是回答了不同问题。所提检验拒绝了四个候选轴中的三个，缺陷定位于动作通道。目标平均可单独恢复任务性能；最差一致度仅停留在0.78，加入不一致惩罚后升至0.995，并在潜空间 rollout 中决定最差情形是"侵蚀"还是"保持"；在PushT上仅靠平均无法修复该轴。

Conclusion: 动作参数化是世界模型中被忽视的隐式不变性缺陷，且不能在输出端廉价修复；审计动作通道并采用目标平均加不一致惩罚可显著收紧最差情形一致度，但对某些任务（PushT）仍不足，需要参数化层面的进一步设计。

Abstract: A robot policy is trained with one of two action parameterizations: absolute joint targets, or deltas relative to the current state. The choice is a live engineering decision in robot learning, and a world model conditioned on actions inherits it silently. We show the inheritance is catastrophic. A latent dynamics model trained on one parameterization and handed the identical commanded trajectory written in the other collapses: retrieval degrades by 2.6-13.4x across three robot datasets and two morphologies, goal-conditioned action selection falls from 53% to 15%, and on PushT the two beliefs about the same future are near-orthogonal (cos = 0.067, worst case -0.377), so the predictor does not degrade gracefully, it answers a different question. This is not a distribution-shift artifact in the usual sense: the two encodings are mutually reconstructible at R^2 = 0.996 given the joint input, so no information is lost, and we give the test that separates a valid re-parameterization from a lossy summary or a sensor swap. The test rejected three of the four axes we proposed. The defect lives in the action channel, which the invariance literature for visual models does not examine: work there concerns crops, jitter and camera pose, while the parameterization of the commands goes unaudited. The repair is averaging over the two encodings, and where it goes matters. Averaging the objective restores task performance by itself; averaging the outputs, safe for probabilities by concavity, is not available for direction-valued prediction, where the normalized mean can score below every member of the orbit. What objective-averaging leaves behind is the tail: worst-case agreement stays at 0.78, a disagreement penalty closes it to 0.995, and over a latent rollout it is the difference between a worst case that erodes and one that holds. On PushT, averaging alone does not repair the axis.

</details>


### [290] [Scenario MPC with STL Specifications and Pareto-Based Feasibility Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23263)
*Tianhao Wu,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种 MPC 框架，将 STL 约束的可行性修复建模为 Pareto 优化问题，并给出 STL 违反率的概率证书，用于包含随机不可控智能体的自动驾驶场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统的 STL 控制合成通常假设智能体协作且动力学已知，而自动驾驶等真实场景中存在随机、不可控智能体；现有鲁棒或概率方法要么只保证严格满足约束而不处理不可行恢复，要么以自车目标为导向松弛约束，缺乏对智能体间目标折衷的显式刻画与不确定性量化。

Method: 采用模型预测控制框架，将可行性修复（feasibility repair）视为 Pareto 优化问题，显式刻画各智能体目标之间的折衷；同时提出对 STL 违反率的概率证书，以形式化量化随机与不可控智能体带来的不确定性。

Result: 在两个自动驾驶场景上评估，框架能够恢复可行控制并表现出安全行为。

Conclusion: 将可行性修复作为 Pareto 优化并结合违反率概率证书，为随机不可控多智能体场景下的 STL 约束控制提供了一种兼顾可行性恢复与不确定量化的 MPC 方案。

Abstract: Temporal logic is a formal language for reasoning about system behaviors over time. Signal temporal logic (STL), in particular, has been used to encode spatio-temporal requirements for control synthesis in multi-agent systems, often under the assumption that agents are cooperative and their dynamics are known. However, real-world multi-agent applications, such as autonomous driving, typically involve stochastic and uncontrollable agents. Recent work explored robust control with worst-case or probabilistic formulations, but remains limited in that it either (1) certifies strict satisfaction of STL constraints without addressing feasibility recovery, or (2) relaxes infeasible constraints with ego-centric objectives. In this paper, we propose a model predictive control (MPC) framework that treats feasibility repair as a Pareto optimization problem to explicitly characterize tradeoffs among agent objectives. We further provide a probabilistic certificate on STL violation rate to formally quantify uncertainty under stochastic and uncontrollable agents. The proposed framework is evaluated on two autonomous driving scenarios. Results show that the framework recovers feasible control with demonstrated safe behaviors.

</details>


### [291] [Latent Telepathy: Multi-Robot Communication with Self-Supervised Perceptual Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23269)
*Howard Wang,Han Zheng,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Telepathy：机器人间直接广播自监督预训练并冻结的感知隐向量作为通信消息，接收方仅靠任务奖励学会使用；在内容受控协议下，隐向量使导航员规避遮挡危险达 99.7%，远超位置/轨迹消息，并指出 MARL 通信迁移到连续控制需保证决策可被探索到达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的去中心化多机器人团队中，决定某机器人下一步动作的关键事实往往只对队友可见。现有去中心化方法只传运动学信息（位置、规划轨迹），无法表达队友所感知的内容；MARL 学到的通信虽能携带感知信息，但消息与任务耦合、不可解释、难以迁移。作者希望找到一种零额外计算、语义统一且对接收方"不透明"的通信载体。

Method: 核心做法是把机器人自身感知流程中本已计算的隐向量直接当作消息广播出去。该隐向量来自以自监督联合嵌入预测目标（JEP）训练的编码器，训练后在策略学习前即冻结，并被全队共享。接收机器人只通过任务奖励学习如何在其上行动，不知道消息的语义。为验证，设计了内容受控协议：固定带宽、延迟、拓扑与接收方，仅改变消息内容，从而隔离"内容"这一变量。

Result: 广播感知隐向量使导航员在 99.7% 的回合中避开被遮挡危险，与无噪声手工设计消息相当。位置与轨迹消息维持在随机水平；原始相机图像宽度为隐向量的 186 倍，可靠性反而更低。结论从离散网格世界延伸到连续速度控制下的渲染像素；在真实机器人相机上，编码器在 102/102 次实时决策中成功解出危险。作者还识别出将 MARL 通信结果移植到连续控制的一个必要条件：消息所影响的决策必须保持可被探索到达，并给出恢复该条件的方法。

Conclusion: 把自监督、冻结、共享的感知隐向量用作去中心化通信消息，可在不增加计算与带宽的前提下实现高效、语义一致的队友间感知共享，且明显优于运动学消息与原始图像。该结果在网格世界、渲染连续控制和真实机器人相机上一致成立，并给出 MARL 通信迁移至连续控制的可探索性要求及补救办法。

Abstract: In a decentralized multi-robot team under partial observability, the fact that decides a robot's next action is often visible only to a teammate. Existing decentralized methods communicate kinematic information, such as position or planned trajectory, which cannot convey what the teammate perceives. Learned communication in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) can carry perceptual content, but the resulting messages are task-coupled and opaque. We propose Latent Telepathy. Each robot broadcasts the perceptual latent vector it already computes for its own use, the output of an encoder trained with a self-supervised joint-embedding predictive objective, frozen, and shared across the team. A teammate learns to act on it from task reward alone. Because the encoder already runs for perception, the message costs no additional computation and a single compact vector of bandwidth. Because the encoder is frozen before any policy is trained, the message means the same thing to every robot, and the receiving robot is never told what it means. We evaluate Latent Telepathy with a content-controlled protocol in which bandwidth, latency, topology and receiver are held fixed and only the message content varies. Broadcasting the latent lets a navigator avoid an occluded hazard in 99.7% of episodes, matching a noiseless hand-designed message. Position and trajectory messages remain at chance, and the raw camera image, 186 times wider, is less reliable than the compressed latent. The result holds from a discrete gridworld to rendered pixels under continuous velocity control, and the encoder decodes the hazard from a physical robot's camera in 102 of 102 live decisions. We also identify a requirement for porting MARL communication results to continuous control, that the decision a message informs must remain reachable by exploration, and show how to restore it.

</details>


### [292] [Steering Through Contact: A Finite-Support Motion Model for Single-Track Center-Articulated Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23271)
*Mohamed Dhia Ounally,Nicolas Samson,Mathis Turgeon-Roy,Veronica Vannini,Johann Laconte,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出有限支撑二次模型(FSQ),通过横向滑移的准静态平衡将接触点沿接触区域分布的转动阻力纳入单轨中心铰接车辆动力学,给出闭式解用于实时预测横向速度与偏航率,在沥青、草地、冰面及混合路面的7 km实测数据上将5 s预测的平移与偏航加权中位误差分别降低53.5%和68.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外铰接车辆的轨迹规划与控制依赖对推进/转向如何影响运动的预测,但滑移下接触点不再成立。经典点接触运动学模型(PCK)只考虑连杆几何,忽略沿接触区分布的转动阻力,导致滑移路面(草地、冰面)上的横向速度与偏航率预测偏差大。

Method: 面向单轨中心铰接车辆提出有限支撑二次模型(FSQ):以有限支撑接触替代点接触假设,将二次滑移代价精确归约为接触力矩,通过横向滑移准静态平衡建模转动阻力,并给出紧凑的闭式解用于实时预测横向速度与偏航率,可视为对PCK的泛化。

Result: 在沥青、草地、冰面及混合路线的真实实验中,使用超过7 km数据,5 s预测的加权中位平移误差降低53.5%、偏航误差降低68.9%(相对PCK)。

Conclusion: FSQ以极小的计算开销(闭式解)提升了滑移路面下铰接车辆的动力学预测精度,为野外机器人的轨迹规划与控制提供了更可靠的实时预测模型。

Abstract: Trajectory planning and control in field robotics rely on predicting how propulsion and steering affect vehicle motion when contact points undergo slip. For articulated vehicles, the point-contact kinematic model (PCK) accounts for the linkage geometry but neglects the rotational resistance distributed along the contacts. We propose a finite-support quadratic model (FSQ) for single-track, center-articulated vehicles that incorporates this resistance through a quasi-static balance of lateral slip. Our approach generalizes the standard PCK formulation by relaxing the contact-point assumption. An exact reduction of the quadratic slip cost to contact moments gives a compact closed-form solution for real-time prediction of lateral velocity and yaw rate. We evaluate the proposed method in real-world experiments across asphalt, grass, ice, and mixed routes, using more than 7 km of data. For five-second predictions, FSQ reduces the weighted median translation and yaw errors by 53.5% and 68.9%, respectively, relative to PCK.

</details>


### [293] [SCULPT-VLA: Learning Structured Control through Staged Action Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23275)
*Wenbo Li,Yiteng Chen,Wei Zhang,Wenhao Li,Jun Yang,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCULPT-VLA 通过分阶段动作接地，让 VLA 策略先用教师脚手架学习任务进度、场景动态与空间接地等结构化中间因子，再在撤除脚手架后以因子组合接地粗糙动作，最后恢复感知进行连续精调。在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv-WidowX 与 RoboTwin 2.0 Full 上平均成功率超过共享骨干基线，真机四任务分布偏移下平均成功率 58.1%（π0.5 为 45.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略越来越多地引入结构化中间监督，但"中间表示应编码什么"与"动作预测如何学会依赖它"是两个独立问题，后者常被忽视，导致结构监督与动作学习之间的依赖关系未被显式塑造。

Method: 提出 SCULPT-VLA：动作条件状态由任务进度、场景动态、空间接地三类互补因子构成。训练分阶段——先用教师脚手架形成各因子；再以其组合为输入接地粗糙动作，并逐步撤除脚手架；随后恢复直接感知访问，将学到的状态与感知细节结合做连续精调。课程设计将"学习以结构为条件预测动作"与"精调连续控制"解耦，部署时不需教师或离散动作自回归。

Result: 在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv-WidowX、RoboTwin 2.0 Full 上平均成功率高于共享骨干基线；SimplerEnv-WidowX 最终成功率 83.5%，而 Stage-II 直接访问视觉语言时为 71.3%；四个真机任务在测试分布偏移下平均成功率 58.1%，π0.5 为 45.6%。训练消融与逐因子干预支持分阶段设计，并显示恢复直接感知后所学状态仍持续贡献于控制。

Conclusion: 通过分阶段动作接地可显式塑造动作预测对结构化中间表示的依赖；所学结构化状态在恢复感知访问后仍对连续控制有贡献，且部署无需教师或离散动作自回归。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies increasingly incorporate structured intermediate supervision beyond action labels. Yet specifying what an intermediate representation should encode leaves open how action prediction learns to depend on it. We introduce \textbf{SCULPT-VLA}, a policy that learns structured control through staged action grounding. Its action-conditioning state comprises complementary factors for task progression, scene dynamics, and spatial grounding. Training first forms these factors with teacher scaffolds, then grounds coarse action prediction through their composition as scaffold inputs are withdrawn. Direct perceptual access is subsequently restored for continuous refinement, combining the learned state with perceptual detail. The curriculum separates learning to condition actions on structure from refining continuous control. Deployment requires neither teachers nor discrete-action autoregression. SCULPT-VLA achieves higher average success than shared-backbone baselines on LIBERO, SimplerEnv-WidowX, and RoboTwin 2.0 Full. On SimplerEnv-WidowX, final success is 83.5\%, versus 71.3\% when Stage-II action learning directly accesses vision and language. Across four physical robot tasks, average success under the tested distribution shifts reaches 58.1\%, compared with 45.6\% for $\pi_{0.5}$. Training ablations and factor-wise interventions support the staged design and show that the learned state continues to contribute to control after direct perceptual access is restored.

</details>


### [294] [Safety-Critical Control under Uncertainty via Adaptive Conformal Quantile Prediction Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23280)
*Hao Zhou,Yanze Zhang,Yiwei Lyu,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应不确定性量化安全控制框架，用ACP+CQR构建校准的状态依赖非对称预测区间，嵌入概率CBF并结合MPC实现高概率安全保证，降低保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键控制中的不确定性表征需同时满足统计有效性（可认证性能）与可强制执行的安全约束。现有方法要么预设特定分布以获得安全保证，要么给出对称、输入无关的预测区间，导致控制综合中的安全约束失配或过于保守。

Method: 以自适应保形预测（ACP）为基础，扩展保形分位数回归（CQR）以构造分布无关、非对称、状态依赖的预测区间，具备可认证的概率覆盖率；将所得不确定性集合集成到概率控制屏障函数（CBF）公式中，形成不确定性感知的安全约束，并嵌入模型预测控制（MPC）框架求解。

Result: 仿真与理论结果共同验证了方法的有效性，表明可在高概率下提供可证明的安全行为，同时相比对称/输入无关区间方案降低保守性。

Conclusion: 该工作将保形不确定性量化与CBF/MPC安全控制结合，为不确定性下的安全关键控制提供了兼顾统计有效性与低保守性的实用框架。

Abstract: Safety-critical control under uncertainty requires uncertainty representations that are both statistically valid (for certifiable performance) and compatible with enforceable safety constraints. However, existing methods often assume particular distributions of uncertainty for provable safety guarantees or establish symmetric and input-agnostic prediction intervals for robust safety, which can lead to misaligned or overly conservative safety constraints in control synthesis. In this paper, we introduce a novel safe control framework with adaptive uncertainty quantification that constructs calibrated and state-dependent prediction intervals to enable high-probability safety guarantees, while improving constrained control performance. The framework leverages adaptive conformal prediction (ACP) and extends it with conformal quantile regression (CQR) to capture distribution-free, asymmetric uncertainty intervals with certifiable probabilistic coverage, and integrates the resulting uncertainty sets into a probabilistic control barrier function formulation to enforce robust safety with reduced conservativeness. This yields uncertainty-aware safe control constraints that can be incorporated within a model predictive control(MPC) framework to provide provably safe behaviors with high probability. Simulation and theoretical results are provided to demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [295] [Shared Execution-Clock Drifting Policy for Dynamic Precision Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23305)
*Zhenchen Dong,Qingran Wu,Jinna Fu,Jiaming Wu,Fulin Chen,Hongyu Yu,Yide Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SECD 将执行节奏显式建模为可学习的"共享单调时钟"，使单步策略在保持固定频率控制接口与单次网络推理的同时，联合预测进度索引动作曲线与时钟映射，在四类真机时序任务上取得 77% 平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间约束下的操作（如拦截运动物体、在截止时间内完成调整序列）不仅要求动作准确，还要求执行节奏与演化场景匹配。单步策略虽降低生成开销，但直接预测动作序列会使时间分配隐式化，导致节拍不可控、难以对齐演示中的时序结构。

Method: 提出 Shared Execution-Clock Drifting（SECD）：以观测与隐变量采样为条件，策略联合预测（1）按进度索引的动作曲线，（2）将固定控制时刻映射到曲线位置的共享单调时钟。用演示数据导出的对齐作为该分解的监督锚点，并通过在解码动作上的 drifting 联合训练。推理时仅需一次网络前向，控制接口仍为固定频率。另设固定时钟变体作为对照。

Result: 在 NVIDIA Thor 上对四个真机任务推理，300 次试验平均成功率 77.00%，在每个任务上均优于所评估的单步基线；16 m/min 传送带取杯成功率 91%，90 s 内还原并折叠皱衬衫 54%；固定时钟变体在同一传送带协议上达 79%。RoboMimic 状态域实验（含 Transport、Square 的跨种子消融）进一步支持时序表征与演示对齐的联合设计。

Conclusion: 将执行节奏作为显式可学习变量嵌入单步动作生成，可在不牺牲推理效率的前提下提升时序敏感操作的表现；演示导出的对齐对时钟—曲线分解的训练是关键。

Abstract: Manipulation under time constraints requires both accurate actions and an execution rhythm that matches the evolving scene. This becomes critical when a robot must intercept moving objects or complete a sequence of adjustments before a deadline. Although one-step policies reduce generation cost, their directly predicted action sequences leave temporal allocation implicit. We propose Shared Execution-Clock Drifting (SECD), which makes execution rhythm an explicit part of one-step action generation. Conditioned on an observation and a latent sample, the policy jointly predicts a progress-indexed action curve and a shared monotone clock that maps fixed control times to locations on the curve. Demonstration-derived alignment anchors this decomposition, which is trained jointly through drifting on the decoded actions. The resulting policy retains a fixed-rate control interface and requires one network evaluation. We evaluate SECD across four real-robot tasks with inference on NVIDIA Thor. Across 300 trials, it achieves 77.00% task-averaged success and outperforms the evaluated one-step baselines on every task, including 91% success in cup retrieval from a 16 m/min conveyor and 54% in restoring and folding a crumpled shirt within 90 s. A fixed-clock variant reaches 79% on the same conveyor protocol. Complementary state-based RoboMimic experiments, including cross-seed ablations on Transport and Square, further support the joint design of the temporal representation and demonstration alignment. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [296] [Manipulation Feasible Navigation Among Movable Obstacles with Discrete Contact Pushing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23312)
*Shaohu Wang,Aiguo Song,Yulong Yuan,Zhongyu Sun,Tianyuan Miao,Qinjie Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层「可移动障碍物导航」(NAMO) 框架：高层从参考路径中识别关键阻挡物并搜索满足几何、操作与下游导航约束的搬移方案，必要时调用 LLM 推断辅助操作依赖并由确定性几何规划验证；底层定义箱形障碍物表面的离散接触模式，在线选择接触面与区域实现直推、侧推、角推，并用循环强化学习策略协调移动底盘与机械臂跟踪 TCP 目标，仿真与真机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在大型可移动障碍物的环境中，纯绕行导航效率低甚至不可行，而交互式导航需要同时推理「绕行 vs 搬移」的收益、可行放置位置与可执行的操作动作。现有工作多针对可抓取小物体或仅做避障，缺乏面向大型不可抓取障碍物的分层导航—操作统一框架。

Method: 分层框架：1) 高层规划器从参考路径提取关键阻挡障碍物，联合搜索同时满足几何、操作和下游导航约束的重定位方案；2) 当直接搬移被其他可移动物体阻挡时，选择性调用 LLM 推断辅助操作依赖（如先移开哪块障碍物），再由确定性几何规划器验证；3) 底层在箱形障碍物表面定义离散接触模式，依据位姿误差在线选择接触面与接触区域，通过接触切换实现直推、侧推与角推；4) 使用循环强化学习策略控制移动底盘与机械臂协同跟踪 TCP 目标，并在持续推动过程中保持末端可达性。

Result: 仿真与真机实验展示了绕行、单障碍物与多障碍物重定位、以及依赖关系约束场景下可行的导航—操作行为，验证了框架在大型不可抓取障碍物交互式导航中的有效性；项目已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作为大型不可抓取障碍物场景提供了「高层任务与依赖推理 + 低层接触式推动控制」的可执行 NAMO 框架，兼顾约束满足与操作可行性；未来可扩展至更复杂接触模式、非箱形物体以及 LLM 推理的鲁棒性与实时性优化。

Abstract: In environments with large movable obstacles, detour-only navigation can be inefficient or even infeasible, while obstacle interaction requires reasoning about navigation benefit, feasible placement, and executable manipulation. We present a hierarchical navigation among movable obstacles (NAMO) framework for mobile manipulators. At the high level, the planner identifies key blocking obstacles from reference paths and searches for relocation plans that jointly satisfy geometric, manipulation, and downstream navigation constraints. When direct relocation is hindered by other movable objects, a large language model (LLM) is selectively invoked to infer auxiliary manipulation dependencies, which are then verified by deterministic geometric planning. To execute the resulting relocation goals, we define discrete contact modes on the surfaces of box-shaped obstacles and select contact faces and regions online based on position and orientation errors, enabling straight, side, and corner pushing through contact switching. A recurrent reinforcement-learning policy coordinates the mobile base and manipulator to track tool center point (TCP) targets while preserving end-effector reachability during sustained pushing. Simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate feasible navigation-manipulation in detour, single- and multi-obstacle relocation, and dependency-constrained scenarios, validating the framework for interactive navigation with large non-graspable obstacles. The open-source project is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [297] [Towards Reliable Underwater Diver-Robot Interaction: Gesture Design, Interaction Logic, and Real-World Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23392)
*Yingqi Liu,Kanzhong Yao,Zimeng Peng,Yuanbo Bi,Anran Li,Zhe Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出闭环潜水员-机器人交互框架，包含七手势词汇表、轻量级关键点识别与命令级交互逻辑，并通过用户研究与水池实验验证可用性、可识别性与执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下人机交互要求手势既便于潜水员使用，又要能被机器人稳定识别；作者希望系统性考察"人类可用性—手势可识别性—执行可靠性"三者如何共同决定水下手势交互的成败。

Method: 构建闭环交互框架：紧凑的七手势词汇 + 基于关键点（landmark）的轻量识别模型 + 命令级交互逻辑（对逐帧识别结果做时序聚合与触发判定）。评估手段为潜水员用户研究（学习后手势复现一致性）与实验室水箱、游泳池中的水下机器人实验，并分析手势视觉相似度导致的混淆与手势形成过程中间姿态带来的时序歧义。

Result: 用户研究显示手势在简短学习后具备可复现性；识别分析表明视觉相似性与手势混淆相关，手势形成期的中间姿态引入时序歧义；命令级处理削弱了瞬时识别错误对机器人执行的影响，减少了误触发和任务中断。

Conclusion: 可靠的水下手势交互不能只靠提升识别精度，而需同时满足人类易用性、手势可区分性与执行层稳健性；命令级交互逻辑是缓解瞬时误识别、保障任务连贯性的关键设计。

Abstract: Underwater human--robot interaction requires gesture commands that are both easy for divers to use and reliable for robots to recognize. We investigate these aspects through a closed-loop diver--robot interaction framework integrating a compact seven-gesture vocabulary, lightweight landmark-based recognition, and command-level interaction logic. We evaluate the framework through a user study and underwater robot experiments in a laboratory tank and a swimming pool. The user study supported the reproducibility of the gestures after brief learning. Recognition analysis further showed that visual similarity was associated with gesture confusion, while intermediate poses during gesture formation introduced temporal ambiguity. Command-level processing mitigated the effects of transient recognition errors on robot execution, reducing unintended triggers and premature task interruptions. These findings show that reliable underwater gesture interaction depends on human usability, gesture recognizability, and execution reliability in underwater interaction.

</details>


### [298] [EmoPose: Vision-Language Model Guided Emotion-Aware Gesture Generation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23414)
*Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Fulong Ma,Wenjun Yue,Liang Zhang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmoPose：以VLM作为高层语义规划器，输出「手势计划」（类别+库变体+强度+语音锚点），由机器人侧确定性模块生成14-DoF轨迹并在Unitree G1实物上执行，支持中断与队列管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需通过手势与语音共同传达情感与意图，但开放式社会交互要求语义灵活，同时底层控制必须确定、可控且身体形态相关。直接让基础模型输出关节命令会破坏这一约束，因此需要一种可执行的语义接口来解耦「社会语义规划」与「体现感知的运动生成」。

Method: 框架分三层：（1）VLM接收语言、对话历史与可选视觉上下文，选择有序手势计划（交流类别、库中变体、强度、语音锚点）；（2）机器人自有可扩展运动库提供表达词汇与14-DoF关节目标，Pose Studio负责自动轨迹生成、MuJoCo预览及新条目与VLM引导的自动同步；（3）确定性机器人端模块负责计划校验、轨迹构建、手势调度、队列与中断管理。互动库可扩展而无需改动控制接口。

Result: 在EmoPose-Bench上，结构化GPT-5.5规划在Easy档达98.25±0.52%，整体76.50±0.54%，优于同模型直接打标签提示；对话上下文使用与有序多动作组合通过验证。系统完成名义MuJoCo测试集，并在实体Unitree G1上实现全部29个自编变体；四站实验室导览展示带中断的表达性讲述、相机接地对话与导航。

Conclusion: 通过「可执行语义接口」的分层设计，兼顾了社会交互的语义灵活性与确定性、身体感知的机器人控制，实现了实物人形机器人的情感手势表达与导览应用。

Abstract: Socially competent humanoid robots must communicate affect and intent through gesture as well as speech, yet open-ended interaction must become motion that is both expressive and executable on a specific body. This demands semantic flexibility for contextual social intent while preserving deterministic, embodiment-aware robot control. We present EmoPose, a vision-language model (VLM)-guided framework that bridges this gap through an executable semantic interface. Given language, dialogue history, and optional visual context, the VLM selects an ordered gesture plan containing a communicative class, library variant, intensity, and speech anchor. A scalable robot-owned motion library defines the available expressive vocabulary and the source of 14-DoF joint targets. Pose Studio supports automatic trajectory generation, MuJoCo preview, and automatic synchronization of new library entries with the VLM guide; deterministic robot-side modules validate plans, construct trajectories, schedule gestures, and manage queueing and interruption. This division lets the interaction repertoire grow for new social contexts without changing the control interface or delegating raw joint commands to the foundation model. On the EmoPose-Bench, structured GPT-5.5 planning reaches $98.25\pm0.52\%$ on the Easy tier and $76.50\pm0.54\%$ overall, exceeding same-model direct-label prompting. Further tests validate dialogue-context use and ordered multi-action composition. The system completes the nominal MuJoCo suite and realizes all 29 authored variants on the physical Unitree G1. A four-stop laboratory tour demonstrates expressive narration with interruption, camera-grounded dialogue, and navigation.

</details>


### [299] [HEARTH: An Object-Centric RGB-Thermal-3D Dataset for Temperature-Aware Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23418)
*Yuning Su,Borui Li,Yonghao Shi,Bofei Liu,Xing-Dong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HEARTH：首个以物体为中心、含真实测温的RGB-热成像-3D数据集（90个物体、18类、145个状态），并将热纹理绑定到重建网格用于仿真；基于LIBERO构造3个温度相关任务与1200条示范微调π0.5，热观测使温度依赖选物任务成功率从35.0%提升到75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的操作常依赖视觉外观无法揭示的物理属性，温度即为典型一例；但现有机器人学习数据集极少将实测温度与物体外观、几何关联，导致VLA难以执行温度相关指令。

Method: 构建物体级RGB-热成像-3D数据采集与配准流程：相机标定+位姿迁移，将表观表面温度映射到重建网格；数据包含原始温度测量、相机参数、RGB纹理网格与热纹理（可供仿真）。据此基于LIBERO派生3个温度依赖任务，采集1200条示范微调预训练VLA模型π0.5，并进行热观测消融。

Result: 消融实验显示，加入热观测后温度依赖的物体选择任务成功率由RGB-only基线的35.0%提升至75.0%。

Conclusion: HEARTH验证了显式温度感知数据对训练遵循温度相关指令的机器人策略的有效性，为多模态物理属性感知的VLA数据与仿真资产提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Language-guided manipulation can depend on physical properties that visible appearance does not reveal. Temperature is one such property, but object datasets for robot learning rarely associate measured temperatures with object appearance and geometry. We present HEARTH, an object-centric RGB-thermal-3D dataset of 90 physical objects from 18 everyday categories, comprising 145 captured object states. Our pipeline maps apparent surface temperatures onto reconstructed meshes through camera calibration and pose transfer. The dataset includes raw temperature measurements, camera parameters, RGB-textured meshes, and thermal textures for simulation. We use these assets to construct three LIBERO-derived tasks and collect 1,200 demonstrations for fine-tuning a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model, $\pi_{0.5}$. In an ablation study, adding thermal observations to the VLA increases success on temperature-dependent object-selection tasks from 35.0% for the RGB-only baseline to 75.0%. These results demonstrate the utility of HEARTH for training robot policies to follow temperature-related instructions.

</details>


### [300] [RiverVLN: Phase-Grounded Temporal Vision--Language Navigation for Unmanned Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23423)
*Jieling Wu,Yuehao Huang,Jiajun Lv,Tao Huang,Yong Liu,Weiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RiverVLN 基准与 PGT-NAV 框架，用"语义阶段"表示长程河道指令并结合视觉-运动证据在线跟踪，预测 SE(2) 位姿增量，实现 USV 连续运动下的闭环导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内/地面 VLN 假设语言是静态目标、动作离散且近瞬时，在无人水面艇（USV）上失效：河流导航要求惯性下的连续运动与有限机动性，长程指令需依靠稀疏且视觉歧义大的水上地标执行。为此需要专门的基准与方法。

Method: 1) RiverVLN：面向连续河道运动、长程 USV VLN 的基准；2) PGT-NAV：阶段锚定（phase-grounded）时序导航框架。将整条指令转换为有序、可视觉验证的语义阶段序列，通过落地的视觉与运动证据在线维持当前激活阶段；将该显式语义进度状态与视觉-运动历史及阶段专属 grounding 融合，预测 6 个局部 SE(2) 位姿增量；在 predict-execute-re-observe 闭环中执行，朝 W3 前进并更新阶段与 grounding，经基于地图的安全层重规划。

Result: 相比 GNM 风格与 ViNT 风格基线，PGT-NAV 显著降低递归位置与航向漂移；在 Unity-ROS 闭环导航中平均成功率 0.79。未见过的开桥（bridge-opening）试验与真实世界 USV 实验表明阶段锚定表示可从受控评测迁移到物理部署。

Conclusion: 显式语义阶段进度 + 视觉/运动 grounding 的时序导航范式，能有效缓解长程连续运动下的漂移，并具备从仿真到真实 USV 的迁移能力，为水上具身导航提供了基准与方法参考。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) has largely been developed for indoor and terrestrial robots, where language can often be treated as a static goal and motion is approximated by discrete or near-instantaneous actions. These assumptions break down for unmanned surface vehicles (USVs): river navigation requires continuous motion under inertia and limited maneuverability, while long-horizon instructions must be executed through sparse and visually ambiguous maritime landmarks. We introduce RiverVLN, to our knowledge the first benchmark designed for long-horizon USV VLN under continuous riverine motion, and PGT-NAV, a phase-grounded temporal navigation framework for USVs. Rather than directly mapping an entire instruction to motion, PGT-NAV converts it into an ordered sequence of visually verifiable semantic phases and maintains the active phase online through grounded visual and motion evidence. This explicit semantic progress state is fused with visual-motion history and phase-specific grounding to predict six local SE(2) pose increments. The resulting trajectory is executed in a predict-execute-re-observe loop, where the vessel executes toward W3, updates phase and grounding, and replans through a map-based safety layer. Experiments show that PGT-NAV substantially reduces recursive position and heading drift relative to GNM-style and ViNT-style baselines and achieves an average success rate of 0.79 in Unity-ROS closed-loop navigation. Unseen bridge-opening trials and real-world USV experiments further demonstrate that the phase-grounded representation transfers from controlled evaluation to physical USV deployment.

</details>


### [301] [RopeFormer: Cross-Trial Adaptation from Interaction History for Dynamic Rope Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23432)
*Menglin Wu,Kaixiang Yao,Shangbo Luan,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RopeFormer：以历史交互为上下文的条件策略，无需在线估计绳索参数，即可稳定提升动态绳索操作（单臂/双臂旋转、瞬态挥鞭）的后续控制性能，并在 Unitree H1-2 实机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态绳索操作对未知物体动力学高度敏感：相同机器人运动在不同绳索上会引起差异显著的响应，而显式辨识绳索物理属性（质量、刚度、摩擦等）又很困难。因此需要一种不依赖显式参数估计、能利用先验交互经验来适应绳索差异的控制框架。

Method: 提出 RopeFormer，一个历史条件化（history-conditioned）框架：策略在权重冻结（不更新参数）的前提下，保留跨 trial 的动作—响应历史作为上下文，并以此条件化后续控制；不做在线绳索参数估计。在仿真中进行匹配对照评估（单臂持续旋转、双臂旋转、瞬态挥鞭），对比“保留上下文”与“重置同一 checkpoint”；随后将冻结策略部署到 Unitree H1-2，操作此前未见过的真实绳索。

Result: 匹配仿真实验中，保留上下文相对重置同一 checkpoint 一致改善后续控制，收益随绳索动力学与观测设置变化。真机从 T1 到 T3：Rope Swing 目标获取时间下降 30.9%，Rope Twirl 下降 33.9%；Rope Whip 平均目标命中数从 0.2 提升至 2.3（满分 3）。

Conclusion: 先验交互历史可为动态可变形物体操作提供有效的控制上下文，且无需在线参数辨识；冻结策略配合上下文保留即可在未见真实绳索上持续改善性能。

Abstract: Dynamic rope manipulation is highly sensitive to unknown object dynamics: the same robot motion can produce substantially different responses across ropes, while explicitly identifying the relevant physical properties is difficult. We present RopeFormer, a history-conditioned framework that uses prior task interaction as context for subsequent control. The policy retains cross-trial action-response history while keeping its weights fixed and requires no explicit online rope-parameter estimation. In matched simulation evaluations across sustained single-arm rotation, bimanual rotation, and transient whipping, retaining context improves subsequent control relative to resetting the same checkpoint, with the benefit varying across rope dynamics and observation settings. We further deploy the frozen policies on a Unitree H1-2 with previously unseen physical ropes. From T1 to T3, target-acquisition time decreases by 30.9% for Rope Swing and 33.9% for Rope Twirl, while mean Rope Whip target hits increase from 0.2 to 2.3 out of three. These results show that prior interaction can provide effective control context for dynamic deformable-object manipulation. Robot videos, code, and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [302] [Receding-Horizon Pushing with Composable Object-Centric Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23439)
*Zhiyi Yuan,Tianrun Hu,Anxing Xiao,Yuhong Deng,David Hsu,Hanbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层反馈引导的推挤框架：低层学习策略从归一化点云预测接触动作并用准静态稳定性评分筛选，高层BIT*搜索物体路径并借助接触预测与运动规划验证子目标可行性，失败反馈修正代价并重规划，实现任意形状3D物体长时程非抓握推挤，含真机实验与零样本sim-to-real。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大、重或几何上不可抓取物体的移动作业依赖非抓握推挤，但任意形状3D物体的长时程推挤耦合三个难题：推点选择（如何逼近目标位姿）、单次推的稳定性与可达性、以及后续动作的可持续可行性。现有方法多只解决其中局部问题，缺乏将单步接触预测与全局可行性反馈闭环的统一框架。

Method: 分层框架。低层：学习型策略以位姿与尺度归一化的点云为输入，在近单步子目标条件下预测接触动作；并用准静态"滑动vs倾倒"分析给出稳定性评分对候选接触筛选。高层：BIT*先搜索物体路径，随后若干子目标经接触预测与机器人运动规划做未来可行性检查；运动规划失败作为反馈修改局部路径代价并触发重规划。执行时只落实第一个可行动作，形成滚动闭环。

Result: 仿真中在6个不同场景、22个物体上评测，并做多组消融，性能明显优于基线，可在多种情形下可靠完成长时程推挤。真机方面用Franka机械臂做定量实验，并在移动操作平台上对大而重物体做定性演示，实现直接的零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 将接触动作学习、准静态稳定性判别与基于搜索的可行路径规划通过失败反馈耦合，可在不需真机数据的情况下泛化到真实大重物体推挤；分层加滚动执行是长时程非抓握操作的有效范式。

Abstract: Non-prehensile manipulation is practical for relocating large, heavy, or geometrically ungraspable objects. Yet, long-horizon pushing of arbitrarily-shaped 3D objects couples three problems: 1) where to push the object so as to approach the target pose, 2) whether each push is stable and reachable, 3) whether subsequent actions remain feasible. We present an object-centric pushing policy within a feedback-guided hierarchical framework. At the low level, a learning-based policy predicts contact actions from a pose- and scale-normalized point cloud, conditioned on a near single-step subgoal. A stability score is applied to evaluate the predicted contacts by a quasi-static sliding-versus-tipping analysis. At the high level, BIT$^*$ first searches for an object path, and the next several subgoals are checked by contact prediction and robot motion planning for future feasibility. Failed motion plans, as feedback, change the local path costs and trigger re-planning. During execution, only the first feasible action is executed. In simulation, we evaluate 22 objects in six different scenes, upon which we also conduct comprehensive ablation studies. Results demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines with a clear margin and can reliably achieve long-horizon object pushing tasks under different situations. We also report quantitative real-robot experiments with a Franka arm and qualitative demonstrations with a mobile manipulator for large and heavy objects, with directly zero-shot sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [303] [BiRoAD: Learning Shared and Role-Adaptive Representations for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23445)
*Yan Shen,Yuchen Liu,Feng Jiang,Hangtian Hu,Xiaoqi Li,Shu Chen,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiRoAD 将双臂策略特征分解为“交换对称/反对称”两个分量，分别刻画与左右臂无关的协调结构和随功能角色变化的角色特异性表征，再以残差形式重组成回原双臂表征，作为即插即用特征变换提升角色分配不均衡下的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作需要两臂协调并随场景几何、物体配置和任务上下文自适应地交换功能角色；但演示数据中角色分布不均，导致欠代表的手臂-角色组合泛化差；且多数策略在固定的左/右臂动作空间中预测，未显式建模角色互换时行为应如何变换。

Method: 给定双臂轨迹或动作 token 特征，将其分解为交换对称分量（对臂交换不变的协调结构）与交换反对称分量（随功能角色分配一致变化的角色特异区分）；两者重组为对原始成对臂表征的残差更新。模块化设计，不改变策略输入、模仿学习目标，也无需人工角色标签。

Result: 在多个角色分布均衡与不均衡的双臂操作任务上，BiRoAD 相较对应基座策略提升了跨角色配置的鲁棒性，在欠代表的角色配置上增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 显式的对称/反对称角色分解可在不增加监督与接口改动的条件下，缓解双臂策略中的角色分布偏置，为双臂角色自适应提供了一种通用、可插拔的表征变换思路。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation requires policies that coordinate two arms while adapting their functional roles to scene geometry, object configuration, and task context. Learning such scene-conditioned role adaptation remains challenging, as demonstrations may contain uneven role distributions that limit generalization to underrepresented arm--role configurations. In addition, many bimanual policies predict actions in fixed left- and right-arm action spaces. While this provides a natural parameterization for robot control, it does not explicitly specify how behaviors should transform when functional roles are exchanged across arms. Across different scene initializations, the two arms may follow a similar coordination pattern, but the role-specific behavior assigned to each arm should change with the scene. Therefore, we propose BiRoAD, a Bimanual Role-Adaptive Decomposition framework for learning shared and role-adaptive representations in bimanual policies. Given bimanual trajectory or action-token features, BiRoAD decomposes these features into swap--symmetric and swap--antisymmetric components: the former captures coordination structure invariant to arm exchange, and the latter captures role-specific distinctions that vary consistently with functional role assignment. The two components are then recomposed as residual updates to the original paired arm representations, allowing BiRoAD to serve as a modular feature transformation without changing the policy inputs, imitation-learning objective, or requiring manually defined role labels. Across multiple bimanual manipulation tasks with balanced and imbalanced role distributions, BiRoAD improves robustness across role configurations over corresponding base policies, with notable gains on underrepresented role configurations.

</details>


### [304] [Smartphone GNSS Booster: Centimeter-Level Pedestrian Positioning Using a Portable Signal Re-Radiator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23456)
*Taro Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种贴附于智能手机的外部GNSS信号重辐射系统，将紧凑有源螺旋天线与无源贴片天线直连，在不更换手机内置接收机与天线的条件下提升观测质量，实现行人厘米级定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机GNSS定位精度长期停留在米级，难以满足人行道级行人导航与精准位置服务需求。瓶颈主要来自手机内紧凑线极化天线：观测噪声大、载波相位跟踪不稳定。

Method: 设计外置GNSS信号重辐射（booster）系统，直接将紧凑有源螺旋天线与薄型无源贴片天线相连进行重辐射，并贴合于手机。机理上通过提高接收信号强度降低热噪声、稳定信号跟踪；利用圆极化特性提升多径鲁棒性；稳定天线相位中心变化。

Result: 静态实验显示载波相位观测质量较标准手机定位显著提升；行人动态实验中实现稳定的载波相位整周模糊度固定，获得厘米级定位结果。

Conclusion: 外置信号重辐射可在沿用手机内置接收机与天线的前提下突破手机GNSS观测质量瓶颈，为行人高精度导航提供低成本可行方案。

Abstract: High-precision positioning using the global navigation satellite system (GNSS) embedded in smartphones is demanded for sidewalk-level pedestrian navigation and pinpoint location-based applications. However, current smartphone positioning accuracy for pedestrians remains at the meter level. This is mainly because limitations of the compact linearly polarized (LP) antenna in smartphones increase GNSS observation noise and hinder stable carrier phase tracking. In this paper, we propose an external GNSS signal re-radiation system that boosts smartphone GNSS observation quality while still using the smartphone's built-in GNSS receiver and antenna. The system directly connects a compact active helical antenna and a thin passive patch antenna for re-radiation, and is designed to be attached directly to the smartphone. The proposed GNSS booster achieves (1) reduced thermal noise and stable signal tracking via increased received signal strength, (2) improved multipath robustness compared with a LP antenna, and (3) stabilization of antenna phase center variation. Static experiments confirm a substantial improvement in the observation quality of GNSS carrier phase measurements compared with standard smartphone positioning. Furthermore, in pedestrian experiments, the proposed booster enabled stable carrier phase integer ambiguity resolutions, achieving centimeter-level positioning using a smartphone.

</details>


### [305] [STRIDER: Stepping-Enabled Multi-Gait Hierarchical 3D Loco-Manipulation Framework for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23483)
*Yuanzhuo Li,Wen Zhao,Zhe Yong,Xiang Meng,Gang Han,Hengle Ren,Xiaoyang Zheng,Zhen Wang,Yijie Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 STRIDER 分层多步态人形 loco-manipulation 框架，结合地形感知 3D 落脚、AMP 自然行走与笛卡尔上身控制，并提出 LD-PPO 蒸馏算法以实现异构专家到统一学生策略的技能迁移，在 TianGong Omni 上验证落脚与末端跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续速度指令控制无法精确调节落脚点，专用落脚跟踪模块难以与全身操作集成；同时标准动作式模仿蒸馏仅迁移动作，未显式构建异构技能的共享表征。目标是统一行走与踩踏专家，实现精确落脚与全身操作。

Method: STRIDER 分层框架：地形感知 3D 踩踏专家在支撑脚坐标系中选取可行落脚点并生成考虑离地高度的摆动轨迹；AMP 提供自然行走；上身采用笛卡尔控制。提出 LD-PPO 蒸馏算法，通过教师条件潜变量对齐，联合优化在线强化学习、DAgger 动作重建与潜变量对齐，将行走与踩踏专家融合为单一可执行学生策略。

Result: 仿真与真机实验（TianGong Omni 人形）表明，LD-PPO 在落脚跟踪与姿态跟踪精度上优于 vanilla distillation-PPO；硬件部署实现多步态 loco-manipulation，落脚与末端执行器跟踪准确。

Conclusion: 教师条件潜变量对齐的蒸馏可实现异构步态专家的统一学生策略，提升落脚与姿态跟踪精度，为人形多步态 loco-manipulation 提供可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation faces two prominent limitations: controllers using continuous velocity commands cannot precisely regulate individual footholds, while specialized foothold-tracking modules are difficult to integrate with whole-body manipulation. Furthermore, standard action-based imitation distillation primarily transfers expert actions, without explicitly encouraging a shared representation of heterogeneous skills. This paper introduces STRIDER, a hierarchical multi-gait framework to bridge these gaps. The framework integrates terrain-aware 3D stepping logic, Adversarial Motion Priors (AMP)-based natural walking, and Cartesian upper-body control: its stepping expert selects feasible footholds in the stance-foot frame and generates clearance-aware swing trajectories. To fuse distinct walking and stepping experts into one executable student policy, we propose Latent Distillation Proximal Policy Optimization (LD-PPO), a distillation algorithm augmented with teacher-conditioned latent alignment. By jointly optimizing on-policy reinforcement learning, DAgger-based action reconstruction, and latent alignment, LD-PPO transfers expert actions while encouraging a shared skill representation across heterogeneous modes. Simulation and real-robot evaluations on the TianGong Omni humanoid show that LD-PPO outperforms vanilla distillation-PPO in foothold-tracking and posture-tracking accuracy. Deployed on hardware, STRIDER realizes multi-gait loco-manipulation with accurate foothold and end-effector tracking.

</details>


### [306] [Cognitive Action Reasoning for Proactive Robots from Human-Centered Multimodal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23486)
*Zhihao Gu,Kechao Zhu,Yuanfeng Wu,Mohan Liu,Ankit Kumar Shaw,ChenDong Hong,Xuanyu Chen,Dengchen Mei,Xu Tianyi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ProRobo问题（无显式指令下基于多模态人类线索推理主动高层动作）与ProAction真实世界多模态数据集（10K样本、12场景、5类场景，含视觉/音频/文本），用两阶段human-in-the-loop标注构建认知层面监督，并给出参考模型MMC2Act；实验表明通用MLLM主动推理能力弱，而基于ProAction训练显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习数据集多将观测与任务指令或低层动作配对，机器人被设计为执行显式指令；虽有主动式具身辅助的探索，但已有资源面向的场景与动作层级不同，真实人类中心的多模态决策（无需显式指令判断该做什么）仍缺乏系统性定义与数据支撑。

Method: 1) 形式化ProRobo任务：机器人须依据多模态人类与环境线索，在无显式动作指令下决定采取何种动作；2) 构建ProAction数据集：10K真实世界样本，涵盖视觉观测、音频信号与文本输入，覆盖5种常见场景下12个日常生活情境；3) 设计两阶段human-in-the-loop标注流程：appraisal-guided候选动作生成 + 基于Affective Theory-of-Mind的人工精修，将人类状态、紧迫性、可行性与潜在风险的上下文判断纳入动作标注；4) 提出参考模型MMC2Act，隐式学习多模态观测到认知层面高层动作的映射；5) 在模态设置、主体不相交泛化、跨数据集迁移与人工评估上进行基准测试。

Result: 通用MLLM在从多模态线索主动推理高层动作上表现不佳；基于ProAction训练可显著提升性能。在多种模态设置、subject-disjoint泛化、跨数据集迁移及人工评估中均验证了该结论。

Conclusion: 主动机器人动作推理是一个尚未被充分研究的认知决策问题，真实多模态数据与认知基础的高层动作监督是关键；现有通用多模态大模型难以胜任，针对性训练可有效改善，为人类中心环境的主动具身决策提供数据与基准基础。

Abstract: Robots operating in human-centered environments are typically designed to execute explicit instructions, and most robot-learning datasets likewise pair observations with task instructions or low-level actions. Although recent work has begun to explore proactive embodied assistance, existing resources target different settings and action levels, leaving real-world human-centered multimodal decision-making underexplored. We formulate this problem as \textit{Proactive Robot Action Reasoning} (\textit{ProRobo}), an upstream cognitive decision problem in which a robot must determine which action to take based on multimodal human and environmental cues without explicit action instructions. To support ProRobo, we introduce \textit{ProAction}, a real-world multimodal dataset containing 10K samples of visual observations, audio signals, and text inputs across 12 daily-life scenarios in five common scenes. To construct cognitively grounded high-level action supervision, we develop a two-stage human-in-the-loop pipeline that combines appraisal-guided candidate generation with Affective Theory-of-Mind-guided human refinement, explicitly incorporating contextual judgment about human states, urgency, feasibility, and potential risk into action annotation. Based on this supervision, we benchmark representative Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and introduce \textit{MMC2Act}, a reference model that implicitly learns the mapping from multimodal observations to cognitively grounded high-level actions. Experiments across modality settings, subject-disjoint generalization, cross-dataset transfer, and human evaluation show that general-purpose MLLMs struggle with proactively reasoning high-level actions from multimodal cues, whereas training on \textit{ProAction} substantially improves performance.

</details>


### [307] [Design and Control of a Cable-Driven Switchable Actuator with Torque/Tension Dual Modes for Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23487)
*YuanLong Ji,Xu Liu,Xinyuan Cai,Qihan Ye,Xiangyu Xie,Ruizhe Jiang,Shuhan Xiang,Wenjing Liu,Qijun Wang,Yang Chen,Xingbang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种缆驱动可切换执行器（CDSA），可在关节力矩与线性拉力两种模式间快速切换，并借助近端传感融合与ILC双模力控实现对末端输出力的估计与控制，经平台及人体穿戴实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴外骨骼执行器通常只有单一机械输出模态（关节力矩或线性牵引），难以适应多样化训练场景，且传感/驱动部件分散带来远端仪器化负担。

Method: 设计CDSA缆驱动可切换执行器，将传感与驱动集中于近端驱动单元；采用耦合动滑轮机构（CMPM）实现远端拉力放大，双向缆驱动棘轮机构（CDRM）实现模式切换与预紧调节；用多源近端传感加数据驱动融合模型估计远端输出力；并提出基于迭代学习控制（ILC）的自适应双模力控策略。

Result: 平台实验测得力矩模式与拉力模式传动效率分别为(92.4±2.0)%与(96.5±3.3)%，拉力放大比2.77±0.10；模拟膝关节步态轨迹与短行程拉力曲线跟踪RMSE分别为未控峰值的(4.52±0.51)%与(3.15±0.19)%；坐姿人机耦合实验验证了两种模式下可控力输出。

Conclusion: 该可切换缆驱动架构以近端集中式传感/驱动+双模力控，实现了单套硬件对关节力矩与线性牵引两类外骨骼训练模式的支持，具备实用可行性。

Abstract: Existing wearable exoskeleton architectures are typically constrained by a single mechanical output modality, providing either joint torque around an anatomical joint or linear traction along a limb-training-oriented direction, which limits adaptability to diverse training scenarios. This letter presents a cable-driven switchable actuator (CDSA) that can rapidly switch between torque and tension modes while centralizing all sensing and actuation components at the proximal drive unit. A Coupled Movable Pulley Mechanism (CMPM) provides tension amplification at the distal end-effector, while a bidirectional Cable-Driven Ratchet Mechanism (CDRM) enables mode switching and preload regulation. To eliminate the need for distal instrumentation, multi-source proximal sensors are integrated with a data-driven fusion model to estimate distal output forces. An adaptive dual-mode force control strategy based on iterative learning control (ILC) is further developed. Platform experiments demonstrate transmission efficiencies of $(92.4 \pm 2.0)\%$ and $(96.5 \pm 3.3)\%$ in the torque and tension modes, respectively, along with a tension amplification ratio of $2.77 \pm 0.10$ under tension mode. Tracking tests on simulated knee-joint gait trajectories and short-stroke tension profiles yield stable control, with RMSEs of $(4.52 \pm 0.51)\%$ and $(3.15 \pm 0.19)\%$ of the uncontrolled peak value, respectively. Finally, seated human-coupled experiments validate the system's controllable force generation in both joint-torque and linear-traction application modes.

</details>


### [308] [FeasibleFlow: One-Step Joint Transport of Configuration Feasibility and Trajectories for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23488)
*Xiang Li,Bikun Wang,Qing Xu,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FeasibleFlow——一步式端到端生成框架，将配置空间可行性场与多模态自车轨迹联合测度传输；用非对称联合 MeanFlow 把场演化注入轨迹生成，并以 ARR 排序器与 Pareto-ReinFlow 平衡安全与进度，在 NAVSIM 上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶把当前观测直接映射为未来轨迹，但轨迹必须随场景演化保持可行。现有未来状态建模中的通用表示含大量与自车规划无关的信息，且仅通过辅助监督、静态条件或候选评估影响轨迹生成，造成时间错位未真正解决。
