Result: 导航策略对全部输入模态均敏感，不依赖单一模态；智能体编码了导航进度并保留VLM骨干的语义结构，支持概念级激活操控；抽象行为激活向量可零样本迁移至分布外真实场景，在无额外微调下提升性能。

Conclusion: VLN策略是多模态依赖的，其内部表征具有可解释的进度与语义结构，使得概念级与行为级激活操控成为可能，为可解释、可迁移的具身导航提供新途径。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) models rely mostly on pre-trained large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to predict navigation actions. While this fusion of language instructions and visual observations allows multimodal reasoning, it obscures how information is routed across modalities or what mechanisms drive navigation decisions. Thus, it remains unclear whether VLN models ground their predictions in relevant semantic cues or can track task progress. In this work, we study the interpretability and steerability of VLN models. We use intervention-based metrics that measure how visual observations, instructions, and visual memory causally influence navigation decisions. Our results show that these navigation policies are sensitive to all input modalities and do not depend on a single one. We further show that these agents encode navigation progress and retain semantic structure from their VLM backbones, enabling concept-level steering through internal activations. Finally, we extract activation vectors for abstract behaviors to transfer them zero-shot to out-of-distribution real-world scenarios, improving performance without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [379] [Learning tactile perception from high-bandwidth single-point sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24621)
*Joseph Rigal,Emmanuel Virot,Caroline Pascal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SpectRobot框架，将单点高带宽触觉信号转换为时频谱图，用视觉编码器处理，从而在无空间阵列的情况下利用接触动力学完成视觉遮挡下的操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型机器人操作多依赖空间分布式触觉阵列，成本高、易磨损、难以长期部署。作者希望利用稀疏单点高带宽（可达100 kHz）测量的丰富动力学信息，替代或补充空间密度扩展。

Method: 将单点触觉信号转为固定尺寸的时频谱图（time-frequency spectrogram），作为类图像表示输入标准视觉编码器，复用视觉学习流水线；传感器安装在远离接触面但与接触面机械耦合的位置以降低磨损；通过加速度、力、应变等不同触觉技术的统一表示验证通用性。

Result: 实验显示：(1) 机器人可利用单点振动信号完成视觉遮挡下的操作任务；(2) 时序历史显著影响策略表现，感测带宽决定可用频谱信息，测量可扩展至100 kHz；(3) 同一表示可跨加速度、力、应变等不同触觉传感技术使用；(4) 用现成商用硬件即可实现以往需研究级仪器才能获得的能力。

Conclusion: 高带宽触觉感测的易得化有助于将接触动力学整合进具身学习系统，在某些任务上可作为增加触觉空间密度的替代或补充方案。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is increasingly being incorporated into learning-based robotic manipulation, yet many existing approaches rely on spatially distributed sensors. Here we introduce SpectRobot, a framework that transforms single-point tactile signals into compact time-frequency spectrograms. These spectrograms encode high-bandwidth tactile histories as fixed-size image-like representations. They can be processed by standard vision encoders and integrated into learning pipelines originally developed for vision, while preserving temporal and frequency information unavailable to conventional cameras. Rather than increasing spatial density through arrays of tactile elements, SpectRobot exploits the rich dynamics contained in sparse, high-bandwidth single-point measurements. In our implementation, the sensors are mounted away from the contact surface while remaining mechanically coupled to it, reducing direct exposure to wear and potentially improving robustness in harsh environments and for long-term deployment on dexterous robots. Our experiments demonstrate that: (1) a robot can exploit single-point vibration signals to solve a visually occluded manipulation task; (2) temporal history strongly influences policy performance, while sensing bandwidth controls the spectral information available, with measurements extending to 100~kHz; and (3) the same representation can be used across different tactile sensing technologies mediated by acceleration, force, or strain. We further show that capabilities previously associated with research-grade instrumentation can be accessed using readily available, off-the-shelf hardware. We believe that broader access to high-bandwidth tactile sensing could facilitate the integration of contact dynamics into embodied learning systems and, for some tasks, offer an alternative or complement to increasing the spatial density of tactile sensing.

</details>


### [380] [From Semantic Decisions to Feasible Trajectories: Self-Evolving LLM-Guided Optimal Control for Narrow-Space Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24631)
*Zhengbao Yao,Yuanfu Luo,Kehan Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SE-LLM-OCP将大语言模型的高层离散机动决策与最优控制底层求解器统一起来：LLM在线提出稀疏机动序列，把非凸窄空间泊车分解为短时域轨迹优化子问题；求解失败时汇聚失败证据触发重规划，离线从在线失败中自演化结构化决策知识库。在类车模型与差速驱动机器人仿真中验证，提升窄场景安全泊车并实现机动表示跨平台迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸窄环境自主泊车困难：最优控制方法可显式施加车辆动力学与碰撞约束，但非凸性损害求解器鲁棒性、易失败；LLM具备强语义推理，却难以直接生成保证物理可行的稠密轨迹。因此需要一种兼顾语义推理与物理可行性的分层框架。

Method: 提出SE-LLM-OCP统一框架：LLM负责高层离散机动决策，最优控制模块负责底层动力学与碰撞约束。在线阶段LLM提出稀疏机动计划，将泊车任务分解为一系列短时域轨迹优化问题，低层求解器顺序求解OCP；求解失败时LLM聚合来自求解器与验证阶段的失败证据以引导重规划。离线阶段由累积的在线失败驱动，从零自动演化出结构化决策知识库。

Result: 在类车车辆模型与差速驱动机器人上的仿真实验表明，SE-LLM-OCP能在窄场景下实现更安全的自主泊车，并证明同一机动表示可迁移到不同运动学平台。

Conclusion: 将LLM的高层语义推理与最优控制的物理约束执行相结合，并以失败驱动的知识库演化提升鲁棒性，是应对非凸窄空间泊车等难任务的有效途径；所提机动表示具备跨运动学平台的可迁移性。局限在于验证仅限仿真，缺乏真实平台部署与真实世界证据。

Abstract: Autonomous parking in nonconvex and narrow environments remains challenging. Although optimal-control methods can explicitly enforce vehicle dynamics and collision constraints, nonconvexity compromises solver robustness and can cause failures. Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong semantic reasoning capabilities, but directly generating dense trajectories makes it difficult to guarantee physical feasibility. We introduce SE-LLM-OCP, a unified framework in which LLMs make high-level discrete maneuver decisions, while an optimal-control module enforces low-level vehicle dynamics and collision constraints. Online, the LLM proposes sparse maneuver plans, decomposing the parking task into a sequence of short-horizon trajectory-optimization problems. A low-level solver then sequentially solves optimal-control problems. If the solver fails, the LLM aggregates failure evidence from the solver and validation stages to guide replanning. Offline, SE-LLM-OCP automatically evolves a structured decision-making knowledge base from scratch, driven by accumulated online failures. We validate our proposed framework in simulation on a car-like vehicle model and on a differential-drive robot. Our experimental results show that SE-LLM-OCP enables safer autonomous parking in narrow scenarios and demonstrates transfer of the same maneuver representation to a different kinematic platform.

</details>


### [381] [Feasibility Distance Fields for Heterogeneous Constraints in Robot Configuration Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24632)
*Xijing Cui,Huayan Pu,Jun Luo,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"可行性距离场"(FDF)：在固定正定关节空间度量下，将各类机器人约束统一表示为到不可行构型集合的距离，从而实现异构约束的可比、可组合构型空间裕度，并用神经近似实时预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作器现有约束监控指标单位与梯度尺度不一致，无法给出"距离违反还剩多少构型空间运动"的统一度量，导致异构约束难以比较与组合，也难以指导多机协同反应。

Method: 以固定正定关节空间度量为基准，定义FDF为到不可行构型集合的并集的距离；借助经典距离集理论获得1-Lipschitz连续性、几乎处处可微与单位对偶梯度范数；给出可接受性分析证明实际约束（外/自碰撞、关节限位、灵巧度、笛卡尔与任务投影顺应性、负载下关节力矩、动态可操作度）为闭集；用点对点最小值组合异构约束，条件约束保持固定距离空间，多机约束产生块稀疏梯度；基于投影生成标签，用距离损失与Eikonal惩罚训练神经网络近似。

Result: 在UR5e与双臂单元上评估7个场：8000个构型中最大可行侧割线比为0.920，学习梯度范数均值0.994–0.998，投影残差0.011–0.034 rad；24条随机障碍路径下外部与组合碰撞场在3 cm内成功率90.4%与91.6%，符号错误率低于2%。误差主要出现在中轴附近与稀疏采样边界。

Conclusion: FDF为异构约束提供了统一的构型空间裕度度量，兼具理论性质（Lipschitz、单位梯度、闭集性质）与可学习的实时近似能力，可作为机器人约束监控与多机协同的基础表示。

Abstract: Robot manipulators are monitored by constraint-specific indicators whose units and gradient scales are not comparable, so they do not provide a common measure of the configuration-space motion remaining before violation. We define the feasibility distance field (FDF) as the distance, under a fixed positive-definite joint-space metric, to the union of infeasible configuration sets. Classical distance-to-set theory gives 1-Lipschitz continuity, almost-everywhere differentiability, and unit dual-gradient norm wherever the nearest projection is unique. The robotics contribution is an admissibility analysis showing when practical constraints define non-empty closed sets. We derive admissible formulations for external and self-collision, joint limits, dexterity, Cartesian and task-projected compliance, joint torque under payload, and dynamic manipulability. Since every field uses the same metric, heterogeneous constraints compose by a pointwise minimum, conditioned constraints retain a fixed distance space, and multi-robot constraints produce block-sparse gradients that identify which robots must react. We generate projection-based labels and train neural approximations with a distance loss and an Eikonal penalty. Simulations on a UR5e and a dual-arm cell evaluate seven fields using value, projection, sign, gradient, composition, and moving-obstacle diagnostics. Across 8,000 configurations, the largest feasible-side secant ratio is 0.920, mean learned gradient norms range from 0.994 to 0.998, and projection residuals range from 0.011 to 0.034 rad. Across 24 random obstacle paths, the external and composed collision fields achieve 90.4% and 91.6% success within 3 cm, with sign-error rates below 2%. The results support a common configuration-space margin and identify approximation errors near medial axes and sparsely sampled boundaries.

</details>


### [382] [Touch2Robot: Robot Touch in the Human Demonstration Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24660)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiaoyang Cheng,Hong Ying,Xiaoying Zhou,Jiaming Jiang,Haoran Guo,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Touch2Robot 让人类在采集演示时同步看到目标机器人手的接触可视化，通过在模拟中重建机器人-物体接触来引导人类调整后续交互，从而在保证数据质量的同时降低采集成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示数据采集成本低、规模易扩展，但其接触模式迁移到机器人手时可能不稳定或不可行；直接让目标机器人采集演示虽可避免这一问题，却会显著增加成本。作者希望同时解决数据质量与采集效率的权衡。

Method: 采集人手运动、触觉手套测量和物体运动数据，用这些记录引导物体特定的强化学习策略复现演示物体运动并偏好与人类触觉一致的接触；将学到的行为蒸馏为统一的实时重定向器，把人类观测与物体几何映射为机器人手构型；采集过程中将预测机器人构型与跟踪物体位姿在模拟中同步以重建机器人-物体接触并可视化，供演示者调整交互。

Result: 四个真实任务中，相比仅视觉反馈，平均真实机器人回放完成率从 37.9% 提升至 72.1%，每次回放成功演示的采集时间从 58.6 s 降至 18.2 s；重建的目标手接触与真实机器人触觉测量 F1 为 44.2%；基于 Touch2Robot 演示训练的策略在下游 Diffusion Policy 上比仅视觉反馈提升 29.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将机器人触觉引入人类演示闭环可同时提升灵巧操作数据采集的质量和效率，为可扩展的灵巧操作数据采集提供可行路径。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer a scalable way to collect manipulation data, but their contacts may be unstable or infeasible when transferred to a robot hand. Collecting demonstrations directly on the target robot avoids this mismatch, but substantially increases the cost of data collection. To address this trade-off, we present \textbf{Touch2Robot}, a framework that lets humans collect demonstrations while seeing how the target robot hand would contact the object. We capture human hand motion, tactile-glove measurements, and object motion during human manipulation. These recordings guide object-specific RL policies to reproduce the demonstrated object motion while favoring contacts consistent with the recorded human touch. We distill the learned behaviors into a unified real-time retargeter that maps incoming human observations and object geometry to robot hand configurations. During collection, the predicted robot configuration is synchronized with the tracked object pose in simulation to reconstruct robot-object contacts, which are visualized to help the demonstrator adapt subsequent interactions to the target hand. Across four real-world tasks, Touch2Robot improves average real-robot replay completion from 37.9\% to 72.1\% over visual-only feedback, while reducing the collection time per replay-successful demonstration from 58.6~s to 18.2~s. Reconstructed target-hand contacts achieve 44.2\% F1 against real-robot tactile measurements, and policies trained on Touch2Robot demonstrations improve downstream Diffusion Policy performance by 29.1 percentage points over visual-only feedback. These results show that bringing robot touch into the human demonstration loop improves both the quality and efficiency of scalable dexterous data collection. \textit{Project webpage: \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.}

</details>


### [383] [Think Like a World Model, Act Like a VLA: Distilling World-Model Representations into Compact Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24682)
*Trung Dao,Sankalp Yamsani,Jaden Park,Joohyung Kim,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过一次缓存冻结世界模型特征并加一个对齐损失，让VLA策略"继承"世界模型的物理表征而不承担生成成本，部署时与基线完全一致，在LIBERO/RoboCasa与真实双臂平台均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型直接把观测映射为动作，缺乏"世界如何响应"的目标，鲁棒性受数据覆盖限制；世界模型虽具备该目标与物理接地性，但未来rollout每步耗时达秒级，无法进入控制回路。作者希望在不增加推理成本的前提下获得世界模型的表征收益。

Method: 核心观察：世界模型对物理场景的知识存于内部特征，生成未来只是产生这些特征的训练目标，因此可以只继承表征而抛弃生成机制。做法是在常规VLA训练中加一项特征对齐损失：预先用冻结的世界模型对全部训练帧推理一次并缓存目标特征，学生网络学习与该缓存一致；训练时无需加载teacher，投影器用后即弃，部署策略与未蒸馏基线完全相同。

Result: 部署策略在消费级RTX 5090上以32 ms、1.86 GB运行。0.8B学生模型在LIBERO达97.9%；RoboCasa-GR1人形操作从48.2%提升到50.5%；同一目标在真实硬件（单臂与双臂平台）上同样有效。增益在学生规模、backbone、对齐层与teacher更换后依然成立。

Conclusion: 世界模型的价值可与其生成成本解耦：单纯继承内部表征即可为VLA带来稳定的物理先验，而非两个特定网络间的脆弱对齐；该方法跨规模、架构与教师具有可迁移性，且零推理开销。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map observations to actions with no objective that accounts for how the world responds, so their robustness is bounded primarily by data coverage. World models carry precisely that missing objective and are better grounded for it, yet rolling the future forward costs seconds per decision and rules them out of the control loop. We show the two can be separated. What a world model knows about physical scenes lives in its \emph{internal features}; generating the future is merely the objective that produced them, so the grounding can be inherited while the generative machinery is left behind. We add one feature-alignment term to ordinary VLA training: a frozen world model is run over the training frames once and cached, and the student learns to agree with that cache. No teacher is loaded during training, the projector is discarded after it, and the deployed policy is identical to the undistilled baseline, running in $32$~ms and $1.86$~GB on a consumer RTX~5090, so every gain is attributable to the representation rather than to added capacity or test-time compute. A $0.8$B student reaches $97.9\%$ on LIBERO, improves from $48.2\%$ to $50.5\%$ on RoboCasa-GR1 humanoid manipulation, and the same objective carries over to real hardware, on both a single-arm and a bimanual platform. The gain survives changes of student scale, backbone, alignment layer, and teacher, indicating a broad representational prior rather than a fragile alignment between two particular networks. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [384] [AC-DC: Adaptive Communication for Scalable Dynamic Average Consensus in Multi-Robot Ergodic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24702)
*Robin Inho Kee,Begum Cannataro,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AC-DC：一种自适应通信的分布式动态平均一致性算法，联合自适应"谁与谁通信、何时通信、交换状态哪些维度"，用于有限通信范围/速率与干扰约束下的多机器人系统，并应用于动态优先级遍历搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人在有限通信范围、有限速率和干扰约束下难以维持高效的一致性估计；通信带宽随团队规模增长、需等待全局通信轮次同步，限制了可扩展性与实时性。

Method: AC-DC 基于本地输入与成功接收的邻居信息，联合自适应通信对象（Who）、时机（When）与状态子集（What）；机器成对异步更新，无需等待所有机器人完成一轮；所选状态消息的坐标维度与团队规模无关（固定状态表示下）。将两条一致性流分别用于团队访问分布估计与区域测量融合以更新不确定度地图与搜索目标。

Result: 在最多80机器人、每种设置20组配对试验、共12种设置的遍历搜索任务中，AC-DC 的归一化协方差迹 AUC 与建模通信负载均为同类去中心化方法中最低；平均相对 SOTA 基线降低 27.5% AUC，通信量减少 8.7 倍。120机器人在 600×600 m 测试中通信量 19.3 MB，接近理想中心化基线的 19.2 MB。

Conclusion: 自适应选择通信对象、时机与状态子集可在保持点对点结构的前提下，使通信负载随规模趋近理想中心化方案，同时提升一致性精度，验证了通信—计算权衡对可扩展多机器人协调的有效性。

Abstract: We study scalable peer-to-peer dynamic average consensus (DC) for multi-robot systems under finite-range, finite-rate, and interference-constrained communication. We introduce Adaptive Communication for Dynamic Average Consensus (AC-DC), which jointly adapts Who communicates with whom, When, and over What parts of the consensus state, using local inputs and successfully received neighbor information. Each robot's consensus state estimates the current average of the robots' local inputs. AC-DC updates these estimates as local inputs change and averages the values exchanged between robot pairs. In AC-DC, robot pairs update without waiting for every robot to complete a communication round, and the selected-state messages carry consensus state coordinates independent of team size for a fixed state representation. We apply AC-DC to dynamic-priority multi-robot ergodic search: one consensus stream estimates team visitation for motion coordination, while the other fuses regional measurement information to update uncertainty maps and search targets. Across twelve settings with up to 80 robots and 20 paired trials per setting, AC-DC has the lowest mean (i) normalized covariance-trace area under the curve (AUC) and (ii) attempted modeled communication payload among the compared decentralized methods. Averaged across settings, AC-DC achieves paired AUC reductions of 27.5% relative to state-of-the-art baselines, with 8.7x less communication traffic. As the number of robots increases, we observe that AC-DC's communication payload approaches that of the ideal centralized baseline (one ground compute-station communicating directly with all robots): with 120 robots in a fixed 600 x 600 m scaling test, AC-DC uses 19.3 MB versus 19.2 MB for the ideal centralized baseline, while remaining peer-to-peer.

</details>


### [385] [SPARSER: Sparse Variable Projection by Exploiting Separable Structure in Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24708)
*Nikolas R. Sanderson,Andrew Fishberg,Haoyu Han,Heng Yang,Jonathan P. How,Hanumant Singh,Michael Everett,Alan Papalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARSER提出面向规范对称问题的变量投影(VarPro)框架，联合利用可分离性与稀疏性，通过无矩阵Schur补算子高效求解机器人感知中的大规模非线性最小二乘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知(如SLAM、SNL、SfM)常需解大规模NLS问题。稀疏性已被广泛利用，但可分离性(如视觉路标在线性残差中，固定位姿后有闭式解)仍未被充分利用。VarPro可解析消去线性变量得到降维问题，但因规范对称性(全局平移/旋转不变性)难以直接应用。

Method: 提出SPARSER：针对规范对称问题的VarPro框架，联合利用可分离性与稀疏性；构造无矩阵Schur补算子以高效计算降维代价、梯度与Hessian-向量积，可集成迭代式NLS求解器；刻画适用问题类别并识别可进一步解析简化的常见情形；证明基于IRLS的鲁棒代价大部分保留可 exploited 结构。

Result: 在合成与真实SLAM、SNL、SfM基准上，CPU/GPU平均比SOTA基线快5–7倍，单数据集增益超40倍；在含外点的多机器人SLAM数据上，鲁棒变体比SOTA GNC求解器快2–16倍。开源C++代码与数据集。

Conclusion: SPARSER有效解决了VarPro在规范对称场景下的应用瓶颈，统一了可分离性与稀疏性利用，为机器人感知NLS求解提供了显著加速且保持鲁棒性的通用框架。

Abstract: Robotic perception often requires solving large nonlinear least-squares (NLS) problems. While sparsity has been widely exploited to scale solvers, a complementary and underused structure is \emph{separability}: some variables, such as visual landmarks, appear linearly in the residuals and admit a closed-form solution once the remaining variables, such as poses, are fixed. Variable projection (VarPro) exploits this structure by analytically eliminating the linear variables, yielding a reduced problem with favorable computational properties. However, its use in robotic perception has been limited by gauge symmetries, such as invariance to global translations and rotations, which introduce challenges for standard VarPro methods. We present SPARSER (\textbf{S}parsity \textbf{P}reserving \textbf{A}nalytic \textbf{R}eduction for \textbf{S}eparable \textbf{R}obotic \textbf{P}erception), a VarPro framework for gauge-symmetric problems that jointly exploits separability and sparsity. Our method constructs a \emph{matrix-free Schur complement operator} for efficient evaluation of reduced costs, gradients, and Hessian-vector products, enabling integration with iterative NLS solvers. We characterize the applicable problem class, identify common cases admitting further analytical simplifications, and show that IRLS-based robust costs preserve most of the exploitable structure. Across synthetic and real SLAM, SNL, and SfM benchmarks, SPARSER is on average $5\times$--$7\times$ faster than state-of-the-art baselines on CPU and GPU, with gains exceeding $40\times$ on individual datasets. On outlier-corrupted multi-robot SLAM data, the robust variant is $2\times$--$16\times$ faster than a state-of-the-art GNC solver. We release open-source C++ code and all datasets.

</details>


### [386] [LLM-based Conversational AI Knowledge Assistant for MyBuddy Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24742)
*Hanxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向13轴MyBuddy人形机器人（Raspberry Pi驱动）构建LLM驱动的对话式AI知识助手，融合语音识别、互联网知识检索（Wikipedia、arXiv）、对话管理与语音合成，实现多轮连续对话与情感支持型HRI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人正走向以人为中心的应用，但传统基于规则、预定义回答与有限知识库的对话系统限制了其智能对话与自然知识辅助能力；LLM为自然、自适应、上下文感知的HRI提供了新基础，可解决上述瓶颈。

Method: 系统级集成方案：LLM驱动的语言理解与推理 + 实时语音识别 + 可扩展互联网引擎知识检索（Wikipedia、arXiv等）+ 灵活对话管理 + 自然语音合成，部署于Raspberry Pi供电的13轴MyBuddy人形机器人，支持多轮持续对话与情感支持交互。

Result: 摘要仅声明"present and implement"，未给出定量评测、用户研究或与基线（如规则式系统）的对比数据，缺乏可验证的性能证据。

Conclusion: 属于LLM赋能HRI的系统集成类工作，工程整合完整但方法新颖性与实证强度有限；若配备真实机器人演示、开源代码与用户研究，可提升相关度。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly being popular and developed for human-centered applications, yet their ability to provide intelligent conversations and natural interactive knowledge assistance remains constrained by traditional rule-based dialogue systems, pre-defined responses and limited knowledge repositories. Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful foundation for enabling natural, adaptive, and context-aware Human-Robot Interaction (HRI), which provides a significant opportunity to address such limitations by enabling robots to understand natural speech language, reason over complicated queries, maintain high-quality conversational context, and generate knowledge-rich responses. In this work, we originally present and implement an LLM-based versatile Conversational AI Knowledge Assistant for the Raspberry-Pi-powered 13-Axis MyBuddy humanoid robot, which integrates LLM-driven language understanding and AI reasoning with real-time speech recognition, knowledge retrieval via extensible access of internet engines (e.g., Wikipedia, arXiv), flexible dialogue management, and natural speech synthesis to enable much more intelligent multi-turn continuous conversations and advanced emotional-support Human-Robot Interaction.

</details>


### [387] [Beyond Visual Quality: A Study of Test-Time Planning with World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24745)
*Jianhao Yuan,Yu Yuan,Benjamin Ramtoula,Lukas Vierling,Paul Newman,Lars Kunze,Philip Torr,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文实证检验了世界动作模型（WAM）利用想象未来进行动作选择（规划）的潜力：同状态反事实分析显示选择机会存在于少数决策点，且基于视觉质量、物理一致性、任务进展等人工评分的选择器与学习价值选择器都只能恢复很小一部分可用提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型同时输出动作与其视觉后果预测，理论上可通过对同一状态采样多动作、比较想象结果并择优来实现规划，但如何用想象未来指导动作选择尚不明确，因此本文实证检验这一规划潜力。

Method: 同状态（same-state）受控分析：从同一状态采样多个动作及其预测后果，先估计oracle上界（选择实际结果最优的候选，成功率68.9%→79.2%），再作为受控干预测试基于视觉质量、物理一致性、任务进展的评分选择器，以及学习价值函数；并通过同状态反事实分支检查候选是否产生不同结果、差异是否可见于预测、评分是否识别这些差异。

Result: 同状态oracle选择将成功率从均匀随机选择的68.9%提升至79.2%；被测试的选择器虽偶有提升但增益不均且只回收了很少的可利用空间；选择机会集中在初始候选集中少数决策上；动作发散度与结果覆盖度并不同步增长；在完整动作执行的跨轨迹阶段评测中，各评分仍只能恢复极少可用提升（学习价值仅有小幅增益）。

Conclusion: 生成有后果差异的动作选项与在生成未来中识别这些差异是两种能力，现有评分器难以把想象未来转化为有效决策；应通过"对决策的实用性"而非单纯视觉质量来评估WAM预测。

Abstract: World action models generate actions together with visual predictions of their consequences. These paired outputs create the potential for planning by sampling multiple actions from one state, comparing their imagined outcomes, and choosing the action with the most promising predicted outcome. However, how to use imagined futures to guide action selection remains unclear. We examine this planning potential empirically. First, we estimate an oracle upper bound on selection by choosing the sampled candidate whose realised outcome is best. In a controlled same-state analysis, this choice raises success from 68.9% under uniform random selection to 79.2%. We then test selectors based on visual quality, physical consistency, and task progression as controlled interventions. Some tested selectors yield higher observed success, but the gains are uneven and the matched selectors leave much of the measured opportunity unrecovered. To investigate this gap, we examine whether sampled actions lead to different outcomes, whether these differences are visible in the predictions, and whether a score recognises them. Counterfactual branching from the same states shows that selection opportunity is concentrated in relatively few decisions in the initial candidate sets. Action spread and outcome coverage need not increase together. In a further evaluation across trajectory phases with complete action execution, the tested scores again recover little of the available improvement despite a small gain from learned value. These findings distinguish producing consequential action choices from recognising them in generated futures, motivating the evaluation of WAM predictions through their usefulness for decisions rather than visual quality alone.

</details>


### [388] [D-JEPA: A Decision-Aligned Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24749)
*Shuaijun Liu,Chengyu Wu,Qifu Wen,Feiyang You,Chenglong Zhang,Shuyang Hao,Xi Lin,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-JEPA：决策对齐的潜在世界模型，用有界置换等变算子学习候选未来间的决策相关序关系，改善动作选择


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: latent world model 能预测动作后果，但预测准确不代表潜在距离能正确排序候选动作；作者识别出“决策局部预测鸿沟”：在少数竞争执行的未来中，被预测更接近目标的候选实际执行结果可能反而更差

Method: 提出 D-JEPA，从已执行结果中学习候选未来间决策相关关系。使用有界、置换等变算子联合推理目标相对预测特征与序数证据，在决策最关键处精修预训练预测几何；通过受限预测器适配与共享序数接口跨互补预测几何扩展对齐；并在 JEPA 兼容的未来表示中实现所学决策结构，以原生潜在距离规划部署

Result: 在潜在控制、操作、预训练动作生成模型、物理机器人和自动驾驶上评估，动作选择改善：PushT 成功率 87.89%，RoboTwin 平均提升 15.04 点，物理机器人任务提升 17 点

Conclusion: 决策相关的关系统结构可作为预测性世界建模与有效控制之间的直接桥梁

Abstract: Latent world models predict the consequences of actions, but accurate prediction does not guarantee that latent distance reflects which candidate will execute successfully. We identify a decision-local prediction gap: among the few futures competing for execution, a candidate predicted closer to the goal can produce a worse realized outcome than an available alternative. We introduce D-JEPA, a decision-aligned latent world model that learns decision-relevant relations among candidate futures from executed outcomes. A bounded, permutation-equivariant operator jointly reasons over goal-relative predictive features and ordinal evidence, refining pretrained predictive geometry where action choices are most consequential. Restricted predictor adaptation and a shared ordinal interface extend this alignment across complementary predictive geometries. D-JEPA further realizes the learned decision structure in JEPA-compatible future representations, enabling deployment through native latent-distance planning. Evaluations across latent control, manipulation, pretrained action-producing models, physical robots and autonomous driving demonstrate improved action selection, including 87.89% success on PushT, a 15.04-point average gain on RoboTwin, and a 17-point gain on physical robot tasks. These results establish decision-relevant relational structure as a direct bridge between predictive world modeling and effective control.

</details>


### [389] [A Switched Adaptive Control Framework for Aerial Manipulators Under Dynamic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24761)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav,Saksham Gupta,Amitabh Sharma,Sarthak Mishra,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy,Simone Baldi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于切换系统的自适应控制框架，无需先验的飞行器-机械臂耦合与状态相关不确定性知识，通过给出保证稳定性的切换信号类来处理任务执行中的动态突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在执行复杂交互任务时会经历动态"切换"过程（接触/脱离、载荷变化等），导致系统动力学出现突变或跳变。现有空中操作控制方法并非为切换工况设计，且大多难以处理飞行器与机械臂之间的紧密耦合，以及由建模困难导致的状态相关不确定性，因此需要新的控制框架。

Method: 采用切换系统（switched systems）建模思路，构建切换型自适应控制框架：一方面不依赖飞行器-机械臂耦合及状态相关不确定性的先验知识，进行自适应估计与补偿；另一方面提出一类切换信号（switching signals），刻画哪些过渡阶段能够保证系统稳定，从而在动力学变化下仍保持稳定操作。

Result: 通过对比实验验证了所提切换型框架相对于现有先进方法的有效性，表明其在过渡阶段稳定性与操作性能上更优。

Conclusion: 将切换系统理论与自适应控制结合，为空中机械臂在动态过渡过程中的稳定操作提供了无需先验模型知识的可行方案，弥补了现有方法在切换工况与耦合不确定性处理上的不足。

Abstract: Aerial manipulators represent the forefront of aerial robotics. Although potentially capable of complex interaction tasks, controlling aerial manipulators throughout the dynamic transitions occurring during task execution presents significant challenges. Abrupt or discontinuous changes in system dynamics generated by the transitions suggest the use of a switched approach, yet the available aerial manipulation methods are not designed for coping with switched regimes. In addition, most available methods fall short in coping with the tight couplings between the aerial vehicle and the manipulator, as well as in coping with the state-dependent uncertainties arising from the difficulty in modeling such couplings. We propose a switched-based adaptive control framework for aerial manipulators not relying on a priori knowledge of the vehicle-manipulator couplings and of state-dependent uncertainties. To guarantee stable manipulation despite changes in system dynamics, the framework provides a class of switching signals characterizing those transition phases for which the system is guaranteed to remain stable. Comparative experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed switched-based framework over the state of the art.

</details>


### [390] [H2RBench: A Real-to-Sim Benchmark for Evaluating Human-to-Robot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24778)
*Chuyang Xiao,Haotian Zhan,Sriram Krishna,Peilin Meng,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H2RBench：基于Real2Sim的标准化基准，用真实人类视频演示与仿真机器人演示统一评测多种human-to-robot技能迁移方法，覆盖四类操作任务，并证明仿真性能可良好预测真机表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H2R（human-to-robot）迁移是有望扩展机器人学习规模的路径，但现有方法在任务集、场景布局、物体实例、机器人监督量等设置上各不相同，缺乏统一比较标准，无法判断哪种跨具身（embodiment gap）桥接策略更优、更易从更多人类数据中获益。

Method: 提出H2RBench：一个Real2Sim评测基准。以真实人类视频演示为源数据、仿真机器人演示为评测载体，构建标准化协议（统一任务、场景、物体与监督量设置）；包含四个交互需求各异的操作任务；纳入多种代表性H2R迁移方法（分别对应不同的具身差距桥接策略），并在统一协议下进行控制变量式对比与数据规模缩放实验。

Result: 系统刻画了各方法随人类演示数量增加的性能缩放规律，发现不同方法利用额外人类数据的能力差异显著；同时验证仿真性能对真机性能具有较强预测性：跨方法-任务配置的Pearson相关 r=0.89、Spearman ρ=0.85、平均最大排名违例MMRV=0.06。

Conclusion: H2RBench为H2R迁移提供了实用且可扩展的比较性评测平台，可在真机部署前筛选与评估方法；但论文本身以基准与评测协议为贡献，未提出新的迁移算法。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation policies from human video demonstrations constitutes a promising avenue for scalable robot learning. However, comparing different human-to-robot (H2R) transfer methods remains challenging, as existing approaches are evaluated under different settings, including differing task suites, scene layouts, object instances, and amounts of robot supervision. To address this challenge, we present H2RBench, a Real2Sim benchmark for evaluating H2R transfer methods. H2RBench provides a standardized protocol built on real human video demonstrations and simulated robot demonstrations, and includes four manipulation tasks spanning diverse interaction requirements. We evaluate multiple representative H2R transfer methods, each adopting a different strategy for bridging the embodiment gap. Using H2RBench, we systematically characterize how each method scales with the amount of human demonstrations, revealing that methods differ substantially in their ability to leverage additional human data. We further show that simulation performance is broadly predictive of real-world robot performance, with an overall Pearson correlation of r = 0.89, Spearman correlation of \r{ho} = 0.85 and Mean Maximum Rank Violation (MMRV) of 0.06 across method-task configurations. These results establish H2RBench as a practical and scalable benchmark for comparative H2R evaluation prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [391] [Uranus: Building the Next-Generation Simulation Infrastructure for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24815)
*Wenkang Qin,Yukun Zhou,Noah Shen,Jisong Cai,Dongxiao Mao,Baicheng Li,Yue Zhang,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uranus 是一个数据驱动的机器人仿真器，基于关节轨迹条件自回归扩散模型，可流式开放式 rollout、低延迟生成（24 FPS），并统一支持多视角、多机器人本体的同步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界交互成本高昂，而传统仿真器构建需大量人工建模。作者希望用数据驱动的方式实现可扩展的机器人仿真，服务于数据生成、策略训练与评估及安全迭代。

Method: 以关节轨迹为条件的自回归扩散模型：在线接收未来关节位置轨迹，每步自回归生成一个 latent frame（对应四帧 RGB），无固定时域；通过推理优化达到 24 FPS；提供统一接口支持多机器人本体与相机配置的多视角同步生成。

Result: 在分布内与分布外数据上开展定量与定性评估，客观衡量 Uranus 的能力并明确其当前局限；开源代码与模型权重。

Conclusion: Uranus 展示了轨迹条件自回归扩散作为可扩展机器人仿真器的可行性，具备流式生成与低延迟特性，但仍有明确局限，代码与权重开源以促进社区实践。

Abstract: Scalable simulation is essential for robot data generation, policy training, evaluation, and safe iteration, yet real-world interaction is costly and conventional simulators require labor-intensive construction. We present Uranus, a data-driven robot simulator built around a joint-trajectory-conditioned autoregressive diffusion model. Uranus offers three key capabilities: (1) streaming, open-ended rollout, which receives future joint-position trajectories online and autoregressively generates one latent frame per step, corresponding to four RGB frames, without a fixed horizon; (2) low-latency generation, achieving 24 FPS after inference optimization; and (3) scalable, extensible robot control, providing a unified interface for synchronized multi-view generation across diverse robot embodiments and camera configurations. We conduct comprehensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data, providing an objective assessment of Uranus and clearly identifying its current limitations. We release the code and model weights to empower the community with practical tools and insights.

</details>


### [392] [Minimum Time Trajectories for a Car-Like Mobile Robot Moving with Rigid Wheels Under Non-Sliding Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24832)
*Joseph Ben-Asher,Elon Rimon,Leeor Ravina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将车形机器人的时间最优轨迹问题从纯运动学模型扩展到包含动力学非滑动约束的模型，新增三种非滑动路径基元（并给出近似解析解），与原有十二种运动学基元共同构成时间最优轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有时间最优轨迹研究基于运动学车形模型，其纯滚动假设依赖前后轮非滑动约束，而该约束本质上只能由机器人动力学刻画，因此运动学解在实际中不可直接行（易打滑）。作者希望弥合这一缺口。

Method: 基于机器人动力学建立非滑动约束，将其并入运动学模型；在原有十二种时间最优路径基元基础上新增三种与非滑动约束相关的路径基元，并对这些新基元推导近似解析解；通过典型机动（如前进/后退转向）的实例分析非滑动约束对时间最优轨迹的影响。控制量为有界前轮加速度与前轮转向速率。

Result: 给出三种非滑动路径基元的近似解析解，连同运动学十二基元构成完整的时间最优轨迹集合；示例显示非滑动约束会改变车形机器人时间最优轨迹的形态与切换结构。

Conclusion: 在无障碍环境下，通过动力学非滑动约束扩展运动学时间最优基元，可得到更符合真实车轮滚动条件的时间最优轨迹；该方法为车形移动机器人的高效轨迹规划提供了可解析的构造性工具。

Abstract: This paper studies the minimum time trajectoriesvof a car-like mobile robot navigating in an obstacle free environment. The robot, with forward and backward speeds, is controlled by bounded front-wheels acceleration and limited front-wheels steering rate. The paper extends previous results which solved this problem for the kinematic car-like robot. However, the kinematic model assumes pure rolling at the wheels ground contacts. This assumption requires non-sliding constraints for the front and rear wheels that can only be handled by the robot dynamics. This paper formulates the non-sliding constraints based on the robot dynamics then augments the kinematic model time-optimal path primitives with three new path primitives associated with the non-sliding constraints. The three non-sliding path primitives together with the kinematic model twelve path primitives form the car-like robot time optimal trajectories. Approximate analytic solutions for the non-sliding path primitives are also provided. Examples study the time-optimal path primitives along representative maneuvers, illustrating how the non-sliding constraints influence the time optimal trajectories of the car-like robot.

</details>


### [393] [PredActor: Predictive Action Diffusion for Steerable Onboard Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24840)
*Lei Ye,Haibo Gao,Yitang Li,Peng Xu,Zetong Jing,Junhan Sun,Fanrong Dong,Ziqi Han,Xue Wang,Jianhua Sun,Cewu Lu,Hao Zhao,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PredActor 是一种仅用本体感知的预测式动作扩散策略，联合生成可执行动作与内部未来状态轨迹，实现文本条件行为选择与测试时运动引导，并在 Unitree G1 上完成真机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层级式人形控制依赖参考轨迹与独立跟踪器，能力受限且恢复行为交给跟踪器；纯动作扩散缺少显式未来状态轨迹，难以在测试时施加运动目标；联合状态-动作扩散虽提供该表示，但常依赖特权全身状态，且行为选择与测试时引导能力分散、不统一。

Method: PredActor 以本体感知历史（可选任务上下文）为条件，联合扩散生成可执行动作与内部未来状态轨迹；训练阶段用无分类器引导增强文本条件行为，测试阶段用分类器引导把预测状态拉向目标任务；仅执行动作，无需独立运动跟踪器，也无需外部估计的全身状态作为策略输入。为满足机载实时性，采用滚动去噪与计算复用等运行时优化。

Result: 仿真中到达全部 15 个目标点，文本检索得分 0.580（条件动作扩散为 0.373），扰动存活率相近；Jetson Orin NX 上完整回调中位 16.790 ms、p95 19.383 ms，均在 20 ms 控制周期之内。在 Unitree G1 上部署，仿真与实机均展示了文本条件运动、扰动响应、手柄控制与语义插值。

Conclusion: 将互补的引导能力（文本条件行为选择、测试时状态引导）统一进单一可直接执行的本体感知策略，兼顾轨迹级可引导性与机载实时性，并在人形硬件上验证。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer flexible motion generation, but translating this flexibility into feedback-responsive humanoid control remains challenging. Hierarchical systems steer motion through references that may exceed a separate tracker's capabilities, leaving recovery and physical execution largely to the tracker. Action-only diffusion generates actions directly but lacks an explicit future-state trajectory for test-time motion objectives. Joint state-action diffusion provides this representation, yet representative controllers often depend on privileged full-body states, and support for learned behavior selection and test-time motion steering remains fragmented. We present PredActor, a predictive action diffusion policy that brings these complementary steering capabilities into one directly executed policy using proprioceptive observations. Conditioned on proprioceptive history and optional task context, PredActor jointly generates executable actions and an internal future-state trajectory. Classifier-free guidance strengthens text-conditioned behavior, while classifier guidance steers predicted states toward test-time objectives. Only actions are executed, without a separate motion-reference tracker or externally estimated full-body states as policy inputs. In simulation, PredActor reaches all 15 destination targets and achieves a text retrieval score of 0.580, compared with 0.373 for conditional action diffusion, with similar observed disturbance survival. To make this guided policy practical onboard, rolling denoising and computation-preserving runtime optimizations reduce the complete callback to 16.790 ms median and 19.383 ms p95 on a Jetson Orin NX, both below the 20 ms control period. We deploy PredActor on a Unitree G1; evaluations across simulation and physical hardware demonstrate text-conditioned motion, disturbance response, joystick control, and semantic interpolation.

</details>


### [394] [CAST: Collision-Aware Assembly with Construction Robots using Simultaneous Trajectory Estimation and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24841)
*Karthik Shaji,Chisung Kim,John D'Amato,Edvard Bruun,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出单一因子图框架，将实测机器人状态与显式碰撞约束、学习到的线缆约束联合优化，实现多机器人施工场景下的同步高维轨迹估计与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人施工环境配置空间维度高、避障约束复杂（含机器人间、装配对象与工作空间边界），且重型机器人存在刚性振动引入的不确定性；同时工作空间会变化，需要统一处理估计与规划的同步框架。

Method: 构建单一因子图同时用于轨迹估计与规划：因子包含机器人状态测量因子、显式碰撞因子以及从数据中学习得到的线缆约束因子；据此在变化工作空间中进行同步高维多机器人运动规划，并显式建模刚性与振动诱导的不确定性。

Result: 在真实施工任务上验证：一台机械臂作为木构件抓取器，第二台机器人作为打钉紧固器，成功完成梁柱式（post-and-lintel）结构的搭建。

Conclusion: 统一的因子图表述可把估计、碰撞与学习到的任务约束整合到同一优化中，支撑变工作空间下的多机器人协同高精度施工，为重型机器人系统提供可行的规划与估计方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems have shown increasing viability in construction due to their ability to execute high-precision actions while reducing human exposure to hazardous tasks. However, these environments have high-dimensional configuration spaces and possess substantial collision-avoidance constraints, which include other robots, assembly objects, and workspace boundaries. We utilize a single factor graph for trajectory estimation and planning that incorporates measured robot states together with explicit collision and learned cable constraints. This supports changing workspaces and enables synchronized, high-dimensional robot motion planning while accounting for the stiff, vibration-induced uncertainty of heavy robotic systems. We demonstrate the success of our framework on the construction of a post-and-lintel structure using one robot arm as a timber gripper, and a second robot as a nail-fastener.

</details>


### [395] [Range-Aided SLAM Initialization Exploiting Accurate Heading Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24846)
*Isabel Lougheed,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向测距辅助SLAM（RA-SLAM）的两步初始化方法：先利用GTRS求解AUV与应答器间的相对位置，再结合高精度航向解线性最小二乘，获得可靠的应答器与机器人位置初值，并在真实AUV/LBL+INS数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般SLAM问题具有可分离结构：已知机器人航向时，地标与机器人位置可线性求解。AUV导航中惯性导航系统能提供高精度航向，而单纯依靠非线性测距进行RA-SLAM初始化容易陷入局部最优、收敛不稳定，因此需要一种利用该先验的可靠初始化方案。

Method: 两步法。第一步：在给定非线性测距测量的条件下，将应答器相对AUV的位置求解建模为广义信赖域子问题（GTRS）并求解。第二步：利用求得的应答器相对位置与AUV已知航向，构建并求解线性最小二乘问题，从而估计应答器与AUV的绝对位置。所得估计连同高精度航向为后续一般非线性RA-SLAM优化提供初值。

Result: 在真实AUV数据集上验证了方法有效性；该数据集同时提供长基线（LBL）测距与INS给出的高精度航向信息，表明所提两步初始化能在实际条件下给出可靠初值。

Conclusion: 利用高精度航向提供的可分离结构，可把原本非凸的RA-SLAM初始化分解为GTRS加线性最小二乘两步，为AUV测距SLAM提供实用且可靠的初始化手段。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel initialization method for range-aided simultaneous localization and mapping (RA-SLAM). The general SLAM problem has a well-known separable structure where landmark and robot positions can be solved for in a linear fashion given known robot headings. This paper considers the case where highly accurate heading information is available, which is typical of autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) navigation, to solve for the range transponder and robot positions. The proposed approach consists of two steps. First, using a generalized trust region subproblem (GTRS), the positions of the transponders relative to the AUV are solved for given the nonlinear range measurements. Second, the relative transponder positions and the known heading of the AUV are used to estimate the transponder and AUV positions by solving a linear least-squares problem. These transponder and AUV position estimates, combined with the highly accurate heading information, provide a reliable initialization method for the general nonlinear RA-SLAM problem. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is tested on a real-world AUV dataset where long baseline (LBL) range measurements are provided in concert with highly accurate heading information provided by an inertial navigation system (INS).

</details>


### [396] [SE(3) Neural Potential Fields for 6-DoF Trajectory Planning Directly from Images Without Explicit 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24864)
*Jeffrey Eiyike,Masoud Ataei,Elvis Gyaase,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出从带位姿RGB图像学习SE(3)神经势场，用导航函数（自由空间测地距离）监督替代图像监督，解决经典人工势场局部极小与贴障碍问题，实现无碰撞6-DoF抓取轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱场景中到达6-DoF抓取位姿需要无碰撞轨迹，传统方法依赖3D重建后规划，代价是重建精度与计算开销；从图像直接学习的势场虽免去重建，却继承人工势场固有缺陷：吸引与排斥梯度相消时下降路径擦过障碍而不绕行，并可能停在目标之前。

Method: 学SE(3)神经势场，输入带位姿RGB图像；监督信号为导航函数——训练时从同一批图像恢复的自由空间中到抓取位姿的测地距离，替代单纯图像监督。

Result: 两个桌面场景、UR10上从被障碍阻挡的起点出发：该场从所有起点均在3 cm内收敛到抓取位姿，执行的每条路径对真值几何均无碰撞（仅图像监督为25%与0%）；平均间隙由不足1 cm升至8.6-8.8 cm，臂杆接触由20.6-50.4%降至2.7-5.5%的执行配置；执行抓取成功率90.0%与40.0%，残余失败源于笛卡尔执行器拒动而非势场本身。规划约2 s，对比在同图像重建上用RRT*的67-133 s；若置于统一离线测试框架两者相当，部署差距来自密集重建的碰撞检测开销而非规划器复杂度。

Conclusion: 导航函数监督的SE(3)神经势场可在无需显式3D重建的情况下生成无碰撞6-DoF抓取轨迹，消除势场局部极小与贴障碍失效；实际耗时优势主要来自免去密集重建的碰撞检测，而非规划算法本身。

Abstract: Reaching a 6-DoF grasp pose in clutter requires a collision-free trajectory, conventionally obtained by reconstructing the scene in 3D and planning inside that reconstruction, at the cost of its accuracy and compute. Potential fields learned directly from images remove that dependency but inherit the classical weakness of artificial potential fields: where attractive and repulsive gradients cancel, the descent grazes the obstacle instead of going around it, and can stall short of the goal. We present an SE(3) neural potential field learned from posed RGB images and supervised with a navigation function, the geodesic distance to the grasp through free space recovered from those same images during training, which removes both failures. On two tabletop scenes, from obstacle-blocked starts executed on a UR10, the field converges within 3 cm of the grasp from every start and every path it executes is collision-free against the ground-truth geometry, against 25% and 0% under image supervision alone; mean clearance rises from under a centimeter to 8.6-8.8 cm and arm-link contacts fall from 20.6-50.4% to 2.7-5.5% of executed configurations. Executed grasp success is 90.0% and 40.0% on the two scenes, the residual failures being refusals of the Cartesian executor rather than of the field. Planning takes about 2 s against 67-133 s for RRT* on a reconstruction of the same images, though under a common offline harness the two are comparable: the deployed margin is the cost of collision-checking a dense reconstruction, not planner complexity.

</details>


### [397] [DualWAM: Dual-System World Action Models for Asynchronous Global Planning and Local Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24868)
*Yixin Zheng,Jiangran Lyu,Yuntian Deng,Kai Liu,Yizhou Zhou,Yizhou Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao,He Wang,Zhizheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双系统 World Action Model（WAM）方法：全局规划器周期性地对较长世界-动作块做高噪声双向去噪以建立全局计划，腕部局部系统从中间去噪状态截取时间对齐的短窗口，结合最新腕部观测完成低噪声精修；两系统沿共享去噪轨迹异步运行，实现高频闭环动作更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 同时生成动作并预测未来世界状态，但视觉预测计算开销大，因此普遍采用长动作块来分摊推理成本，牺牲了闭环响应频率。作者希望在保留较长视野世界-动作生成能力的同时，实现高频闭环动作更新。

Method: 双系统解耦架构，共享一条去噪轨迹：全局系统对更长的世界-动作块执行高噪声双向去噪，得到全局计划；腕部局部系统从中间去噪状态抽取时间对齐的短窗口，利用最新腕部观测（提供动作对齐的局部几何、运动与接触线索）进行低噪声精修。每个全局计划被多次局部更新复用，局部系统持续融入新交互反馈；两系统异步运行，并天然支持边-云部署。

Result: 在 Franka 与 Galbot 的零样本操作任务上，相较最强基线平均成功率提升 4.5 个百分点，关键路径加速 16.6 倍；角色匹配的第一人称与 UMI 数据带来 14 个百分点的成功率提升；解耦设计支持边-云部署，通信开销显著低于基线。

Conclusion: 通过解耦全局规划与局部精修，可在不牺牲长视野世界-动作生成的前提下实现高频闭环控制，兼具性能提升与推理加速，并利于边-云协同部署。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate robot actions and predict future world states, transferring priors from video pretraining to robot control. However, future visual prediction is computationally expensive, so existing WAMs often rely on long action chunks to amortize inference cost across control steps, at the cost of closed-loop responsiveness. We present \method, a dual-system WAM that preserves broader-horizon world-action generation while enabling high-frequency closed-loop action updates by decoupling global planning and local refinement. \systwo periodically performs high-noise bidirectional denoising over a broader world-action chunk to establish a global plan, while wrist-only \sysone extracts a temporally aligned short window from the intermediate denoising state and completes low-noise refinement using the latest wrist observations, which provide action-aligned cues about local geometry, motion, and contact during interaction. The two systems operate asynchronously along a shared denoising trajectory: each global plan is reused across multiple local updates, while \sysone repeatedly incorporates fresh interaction feedback. Across zero-shot manipulation tasks on Franka and Galbot, \method improves success over the strongest evaluated baseline by 4.5 percentage points on average, while achieving a 16.6$\times$ critical-path speedup. Further studies show that role-matched egocentric and UMI data improve success by 14 percentage points, and that the decoupled design naturally supports edge--cloud deployment with substantially lower communication overhead than the baseline.

</details>


### [398] [Steerable and Reactive Grasping Through Modular Design with a Three-Point Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24896)
*Andrew Nguyen,Yonghyeon Lee,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三段式灵巧抓取框架：从预计算抓取可供性热图采样三点接触，模型驱动反应式控制器负责追踪与避碰并引导到达，最后几厘米由仅依赖本体感受（指关节状态与近期动作）的 RL 策略精调并稳定抓取，单一策略跨物体共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取需解决"在哪抓、如何到达、如何维持稳定接触"三类问题；端到端策略难以兼顾全局几何推理与局部接触控制，且通常依赖视觉或目标点，跨物体泛化差。

Method: 紧凑三点式界面：给定物体几何与可选语言指令，从预计算抓取可供性热图中采样接触三元组；模型驱动反应式控制器跟踪物体、避碰并引导手部接近所选接触点；接近末端时用 RL 策略基于本体感受反馈（仅指关节状态与近期动作，无目标点、视觉与几何输入）精修并稳定抓取；采用模块化架构与统一策略。

Result: 仿真中与挤压式及端到端基线对比抓取-抬升成功率，刻画到达收敛性并演示抓取引导（grasp steering）；硬件上在两个训练物体与一个未见物体上完成全流程演示。

Conclusion: 用几何引导到达、用局部反馈锁定抓取，模块化设计使单一无视觉局部策略可在多物体与多抓取构型间共享，形成可复用的灵巧抓取流水线。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping requires deciding where to grasp, reaching the target, and maintaining stable contact. We connect these stages through a compact three-point interface that separates global geometric reasoning from local contact control. Given object geometry and optional language commands, our framework samples contact triples from a precomputed grasp-affordance heatmap. A model-based reactive controller tracks the object, avoids collisions, and guides the hand toward the selected contacts. In the final centimeters, a Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy uses proprioceptive feedback to refine and stabilize the grasp despite reaching and perception errors. It observes only finger joint states and its recent actions, with no target points, visual observations, or object geometry, so a single policy is shared across objects and grasp configurations. In simulation, we compare grasp-and-lift success against squeeze and end-to-end baselines, characterize reaching convergence, and demonstrate grasp steering; hardware demonstrations on two training objects and one unseen object illustrate the full pipeline. Our modular framework uses geometry to guide the reach and local feedback to secure the grasp.

</details>


### [399] [Visuomotor Robotic Pruning in Planar Orchards Using Hybrid Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24906)
*Abhinav Jain,Cindy Grimm,Stefan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出端到端闭环视觉运动控制流水线用于果树冬季修剪，全程仿真+合成数据训练，零样本迁移到真实果园，光学流输入避免3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园冬季修剪劳动密集，而现代平面化整形系统（V-Trellis苹果、UFO樱桃）的修剪需求迫切；机器人修剪的难点在于枝干环境杂乱、需要精确到达切点并保持刀具姿态，且真实数据采集成本极高。

Method: 1) 合成生成平面化果园树网格；2) 构建基于物理的果园仿真器；3) 通过运动规划自动采集成功修剪轨迹；4) 用新型混合强化学习算法（离线示范+在线仿真rollout）训练策略；控制器以腕部相机光学流为输入，连续引导刀具穿越杂枝到达指定切点并保持正确刀具朝向。

Result: 仿真中3000个修剪点任务空间评测：V-Trellis苹果成功率49.9%，UFO樱桃46.0%；38次实机试验（28次商业与实验果园户外、10次室内实验室）验证零样本sim-to-real迁移；实验室硬件上优于经典RRT-Connect基线。

Conclusion: 纯仿真训练的闭环视觉运动策略可实现零样本真实果园部署，光学流输入替代3D重建可行，混合RL有效利用离线示范与在线rollout。

Abstract: Dormant tree pruning is labor-intensive yet essential for maintaining modern high-productivity fruit orchards. In this work, we focus on pruning of modern planar tree training systems - V-Trellis apples and UFO cherries - where trunks and primary branches are trained into approximately planar walls. We introduce an end-to-end pipeline to learn a closed-loop visuomotor controller for robotic pruning. This controller is trained entirely using simulation and synthetically generated data and deployed in real orchards in a zero-shot manner. The pipeline comprises synthetic generation of planar orchard tree meshes, construction of a physics-based orchard simulator, automated collection of successful pruning trajectories via motion planning, and policy learning with a novel hybrid reinforcement-learning algorithm that combines offline demonstrations with online simulated rollouts. The controller uses optical-flow inputs from a wrist-mounted camera - avoiding the need for full 3D-reconstruction - and continuously guides the cutter through cluttered branch environments to a specified cutpoint with correct tool orientation. In exhaustive simulated task-space evaluations over 3,000 pruning points, the policy attains 49.9% success on V-Trellis apples and 46.0% on UFO cherries. We validate the learned controller across 38 physical trials - comprising 28 outdoor field trials in commercial and experimental orchards and 10 indoor laboratory tests - demonstrating zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. The learned policy also outperforms a classical RRT-Connect baseline on physical hardware in laboratory trials.

</details>


### [400] [Learning to Drive on Mars: Visual Multimodal Traversability Estimation for Off-World Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24952)
*Darren Chiu,Cole Wilson,Andrei Tumbar,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Steven Myint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发布火星2020毅力号巡视器500 sol、45km的多模态导航数据集（灰度立体图像、位姿、IMU、摇臂-转向架角度、倾角与滑移估计），并提出不确定性感知的可通行性估计框架，AUROC 0.874、F1 0.758，优于最强基线，并在实体巡视器试验台上接入ENav路径规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地外环境的基于学习导航受限于缺乏大规模数据集；毅力号在Jezero陨石坑经历了沙丘、岩石区、平坦基岩等多样地形，为构建真实跨域可通行性数据集提供了独特条件。

Method: 构建涵盖500 sol、45km（人类操作员与星载ENav规划器共同驾驶）的数据集，包含灰度立体像对、位姿、加速度计读数、摇臂-转向架关节角、倾角与车轮滑移估计；在此基础上提出不确定性感知的可通行性估计框架，从多模态驾驶经验中学习地形表征。

Result: 在Mars 2020数据集上AUROC 0.874、F1 0.758，较最强基线分别提升0.058与0.156，并取得最高平均精度与召回率；视觉表征可集成到ENav等路径规划器中，并在实体巡视器试验台验证。

Conclusion: 真实火星巡视数据可支撑不确定性感知的可通行性学习，且所学视觉表征可实际嵌入星载风格的路径规划器，具备从数据集到规划器的完整闭环验证。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation on Mars requires vehicles to distinguish between traversable terrains across diverse and visually challenging environments. However, progress in learning-based navigation for off-world environments has been limited by the lack of large-scale datasets. Since landing in Jezero Crater, the Mars 2020 Perseverance rover has traversed terrain ranging from sandy dunes, rocky patches, and flat bedrocks. As a result, this paper presents a dataset spanning 500 sols and 45km of trajectories driven by both human operators and the onboard planner, ENav. Our dataset contains grayscale stereo image pairs, poses, accelerometer readings, rocker-bogie angles, and estimates of tilt and wheel slip. Building on this dataset, we introduce an uncertainty-aware traversability-estimation framework that learns terrain representations from multimodal driving experience. We compare our proposed method against existing approaches on the Mars 2020 dataset and show that our method achieves an AUROC of 0.874 and an F1 score of 0.758, outperforming the strongest baseline by 0.058 and 0.156, respectively, while also achieving the highest average precision and recall. Finally, we show that the visual representations can be integrated into path planners, such as ENav, on a physical rover test bed. Videos, code, and the M2020 dataset will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [401] [DexTacWAM: A Visuo-Tactile World-Action Model for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24976)
*Haoran Yuan,Zekai Wang,Boning Shao,Haoran Lu,Trevor Darrell,Ismini Lourentzou,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexTacWAM：视觉-触觉世界-动作模型，将指尖触觉隐变量注入视频扩散世界模型，实现触觉世界建模，在22自由度双臂平台上六个接触密集任务全部最优（70.6 vs 38.0），仅需约4小时触觉编码器适配与每任务约100条演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖接触动力学，而这类信息仅靠视觉往往只能部分观测。现有World-Action Models（WAMs）以视觉为中心，将预测性视频世界建模与动作生成耦合，无法直接建模接触动力学，因此触觉模态的缺失成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出DexTacWAM：对每个指尖触觉独立编码，经"手指-位姿感知"的触觉压缩器聚合特征，再将触觉隐变量注入视频扩散世界模型，进行视觉-触觉联合世界建模与动作生成；采用冻结预训练视觉VAE、仅适配触觉编码器的持续视觉-触觉学习方案，无需触觉中间预训练。

Result: 22-DoF双臂平台六个接触密集灵巧操作任务上全部取得最高分，平均70.6（最强基线38.0）。消融显示：去掉触觉世界建模后四任务均值从74.7降至26.6（触觉特征与动作专家不变）。4小时触觉编码器适配、每任务约100条演示即可扩展至触觉，视觉预测质量保持在纯视觉模型0.5 dB以内。压缩器保留89.4%融合前接触召回率，训练加速2.26x、推理加速1.29x。

Conclusion: 预训练视频先验可被数据与算力高效地扩展到分布式多指接触动力学；关键在于把接触演化作为预测世界状态的一部分（触觉世界建模），而非仅将触觉作为条件输入。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact dynamics that are often only partially observable from vision. Recent World-Action Models (WAMs) couple predictive video world modeling with action generation, but remain largely vision-centric and therefore cannot directly model these contact dynamics. We present DexTacWAM, a visuo-tactile WAM that encodes each fingertip independently, aggregates the resulting features through a finger- and pose-aware tactile compressor, and injects the tactile latent into a video diffusion world model for joint visuo-tactile world modeling. Across six contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks on a 22-DoF bimanual platform, DexTacWAM achieves the highest score on every task, averaging 70.6 versus 38.0 for the strongest baseline. Ablations attribute the gain to modeling contact evolution as part of the predicted world state rather than tactile conditioning alone: removing tactile world modeling reduces the four-task mean from 74.7 to 26.6 while keeping the same tactile features and action expert. After four hours of tactile-encoder adaptation with a frozen pretrained vision VAE, our continual vision-to-touch learning extends the pretrained video model to touch using roughly 100 demonstrations per task without tactile midtraining, while retaining visual prediction quality within 0.5 dB of vision-only counterparts. The compressor retains 89.4% of pre-fusion contact recall while enabling 2.26x faster training and 1.29x faster inference. Together, these results show that pretrained video priors can be extended to distributed multi-finger contact dynamics in a data- and compute-efficient manner.

</details>


### [402] [MIGU: Multimodal Instruction Grounding under Uncertainty for Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24995)
*Mingke Lu,Anxing Xiao,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIGU：融合VLM语义先验与眼-手指向几何似然的模块化多模态指令接地框架，输出统一接地信念用于操作规划，并可主动请求澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在以人为中心环境中工作，需理解语言+手势这类互补但不确定的指令；现有方法少有显式建模多模态不确定性并将其接入规划。

Method: 通过眼-手指向几何传播视线方向与深度不确定性，并考虑手部朝向估计误差，构造3D几何似然；VLM给出候选物体/区域的语义先验；二者经贝叶斯式融合形成统一接地信念，据此进行行为规划（直接执行或请求澄清），接地目标用于移动操作与桌面task-and-motion planning。

Result: 在真实世界基准上优于所有对比基线，消融实验表明显式多模态不确定性建模带来增益。

Conclusion: 显式几何+语义不确定性融合能有效提升多模态指令接地，并可与规划闭环；抽象信息有限（未给出量化指标、代码与硬件细节）。

Abstract: Understanding natural human instructions is crucial for deploying robots in human-centric environments. We study multimodal instruction grounding, where language and gesture provide complementary but uncertain cues. We present MIGU, a modular framework that combines semantic and geometric evidence into a unified grounding belief and connects it to manipulation planning. MIGU constructs a 3D geometric likelihood by propagating viewing-direction and depth uncertainty through eye-finger geometry while accounting for hand-direction estimation error. A vision-language model (VLM) provides semantic priors over candidate objects and regions, which are combined with the geometric likelihood through Bayes-inspired fusion. The resulting belief supports behavior planning to either proceed directly to downstream planning or request clarification. Grounded targets then define goals for mobile manipulation and tabletop task-and-motion planning. On a real-world benchmark, MIGU outperforms all evaluated baselines, while ablations support the benefit of explicit multimodal uncertainty modeling. Project website: this http URL

</details>


### [403] [Learning Beyond What Humans Can Demonstrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24996)
*Yuchen Song,Aditya Mittal,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLIDE 框架针对"专家示范本身不可行"的场景，利用大模型推断任务失败模式并生成可执行的"护栏"（guardrails），用于在数据采集时过滤遥操命令、在策略部署时约束易失败动作，并把采集成功率从 0-10% 提升至 70-90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆依赖专家示范，但在需要动态稳定、精确接触时序或灵巧协调的任务中，人类操作者难以甚至无法采集示范数据，属于"示范不可行（infeasible-demonstration）"区间。现有做法要么依赖领域专家手工编写约束，要么直接用朴素遥操采集低质量数据。

Method: 提出 GLIDE（Guardrails for Learning from Infeasible Demonstrations Efficiently）：输入任务描述与条件化遥操作代码，由模型写出以系统状态为条件的 guardrails，对遥操作指令与策略指令进行过滤、约束易失败动作，并依据轨迹反馈迭代精炼。得到的护栏既用于数据采集阶段，也用于策略执行阶段（混合数据 + 护栏的策略）。

Result: 在三个任务上，GLIDE 发现的护栏超越领域专家硬编码护栏；数据采集成功率由 0-10% 提升到 70-90%；策略执行时混合数据 + 护栏策略在 Tomato plate transfer、Marker handover and stand、Wine serving 上分别达到 70%、60%、60% 成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当直接示范不可行时，自动推断失败模式并转为护栏的框架可有效支撑策略学习，为不可行示范区间的机器人操作学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Behavior cloning for robot manipulation relies on expert demonstrations. However, for tasks that require dynamic stability, precise contact timing, or dexterous coordination, human operators may find it hard or even impossible to collect data. We study this infeasible-demonstration regime and propose GLIDE: Guardrails for Learning from Infeasible Demonstrations Efficiently, a framework that infers task-specific failure modes and converts them into executable guardrails for data collection and policy deployment. Given a task description and the conditioning teleoperation code, GLIDE writes guardrails that use system states to filter teleoperation and policy commands, constrain failure-prone actions, and iteratively improve from trajectory feedback. Across three tasks, GLIDE discovers emergent guardrails that go beyond domain-expert hardcoded ones, improving data collection over naive VR teleoperation and domain-expert hardcoded guardrails. After refinement, GLIDE raises data-collection success from 0-10 percent to 70-90 percent across the three tasks. During policy execution, mixed-data guarded policies reach 70 percent, 60 percent, and 60 percent success on Tomato plate transfer, Marker handover and stand, and Wine serving tasks. These results show that GLIDE can support policy learning when direct demonstrations are infeasible. Project website: this http URL

</details>
